Motivation: KGE模型编辑常采用基于参数支持的局部性测试,但这种测试无法捕捉排名效应——提升目标答案可能将其他正确答案挤出top-k列表,即使被保护事实的分数并未改变。需要一个统一的、基于排名位移的审计框架来评估不同编辑机制的副作用。

Method: 1) 提出三个局部性范围:参数支持(共享被编辑向量的事实)、同查询(同一查询的其他正确答案)、关系范围(同一关系下其他查询的正确答案);2) 使用统一的损伤事件(top-k排名变化)进行审计;3) 推导精确分数保持的可行性条件(维度墙与几何墙);4) 系统比较直接提升、支持正则化实体编辑、严格零空间保持、秩截断保持、岭保持、闭式降秩、梯度降秩、朴素微调及回放微调等编辑策略;5) 在FB15k-237的DistMult/ComplEx上进行实验,并扩展到RESCAL、RotatE、TransE及WN18RR数据集和KGEditor案例研究。

Result: 直接提升总能将目标送入top-10,但仅23.0-23.2%的编辑无损伤;严格零空间保持无损伤但成功率仅1.3-1.4%;支持正则化实体编辑获得最高的联合成功率36.3-37.7%;秩截断保持达到32.8-34.7%,将平均位移答案数从约14降至1.2。维度分析表明编辑性不单调依赖于维度;在KGEditor上,99.16%的目标成功编辑中仍有12.38%造成至少一个关系范围位移。

Conclusion: 局部性是编辑器与保护范围对的属性,而非编辑器单独的属性;成功的修正不应仅报告成功率,还需报告位移的发生率与严重程度。精确保持的覆盖范围受限于维度墙与几何墙;秩截断提供了有用的中间地带;基于降秩的策略安全性在很大程度上取决于可信的阻止项选择;建议根据应用场景预先选择合适的保护范围。

Abstract: Editing a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model to promote a desired answer can displace correct answers from the returned list. Locality tests based only on facts that reuse the edited parameter can miss this ranking effect. We introduce a common rank-displacement audit at three scopes: facts supported by the edited parameter, other correct answers to the target query, and correct answers across queries with the same relation. We also derive dimensional and geometric conditions for an update to improve the target while exactly preserving selected scores. On FB15k-237 with DistMult and ComplEx, direct promotion always moves the target into the top ten, but does so without damage in only 23.0--23.2\% of edits. Strict preservation causes no measured damage, yet succeeds in only 1.3--1.4\%. Support-regularized entity editing gives the highest joint success, 36.3--37.7\%, while rank-truncated preservation reaches 32.8--34.7\% and reduces the mean number of displaced answers from about 14 to 1.2. Experiments across dimensions, scorers, ranking conventions, and a learned editor show that locality depends on both the protected scope and the editing mechanism. KGE editing should therefore report correction success together with the incidence and severity of rank displacement.

</details>


### [100] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了四个面向原子层沉积（ALD）和原子层刻蚀（ALE）实验与模拟过程的、经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，并将其通过QUDT本体进行语义接地，以实现科学文献中异构过程描述的机器可操作重用与跨发表比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD和ALE的实验与模拟文献在材料、工艺条件、配置和结果报告上高度异质化，阻碍了跨研究比较、数据库/知识图谱集成以及机器学习工作流中的可重用性。现有的语义资源（本体、数据库、模式）缺乏同时覆盖ALD/ALE、区分实验与模拟、支持过程级记录、闭合世界校验以及本体接地的量与单位的统一规范。

Method: 采用schema-miner人机协作工作流（LLM + 代表性文献 + 迭代领域专家评审）分别对ALD实验/模拟和ALE实验/模拟文献语料进行三阶段模式挖掘；随后使用schema-miner^pro将定量属性通过quantityValue/quantityKind结构语义接地到QUDT本体；最终将四个JSON Schema发布至Zenodo并转换为ORKG模板。

Result: 产出了四个QUDT接地的开放JSON Schema：ALD实验（275属性，6顶层组，30个QUDT属性）、ALD模拟（212属性，8顶层组，24个QUDT属性）、ALE实验（167属性，11顶层组，20个QUDT属性）、ALE模拟（275属性，8顶层组，32个QUDT属性）。所有Schema共享六个核心QUDT量类（Energy、Length、Pressure、Temperature、Thickness、Time），五个跨Schema共有的归一化领域属性（膜化学组成、压力、前驱体/反应物、反应物流速、基底材料）。已通过基于代理式LLM的抽取工作流对ALD实验Schema进行了验证，并利用ORKG模板发布结构化过程记录。

Conclusion: 四个QUDT接地的JSON Schema为ALD/ALE过程提供了共享的过程级语义基础，同时保留了过程与视角特定的概念；QUDT接地通过值-单位-量类三元组支持单位感知的验证与跨发表比较；通过代理式文献抽取工作流验证了Schema作为将科学文章转换为结构化、合规记录的目标的实用价值。未来工作将扩展对非定量概念（如材料、加工方法、结构等）的本体接地（如PMDco），并扩大文献语料评估以增强跨Schema对齐与AI可重用性。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [101] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM编排中的草稿-验证-修订模式是否能解决指示性歧义（deictic ambiguity），发现修订阶段的LLM倾向于依赖表面线索（字段标签匹配）而非操作性推理，导致性能在某些模型/配置上低于随机水平，且性价比差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草稿-验证-修订（draft-verify-revise）是LLM推理时计算的常见编排模式，但上下文级联过程中，指示性表达（如"previous"）可能因不同阶段LLM的视角不同而发生指代转移（deictic shift）。作者旨在测试修订阶段的LLM（元评估器）能否在视角冲突中正确判定哪一方的解读正确，以及哪些因素影响其判断能力。

Method: 使用10个合成基础示例 × 3种条件（correct-draft、incorrect-transparent、incorrect-opaque）的最小对设计（30个刺激，共630个配置-刺激组合），让元评估器仅做出二元裁决。使用平衡准确率作为主要指标。采用e-value序贯检验替代p-value。测试6个模型（GPT-5-mini/5.2、Claude Haiku 4.5/Opus 4.6†、Gemini 3 Flash/Pro）共21个推理强度配置。另有一项移除错误分类标签的消融实验，以及一项使用单独LLM分析错误推理链的分析。

Result: 平衡准确率范围从0.156（GPT-5.2无推理，低于随机）到接近完美。GPT-5.2从无推理的0.156提升到xhigh的0.942，Gemini 3 Pro在每个推理强度下均超过0.94。Gemini 3 Pro低推理强度以约5%的成本胜过GPT-5.2 xhigh。错误时倾向于"先表达后搁置"模式：表达了操作性解读但仍遵从字段标签匹配。消融实验表明错误分类标签（特别是"hallucinations"）具有干扰作用。Anthropic和Google的较大模型优于较小模型，但OpenAI的GPT-5.2无推理配置差于所有GPT-5-mini配置。

Conclusion: 上下文工程师实现draft-verify-revise流水线时应警惕指示性转移，并在每个阶段明确指代。模型大小和推理强度的最优组合是模型特定的，且不一定随推理强度单调提升。依赖表面线索（字段标签匹配）的失败模式可能是监督微调阶段形成的联想遗留。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [102] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议持续预测任务及MDFB基准（含2,207场真实会议、24,794条查询），并提出GLARE方法——将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成中，通过判别器对当前策略负样本进行排序获得KL正则化的策略奖励。GLARE在效用和类人性上的人类评估胜率分别为0.66和0.70，优于SFT和SPIN，但仍低于真实人类续写。同时将MDFB用作社交推理竞技场，对闭源前沿模型进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交推理基准多依赖脚本化角色扮演或预设目标的受限交互，难以反映长程、自然发生的多人讨论中的行为。预训练虽编码社交先验，但下一token预测不足以保证长程、目标导向交互中的行为落地；SFT仅模仿观测到的续写，而偏好优化和RLHF后训练需要昂贵的标注或独立奖励信号，且冻结奖励模型会随策略漂移而被利用。因此需要：(1)一个面向真实会议的预测基准；(2)一种无需额外偏好标签、能随actor共同进化的自适应奖励方法。

Method: (1) MDFB构建：整合MeetingBank、QMSum（AMI/ICSI/议会）、ELITR、Fora、NCPC、SIM等公开会议语料；通过四阶段LLM辅助流水线——查询生成、证据span识别、参考答案生成、面向未来的查询改写；配合确定性校验和启发式过滤，产出2,207场会议、24,794条查询。(2) 任务定义：给定部分转录前缀和当前讨论问题，一次性生成整段多轮续写；从效用（推进问题）和类人性（对话流畅、角色一致）两个轴通过参考辅助的人类偏好评估。(3) GLARE：将对抗模仿学习适配到条件长程语言生成——用较小的Qwen2.5-3B作为判别器，对"一条观测人类续写+多条当前actor采样"构成的候选集做多分类排序（InfoNCE式softmax损失），用最终token的标量分数作为KL正则化策略奖励；判别器在当前策略负样本上持续重训，使奖励随actor共同进化。actor为Qwen2.5-7B，用verl框架训练。

Result: 主要结果（图3）：GLARE相对SFT将平均效用胜率从0.58提升至0.66，类人性从0.55提升至0.70，超过Qwen和SPIN，但仍低于观测人类续写。GLARE对SFT直接对比：效用0.62、类人性0.69；面对人类续写仍有约28%效用和17%类人性的胜率（开放协议允许）。机器裁判（ChatGPT-5.2-chat）给出GLARE对SFT效用0.65、类人性0.72，与人类评委一致性κ=0.65。判别器容量诊断：7B判别器训练损失下降更快，验证选用较小3B判别器的设计。奖励边际分析：冻结判别器时边际急剧变负，GLARE协同训练时边际接近零，验证自适应奖励的必要性。社交推理竞技场（图4）：在六款前沿模型与GPT-5-chat对比中，GPT-5.6 Sol在GPT-5-chat和Grok-4.3裁判下分别以61.1%和72.6%领先；Gemini 3.8 Flash在自评下以77.9%领先。

Conclusion: MDFB和GLARE共同证明：自适应对抗奖励能在弱监督的会议续写任务上带来一致的人类偏好提升，但与真实人类续写仍有显著差距，议程对齐不自动等于精确的程序性解决。MDFB既支持任务特定训练，也可作为通用及闭源模型在社交推理上的输出对比平台。建议对模型续写仅用于研究和聚合分析，避免在涉及可识别参与者或敏感会议的场景中部署。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [103] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 WinSyn 的自动化管道，用于生成真实企业场景的合成问答评估数据集，重点关注邮件数据。通过多阶段自顶向下的生成流程，结合敏捷方法论构建任务依赖图和日常日志，并采用反射-精化循环确保数据一致性。实验表明现有 agentic RAG 和深度研究系统在企业邮件问答任务上表现欠佳，揭示了当前系统在企业部署中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的企业问答基准测试通常存在真实复杂性不足、回答短小、查询不自然等问题，难以有效评估企业环境中检索增强生成（RAG）和深度研究（DR）系统的真实表现。此外，企业数据具有分布式、时序性强、可能冲突等特点，而真实企业语料由于隐私和合规问题难以获取，因此需要一种能够生成高保真合成企业数据的方法来弥补这一空白。

Method: WinSyn 采用自顶向下的多阶段管道设计：从种子文档（真实或合成）出发，逐步扩展为公司上下文、员工结构、项目 epic 的 DAG 依赖图、每个 epic 下的 task DAG 依赖图，再细化为每日日志（daily diaries），最后基于日志生成问答对和邮件。每个生成阶段后都配有结构化反射、验证和精化循环。关键设计包括：1）将生成过程的内部支架结构与评估时可见的证据分离；2）在生成邮件前锁定问答答案以确保一致性；3）注入干扰邮件（distractors）以测试模型在真实噪声环境中的鲁棒性；4）建立句子级归属映射以支持答案的可审计性。

Result: 作者生成了四个跨不同技术领域的企业邮件问答数据集（Cloud DevOps、Data Platform Engineering、Stripe Billing、VS Code Changelog），每个数据集模拟一个多月、多达 25 名员工的项目。使用 ReAct agent 和基于 Onyx 深度研究代理的改编版本作为基线，使用 gpt-5.4 作为评估器。结果显示：1）两个系统在所有数据集上的聚合得分均低于 80%，表明存在显著改进空间；2）ReAct 在长篇合成查询上表现优于 Onyx（final score 0.717 vs 0.679），尤其在完整性和检索准确性方面；3）Onyx 在短篇查询的相关性和健全性上略优；4）两个系统在长篇查询上的 holistic 得分均低于 2.8，说明跨多文档信息综合仍是难点；5）加入干扰邮件后性能进一步下降，验证了数据集的挑战性。

Conclusion: WinSyn 提供了一个可扩展的框架用于生成真实、高复杂度的合成企业问答数据集，其分层设计和验证机制确保了数据的时序一致性和可审计性。实验表明当前先进的 agentic 系统在企业邮件问答场景中仍面临重大挑战，尤其是在需要跨长时间跨度和多源证据合成的任务上。该工作为评估和改进企业搜索、RAG 和深度研究系统提供了有价值的测试平台，并为未来的数据集扩展（如融入聊天、会议、工单等多种模态）和更复杂的企业动态模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [104] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Soft-PNet，一种神经-符号（NeSy）学习方法，通过原型引导的可行解缓存和单一的KL损失函数，解决在弱监督条件下中间概念无法观测导致的"推理捷径"问题，无需针对每个任务手工设计可微分损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱监督神经-符号学习中，模型仅在最终标签上进行监督，而中间概念是隐变量。许多不同的概念赋值可以满足同一标签，导致模型即使预测正确标签也可能学到错误的中间概念（推理捷径）。现有的原型网络虽然通过锚定概念到少量标注样本来减少捷径，但仍需手工设计的、任务特定的可微分NeSy损失，需要为每个任务重新推导，且难以随符号复杂度扩展。纯概率软基础方法虽然能探索可行集，但缺乏感知信号引导，在初始化时同样会陷入捷径。

Method: Soft-PNet的核心方法包括三个关键组件：(1) **解缓存机制**：在训练前预先计算可行解的全局缓存ℛ，每个训练样本维护一个包含K个候选解的工作集ℛₖ；(2) **原型锚定分布**：为每个概念使用单个标注样本及其数据增强构造原型向量，概念分布定义为嵌入到各原型向量负平方距离的softmax，提供感知信号；(3) **KL损失**：将工作集中的K个候选解按原型分布的温度缩放得分进行加权，得到每个概念的边际软目标分布，通过KL散度训练网络预测与该软目标对齐。训练目标结合KL损失与标准的原网络情景损失。Metropolis游走通过投影算子Π在可行流形上进行探索，使用原型分布（而非原始网络输出）作为接受比率。

Result: 在三个任务的稀缺监督设置下（每个概念仅一个标注锚点）：(1) **MNIST-EvenOdd**：Soft-PNet将数字识别准确率从软基础基线的27%提升到96%（零温极限下），超过PNet+SL的85%，并大幅降低方差；在分布外测试集上泛化能力显著优于基线。(2) **Visual Sudoku**：与PNet+SL在概念和标签层面性能相当（98.6%数字准确率），但训练时间减少约50%（21.5s vs 43.4s/epoch）。(3) **Kand-Logic**：在概念和标签层面与PNet+SL相当（90.26%概念准确率），训练时间略低（76.5s vs 89.8s/epoch），证明了方法在组合可行空间难以枚举时仍可使用代表性子集。消融实验表明：移除原型锚定造成最大性能下降（板F1从88.5%降至32.0%），移除候选种群也导致严重退化，而移除投影游走影响很小——说明原型引导的评分是主导机制。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet通过将手工的、任务特定的NeSy损失替换为原型引导的解缓存和单一KL目标，实现了跨任务统一的训练框架。在MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku和Kand-Logic上以一个标注锚点每个概念即可匹配精心设计的损失工程原型网络，同时恢复软基础基线遗漏的真实概念，且训练成本更低。原型锚定与基于缓存的探索相互依赖、缺一不可。该方法的主要局限在于依赖预计算的全局缓存：当解空间难以代表性采样时，缓存质量直接限制基础质量；未来工作包括缓存候选的概念级注意力、扩展到多位数MNIST-Addition以及自动驾驶基准BDD-OIA。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [105] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GT Bench（24个经典图问题、44种任务-结构设置、超过10万样本、四种图表示）和GTA（Graph Theory Agent），通过偏好训练的表示选择器与计划-分解-执行框架，在不修改执行LLM的前提下显著提升LLM在图算法推理任务上的准确率，并展现出良好的跨基准迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基准评估存在不足：要么任务过于简单，要么评估代码生成而非图本身的推理能力，要么仅采用单一固定输入格式。这导致LLM在多步图算法任务中的真实推理能力难以被准确衡量和改进。

Method: 1) 构建GT Bench：覆盖24个经典图问题（涵盖多项式时间算法、NP难优化与NP完全判定问题），采用自然语言、结构化语言、邻接矩阵、邻接列表四种等价表示，节点规模受提示长度预算约束。2) 通过八种LLM的实证分析，系统刻画输入表示与图结构（密度、大小、拓扑）、模型本身之间的敏感性关联。3) 提出GTA框架：采用偏好训练（DPO）的表示选择器对每个实例自适应选取最佳表示，结合训练自由的算法生成器与SFT+DPO训练的算法分解器，将策略计划分解为子步骤序列，由冻结的执行LLM按序处理完成求解。

Result: GTA将Phi-4在GT-E上的准确率从53.5%提升至69.1%，在GT-H上从33.0%提升至41.5%，超越了八种提示与代理基线方法；零训练迁移到GraCoRe（80.5%）与NLGraph（89.4%）基准；可跨执行模型迁移（GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4o、DeepSeek-R1均取得显著提升）；推理成本可控（每600实例约$1.11，仅次于MaAS与直接提示）；输入溢出率为0%，输出截断率仅1.2%。

Conclusion: GT Bench揭示了LLM图推理能力中表示敏感性的系统性规律（最优表示随任务、图结构与模型变化），GTA通过自适应表示选择与算法分解在冻结执行器前提下稳定提升推理能力，为基于语言的算法图推理提供了可衡量的基准与可迁移的增强框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: this https URL . The project homepage is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架TRAC，通过基于Transformer的神经Oracle（T-Oracle）模拟用户响应，替代传统约束获取（CA）中的人工Oracle，并与FastCA符号学习器交互，实现从标注示例中自动恢复可解释的约束网络，显著减少用户参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束获取（CA）方法依赖与人类Oracle的大量交互（查询耗时且数量大），构成了知识建模的瓶颈；同时，构建约束网络需要领域专家且耗时费力。深度学习和Transformer在结构化数据上展现出了强大的抽象和泛化能力，因此探索用神经Oracle替代人类Oracle，实现无需先验领域知识的自动化约束获取成为关键问题。

Method: 1) **T-Oracle（神经Oracle）**：基于Transformer架构，采用值嵌入与位置嵌入的双嵌入方案表示完整/部分赋值，引入交叉变量注意力机制学习约束矩阵M捕捉变量间结构依赖，通过门控机制（依赖分数S与Transformer概率）进行分类；提出三个变体TO₁（全赋值训练）、TO₂（混合部分/完整赋值训练）、TO₃（按约束scope的局部部分赋值训练）。2) **FastCA（CA学习器）**：基于部分查询的穷举式约束获取算法，每个迭代移除至少一个约束，针对自动化/神经Oracle优化获取吞吐量和查询延迟而非最小化查询数，证明了其soundness、completeness和termination，查询复杂度O(n^k t)，且对噪声反馈鲁棒。3) **神经符号交互**：训练好的T-Oracle与FastCA形成闭环，自动从标注数据中恢复结构化符号模型。

Result: 在10个基准问题（Sudoku、Latin Square、JobShop、Murder、Zebra、随机网络、ExamTT、NurseR等）上的实验表明：(1) FastCA虽查询数更多，但查询时间极低，总获取时间具有竞争力；(2) TO₁在多数基准上达到完美分类，TO₃在稀疏解问题（Murder、Zebra）上达到接近完美的精确率；(3) FastCA+TO₃组合在几乎所有基准上达到100%准确率/精确率/召回率，显著优于其他CA学习器（QuAcq、MQuAcq、GrowAcq）与Oracle的组合，且对Oracle错误具有鲁棒性；(4) TO₃作为约束检查器，对二/三/四元约束均达到>95%准确率，可靠地模拟符号约束检查器。

Conclusion: 本文成功将基于Transformer的神经Oracle与FastCA符号学习器整合，首次实现了无需人类Oracle的自动化约束获取框架。TO₃（基于约束局部训练）能有效捕获组合问题的潜在语义，与FastCA的穷举搜索结合后，对神经Oracle的误分类具有强鲁棒性。该混合方法在神经泛化与符号推理之间搭建了桥梁，是数据驱动可解释模型构建的重要进展；未来需进一步探索在超大规模域中的可扩展性。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [107] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 T-GADE（热力学生成式 AI 驱动的进化），将热力学遗传算法（TDGA）的自由能最小化框架扩展至以 LLM 生成的结构化"制品"（如解释+代码）为个体的演化过程，支持 Fermi 型（不重复基因型）与 Bose 型（允许重复基因型）占据规则，以及世代型与稳态型更新。在 EoH 论文的在线装箱任务上，世代型 Bose-T-GADE（T=0.003）将训练超额中位数从 1.152% 降至 0.815%（约 29% 改进），并通过验证选择在迁移评估上达到与 EoH 相同的中位数 0.496%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 LLM 融入演化计算需要同时控制生成长成能力与种群多样性。现有方法（如 EoH、FunSearch 等）侧重于 LLM 作为遗传算子或制品生成器，但缺乏统一的种群管理框架来显式平衡个体质量与多样性，且不同更新形式（世代型 vs 稳态型）与占据规则之间的关系未被系统化。

Method: 1. 将"制品"定义为 LLM 输出的结构化对象（解释+代码对），由基于执行的任务评估器分配能量 E(x)；2. 多样性通过 10,000 维 L2 归一化特征向量（填充率+紧度排序拼接）的 Gram 矩阵行列式对数度量；3. 种群目标为自由能 F = ⟨E⟩ − T·H，其中 T 为选择温度，H 为归一化熵；4. 借鉴统计物理提出 Fermi 型（同一基因型至多一个个体）与 Bose 型（允许重复）占据规则，并将其与世代型更新（贪心构造下一代种群）与稳态型更新（每次评估后移除一个个体以最小化存活者自由能）组合在统一框架中；5. 理论证明：(a) 稳态更新中一次移除的最优性（命题 1）；(b) 在 T=0、非减能量函数、相同过滤条件下，恢复 EoH 的存活规则（命题 2）；6. 使用 Qwen3-32B 作为生成模型，在 EoH 的在线装箱任务上进行 20 次运行的统计比较。

Result: 在 20 次运行/配置的实验中：(1) 训练性能方面，世代型 Bose-T-GADE（T=0.003）中位超额 0.815%，相比 EoH 的 1.152% 降低约 29%，双侧 Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378；(2) 20 次中有 15 次训练超额 ≤1%（EoH 仅 8 次），且超过 2% 的次数更少（2/20 vs 4/20）；(3) 通过验证实例在最终种群前 2 名中选择，可使迁移超额从 0.962% 降至 0.496%，与 EoH 在相同选择程序下的结果相当；(4) Fermi 型占据与较高温度（T=0.03）效果较差；(5) 附加控制实验表明独立生成（无亲本指导）和无质量选择的进化均显著劣于完整方法，验证了迭代变异与基于质量的保留各自的价值。

Conclusion: T-GADE 展示了热力学选择在 LLM 制品演化中的实用性，统一了世代型与稳态型更新以及 Fermi 型与 Bose 型占据规则，并能恢复 EoH 在零温度下的存活规则。验证选择（而非仅训练排名）对于从保留种群中挑选迁移能力强的个体至关重要。最终种群的多样性组合与训练赢家不同，二者互为补充。代码已开源。局限包括：单一任务（在线装箱）、单一模型（Qwen3-32B）、训练-迁移因果关系未验证、适应度与多样性仅在执行层面定义。未来方向包括：更丰富的结构化制品、行为熵（而非文本熵）用于衡量稳定性、适应度-多样性-温度的联合自适应控制，以及说明-代码继承在 LLM 非确定性下的稳定性分析。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $\delta=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [108] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多回合原型网络（MEPN），通过在推理时聚合多个不相交支持回合的原型来降低原型方差，在DeFACTO传感器故障数据集上将单回合one-shot分类准确率从73.5%提升至93.1%，并在等支持预算下与K=10的ProtoNet统计不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准原型网络在极低样本（K=1）场景下，由于支持集小，每个类别的原型估计方差大，导致分类决策不稳定，尤其对于工业传感器中类边界微弱的细微故障影响严重。

Method: 保留ProtoNet编码器与最近原型分类规则不变，仅修改类代表估计方式：抽取N_agg个不相交支持回合，分别计算每回合各类原型p_c^(j)，再对其取均值作为最终类代表\hat{p}_c。理论分析给出Cov[\hat{p}_c]=Σ_c/(K·N_agg)，并讨论了时间序列相关性导致的方差下界。训练阶段N_agg=1，仅推理时聚合。

Result: 在DeFACTO五类故障分类（100次独立运行）上，K=1时达93.1%±0.3%，相比ProtoNet的73.5%提升19.6个百分点；K=5时达93.8%。等预算下与K=10的ProtoNet均为93.1%，统计不可区分。N_agg消融显示在N_agg=10附近饱和；均值聚合优于中位数聚合（93.0% vs 91.0%，K=1）。混淆矩阵显示Normal/Bias混淆显著降低。MNIST验证亦证实该机制具有普适性。

Conclusion: 类别估计器的设计与编码器同样重要，MEPN通过推理时多回合聚合将固定支持预算增量可用，无需等待全部标签同时到达，为安全关键的工业监测提供了更稳定的少样本故障诊断方案。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [109] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合物理-AI框架（HKM-NN），通过仅使用腕戴IMU估计人体质心（COM）加速度。核心创新包括：1）基于机器人臂运动学启发的简化4段KM模型；2）三种物理-AI集成策略（串行学习ser-和两种同步学习sim1-/sim2-），结合FCNN/LSTM。在10名志愿者的6种步态活动和坐-站转换任务上验证，sim1-方案表现最优，NRMSE在步态活动中为5.3%-9.3%，坐-站任务中最低3.9%。研究表明，物理-数据驱动的耦合方式（而非是否引入物理约束）对精度、鲁棒性和可解释性的平衡起决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕戴IMU虽普及且社会接受度高，但其仅反映局部手臂运动，无法表征全身动力学。COM加速度是评估动态稳定性和功能性移动的生理参考标准。然而，仅凭腕部单IMU估计COM本质上是一个不适定逆问题——不同的全身运动可产生相似的腕部信号。传统方法各有局限：基于物理的方法需要多达17个IMU的全身穿戴；纯数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，鲁棒性受限。因此，如何以原则化方式集成物理先验与数据驱动学习，在最小传感配置下平衡精度、鲁棒性和可解释性，是一项关键但尚未充分探索的挑战。

Method: 1) **简化4段KM模型**：将人体抽象为前臂-上臂-肩部-COM四段链，基于Newton-Euler方程描述线加速度、角速度、角加速度在段间的传播。引入两个归约假设：肘关节锁定（刚体连接前臂与上臂）、肩部刚体连接（保留屈伸自由度）。通过线性变换向量A近似未测量的关节角速度。共27个参数（3×4四元数旋转+3×3位置向量+2×3角速度耦合向量）。2) **HKM-NN集成策略**：①串行学习（ser-）：先独立训练KM，再训练NN学习残差；②同步学习sim1-：联合优化，总损失L_sl = L_f + L_h + 0.5·L_c，其中L_h为KM的机理损失（含加速度MSE和四元数单位范数约束），L_f为NN的数据损失，L_c为KM-NN输出耦合损失（同时KM受真值监督）；③同步学习sim2-：无L_h，KM仅通过L_c作为结构先验引导NN（L_sl = L_f + L_c）。3) **NN架构**：FCNN（隐层16）和LSTM（隐层8）。4) **损失梯度余弦相似度分析**评估耦合项与各损失项的协同/冲突关系。5) **噪声鲁棒性测试**：测试阶段注入高斯噪声和椒盐噪声（SNR=2~10），训练集干净。

Result: 1) **无噪声条件**：KM的NRMSE为6.7%-12.5%（步态）和5.6%（SS）；HKM-NN中除sim2-外均显著优于KM，NRMSE降至5.3%-9.3%（步态），最佳3.9%（SS）。sim2-因KM不受真值监督导致参数收敛至次优，性能较差。2) **高斯噪声**：KM衰减最快（R²迅速降至强负值），ser-因依赖KM输出同样严重退化；sim1-保持最高精度且退化平缓；sim2-精度低但对噪声敏感度有限。3) **椒盐噪声**：KM表现出较强鲁棒性（确定性结构对稀疏冲击不敏感）；FCNN类模型退化大于LSTM类（缺乏时序建模），但ser-FCNN因KM瓶颈补偿了此缺陷。4) **参数分析**：KM的雅可比矩阵秩为16-20（小于27），表明存在冗余或共线性；旋转矩阵R₀¹、R₁²、R₂³及位置向量P₀¹、P₁²对输出影响最大。5) **梯度余弦相似度**：sim1-中∇L_h与∇L_c始终正协同；sim2-中∇L_f与∇L_c从强正协同渐变为轻度负值，表明KM作为软正则器而非严格约束。

Conclusion: 论文证实了仅凭腕戴IMU估计COM加速度的可行性，并揭示了混合物理-AI框架中三个核心结论：1) **sim1-表现最优**，因直接监督KM分支在物理约束与数据驱动适应性间提供了最佳平衡，其参数仍保持活动且任务响应，但已偏离独立训练KM的物理一致性；2) **鲁棒性具有噪声类型依赖性**——sim1-在高斯噪声下优势明显，KM在椒盐噪声下表现稳定，LSTM的时序建模对稀疏冲击有抵抗作用；3) **不同耦合策略决定了误差与约束在系统中的传递路径**：串行学习赋予KM明确计算角色但传播其敏感性，sim2-虽更灵活但物理参数可被弱化甚至趋近零。总体而言，物理-AI混合的价值不仅取决于是否引入物理先验，更取决于物理意义在联合优化中如何被维护。未来的工作应扩展到更多日常活动、提升KM参数的可辨识性与生物力学合理性，并探索自适应耦合策略。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [110] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对深度扰动学习（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种可能角色进行受控评估，发现经审计发现公开实现存在两处正确性问题（图像方向在增强归一化空间计算但应用于原始图像；标签扰动低于float32分辨率），修正后DPL在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18的三个配对种子上均未通过直接删除门，作为效用正则化和攻击热启动的角色也未能可靠优于简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将DPL（基于影响函数的数据扰动）定位为机器遗忘的多功能工具，但弱角色的证据被用来支持强主张（直接遗忘）。本文动机在于：现有评估缺乏角色分离、缺乏与精确重训练的匹配基线、且未审计实现正确性，因此需要严格的受控研究来检验DPL在三种递减强度角色中的真实能力。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 角色分离设计——为直接遗忘、效用正则化、攻击热启动三种角色分别配置匹配的控制基线和预声明的通过/失败门控；(2) 局部理论分析——证明随机删除后重训练保持高遗忘准确率（命题1）、推导删除的一阶参数位移（命题2）、推导匹配该位移的最优输入方向（命题3）、证明坐标错误如何破坏保证（命题4）；(3) 实现审计——追踪公开DPL代码的张量流、变换、符号、投影，识别并修正坐标不匹配和数值无效的标签扰动；(4) 冻结配置的三种子种子配对实验（CIFAR-10/ResNet-18）+ 单种子Tiny ImageNet/VGG-19外部检查。

Result: 核心结果：DPL在所有三个配对种子中均未通过直接删除门（最佳DPL检查点保留94.53%测试准确率但遗忘准确率仍为100%，Avg.Diff为4.366，差于SalUn的1.745和AMUN的3.935）；效用正则化仅在3个种子中通过1个；攻击热启动在严格原始空间比较下未在任何一个种子中同时优于官方初始化和FGSM初始化，且加入方向计算成本后更慢；Tiny ImageNet检查中预处理不一致导致直接比较无定论，但效用和热启动角色均不支持DPL。

Conclusion: 本文得出三个结论：(1) 局部相关的输入-参数混合导数方向不能保证行为级遗忘匹配，需在角色终点评估；(2) 影响函数方向的计算需保持坐标一致性和数值精度，公开实现的两个缺陷使评估的是错误的扰动；(3) 对随机实例删除，遗忘准确率下降本身不是正确的目标——精确重训练在统计上预期保持高遗忘准确率。本文贡献包括角色匹配评估协议和扰动型删除声明的审计清单，呼吁保守报告并区分删除保证与行为审计。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [111] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一个面向多智能体多用户LLM系统的隐私感知内存管理框架，并配套发布了首个多用户内存评估基准MUMBench。该框架通过自动可见性分类、索引级访问控制和多标签操作检测，在共享单一内存存储的同时保障用户级隐私隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM仅作用于单次会话，无法学习用户长期偏好；现有智能体内存系统多局限于单用户层级，限制了跨用户公共知识的共享。该工作旨在解决多用户多智能体场景下私有与共享记忆的协同管理难题。

Method: 框架包含两条级联推理路径——写入路径（多标签意图检测→结构化事实抽取→可见性分类→上下文去重）和读取路径（可见性约束的稠密检索→标签重打分→LLM重排序）。核心创新在于：(1) 通过LLM自动推断public/private可见性标签而非人工标注；(2) 在向量索引层面确定性而非概率性强制执行访问控制；(3) 支持单消息多标签操作检测。配套构建了MUMBench基准数据集，含672条跨4领域（编码、客服、教育、旅行）的多用户交互。

Result: 在MUMBench上，使用GPT-5.4-mini作为主模型时取得最优：可见性分类准确率96.0%、严格操作准确率58.8%、状态感知操作准确率70.5%。五个测试模型（GPT-5.4/5.4-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Mistral-Large-3、Llama-3.3-70B）的可见性分类平均准确率均接近或超过90%。在LOCOMO基准上AIM表现逊于Mem0，因后者为单用户场景，未能体现AIM的多用户优势。

Conclusion: AIM是首个端到端统一LLM抽取、自动隐私分类与索引级访问执行的模块化系统无关框架，在多用户隐私保护方面表现优异。局限性包括：标签提取（35.5%重叠率）和检索相关性（45.0%）有待提升，公共记忆存在被注入错误信息的风险，且LOCOMO单用户评估未能充分验证其核心优势。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [112] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Affective Agent，一种面向可穿戴设备的三层参考架构（感知层、个性化层、推理层），使用亚十亿参数语言模型在设备本地完成个性化干预推理，无需云端依赖或逐用户重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备虽已能通过生理信号推断用户情感/压力状态，但如何决定是否、何时以及如何进行个性化干预仍是难题。现有自适应干预系统（如JITAIs）在适应性、接受度和上下文感知方面存在局限，且云端推理和逐用户重训练在隐私、延迟和功耗方面面临实际约束。

Method: 1) 三层架构：感知层将原始多模态生理信号转换为紧凑结构化证据（含冻结分类器输出）；个性化层通过情景记忆（短期）和语义记忆（长期）维护用户特定状态，采用三阶段偏好生命周期（population default → provisional → learned）；推理层使用微调的亚十亿参数语言模型（Qwen2.5-0.5B/Qwen3-0.6B），通过显式两遍推理（评估+验证）输出受限干预决策（intervene/wait/abstain）。
2) 训练流程：基于WESAD、SWELL-KW、En-Gage先验的合成场景生成（14用户队列）、六级推理复杂度课程（T0-T5）、程序化两遍监督。
3) 评估在Raspberry Pi CM5上完成，与确定性规则基线对比，并进行消融实验（no-memory、one-pass）。

Result: 在1600个决策点上：Qwen3和Qwen2.5的动作准确率分别为0.782和0.811，显著优于规则基线（0.482）。Qwen3在模态准确率上更优（0.864 vs 0.701），Qwen2.5在宏F1上更优（0.677 vs 0.616）。模式合规率均达0.993。消融实验显示：去除记忆使宏F1降至0.501、模态准确率降至0.605；去除验证遍使宏F1降至0.463、ECE恶化至0.377。边缘延迟：Qwen3总延迟83.4秒（解码占75.9%），Qwen2.5为53.7秒。主要不足：置信度校准（ECE 0.255-0.301）差于基线（0.097）。

Conclusion: 亚十亿参数模型可通过结构化记忆和显式两遍推理在可穿戴级硬件上执行结构化干预推理，超越固定阈值规则基线，且无需逐用户重训练。该架构是领域无关的参考蓝图，可推广至情感计算、数字健康、智能家居等多种可穿戴干预场景。未来需进行真实用户纵向研究、改进置信度校准、测量功耗，并探索更激进量化与NPU加速。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [113] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoRA-RC：一种用于储层计算的在线低秩自适应方法，通过在每个时间步对低秩修正进行谱范数投影和一阶低通滤波，保证所有应用的循环矩阵始终处于认证的收缩集内，从而在流式预测误差驱动的在线适应过程中保持增量输入到状态稳定性（δISS），并在Lorenz系统突变漂移实验中显著优于固定RC和仅读出适应RC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算（RC）虽具有快速、数据高效的优势，但固定储层在系统漂移下性能下降；仅读出适应不足以应对特征空间变化，而无约束的循环矩阵适应会破坏回声状态和增量稳定性等关键性质。因此需要一种在在线更新中保持稳定性证书的循环矩阵适应方法。

Method: 将循环矩阵分解为 W_k = W_0 + U M_k V^T，其中 W_0 为离线认证的基储层，U 和 V 为离线固定的正交适应基，M_k 为在线更新的小型 r×r 核心矩阵。在线更新采用投影梯度下降（投影到谱范数球 ℳ 上）后接一阶低通滤波，生成应用核心 M_k。通过谱范数投影保证 ‖W_k‖ ≤ κ < 1，每步保持收缩性；并证明了沿任意在线适应路径均匀成立的 δISS 界。

Result: 在 Lorenz-63 系统的突变参数漂移（ρ 从 28 突变为 40）实验中：漂移后预测 RMSE 较固定 RC 降低 56%，较仅读出适应 RC 降低 51%；消融实验（20 个随机种子）表明去除投影使误差膨胀超过 40 倍，去除滤波使误差膨胀 5.6 倍；超参数敏感性分析显示投影半径和核心学习率最为稳健。

Conclusion: LoRA-RC 成功实现了在保持每步收缩性证书不变的前提下对储层循环矩阵的在线低秩适应，证明了沿适应路径的均匀 δISS 界，在混沌系统漂移场景中展现出显著性能优势。未来工作包括基于 Lyapunov 的条件扩展以扩大可接受集，以及在更高维和实际闭环控制系统中的验证。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE方法，将长程个性化对话中的"人格漂移"问题重新定义为受控的持久人格状态修订问题，通过将轮次局部证据与持久信念更新解耦，并引入不确定性感知的三路路由（Commit/Defer/Ignore）机制来平衡人格稳定性与适应性。同时构建了PERSIST基准用于评估锚定后的人格状态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程个性化语言模型面临"人格漂移"挑战：用户的偏好随交互演变而非固定不变。现有方法缺乏对"是否应将新观察证据写入持久人格状态"的显式决策机制，导致过度激进的更新会覆盖已锚定的偏好，而过度保守又会阻碍对真实偏好的适应。需要一种受控的、可审计的人格状态修订机制来处理这种稳定性-适应性权衡。

Method: CORE采用四步流程：(1) 轮次局部证据推断——对每个槽位从当前对话中提取证据分布（含空选项⊥表示无可用证据）；(2) 不确定性刻画——计算相关性、模糊度（归一化熵）和冲突度（与先前信念的JS散度）；(3) 更新授权——通过策略网络预测Commit/Defer/Ignore三路路由动作；(4) 门控信念修订——基于KL正则化进行连续门控的后验更新，仅当Commit时才执行。训练采用监督预热（SFT学习信念、证据、路由、话语和回复五个损失）加PPO策略优化（奖励权重为动作正确性0.35、状态保真度0.30、回复质量0.25、澄清行为0.10）。

Result: 在PersonaChat和ALOE上，CORE在匹配同骨干方法中取得最高对齐分数（ALOE AL 82.94%，PersonaChat AL 76.54%）；在PERSIST压力测试中，ARUS从71.9提升至80.8，BCD从66.1提升至76.4，漂移从12.6降至10.1；消融实验表明移除可执行更新动作导致最大性能下降（AL降至78.8）；人类评估中CORE较RLPA平均提升11.7%，其中人格一致性相关维度提升11.6%-15.5%，而通用回复质量仅提升4.7%；每轮推理仅增加23.0ms开销。

Conclusion: 实验支持显式更新控制作为鲁棒长程个性化的有效机制，超越了更强的回复生成或更持久的记忆本身。将持久人格修订作为基于证据的显式决策而非每轮局部观察的自动后果，是缓解人格漂移的关键设计选择。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [115] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlueLM-GUI 是一个 35B-A3B 的移动 GUI 智能体，以"真实设备闭环飞轮"为核心，通过"每个样本都重要、每次 rollout 都真实、每个查询都进化"三大原则，构建从数据生成、训练到评估的端到端自演进系统，在 MobileGUI-VBench 与 AndroidWorld 上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 智能体从多模块框架转向端到端原生模型后，工业落地仍面临三大结构性瓶颈：1) 沙箱/模拟器训练与生产环境之间存在分布鸿沟（distribution gap）；2) 真实设备上昂贵的失败轨迹被大量浪费（utilization gap）；3) 固定基准在模型迭代中迅速饱和、丧失诊断能力（attribution gap）。论文旨在通过统一的真实设备飞轮同时弥合这三大缺口。

Method: 方法围绕"飞轮"展开，分数据、训练、评估三层。 数据层：双轨管线 + 全真实设备农场；查询生成基于"功能树 + 三层指令（L1/L2/L3）+ 八类用户人设 + 六维能力画像 + 全局环境记忆 GEM"；轨迹收集含半自动、人工、自动、Schema-guided 四条通道；每条轨迹携带五标签结构化 CoT 并经过水平增强（含异常状态注入、重复-反思转换）。轨迹评估采用"异构三系统共识 HTSC"（终态物理验证 + 里程碑分解 + 整体路径合理性 + 安全一票否决），输出 True/False/Env_ERROR/Undetermined 四种去向；级联归因后由"错误修正与派生模块 ECDM"经"逐步级修正、追溯式查询对齐 RQA、反事实查询派生"三条路径变废为宝。 训练层：基于 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，分 CPT（15B tokens，冻结视觉编码器）→ SFT（5B tokens，意图级动作 + 行为边界）→ 在数百真实手机上的 GRPO 强化学习；奖励由"进度 + 质量"等权构成并叠加逐步奖励，使用原子化、帧绑定、多轮投票的评判。 评估层：MobileGUI-VBench 围绕"场景 / 复杂度 / 交互与风险"三正交轴定义配额化指标体系，基准随模型升级通过调整配额实现"演进"，评估采用多 VLM 评委一致投票 + 人工裁决。

Result: 在 MobileGUI-VBench 上得分 87.4，比最佳闭源 API 模型（Seed2.1-Pro, 82.3）高 5.1 分，创 SOTA；在 AndroidWorld 上得分 84.9，开源模型中最强，与参数量远超自身的闭源大模型（如 Qwen3.8-Max 85.3、Qwen3.8-Flash 84.5）相当。消融实验验证：1) CPT 阶段不可或缺（仅 SFT 64.1 vs. CPT+SFT 70.8）；2) 文本历史扩展收益（+2.8）显著大于视觉扩展（+1.1）；3) 减小 40% 屏幕理解数据反而提升执行成绩（70.8→71.9）；4) 在线真实设备 RL 显著优于离线 RL（82.0 vs. 73.9），证明 RL 提升来自真实环境而非算法本身；5) 原子化、多轮评判的奖励信号将 RL 成功率从 72.52 提升至 77.42。

Conclusion: BlueLM-GUI 证明：将训练轨迹收集、失败回收、迭代评估全部锚定在真实设备上，配合配额化可演进基准，能够以远小于头部闭源模型的参数量产出强健、可迁移的移动 GUI 能力；其"数据-训练-评估"飞轮为工业级 GUI 智能体提供了一条可持续自演进路径。未来方向包括扩大设备规模、融合 GUI+API+CLI 交互范式，以及减少飞轮中的人工参与、提高闭环自治度。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [116] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对3D高斯溅射(3DGS)中神经化趋势进行了系统性的分类与受控实验研究,提出五轴分类法分析19种代表性方法,并通过实验证明适度的神经化(共享外观和不透明度)优于完全神经化(解码几何结构)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS与NeRF之间边界日益模糊,许多系统引入神经网络生成或共享高斯参数,但现有讨论常简单地将这些方法标记为"神经"或"非神经",缺乏对神经化程度和方式的精细区分。作者旨在澄清这一趋势的本质:不是3DGS回归到NeRF,而是参数化方式的演进。

Method: 1) 提出五轴分类法:属性解码(A)、空间共享(S)、视图条件化(V)、拓扑生成(T)、摊销推理(I),每轴取值{0, 1/2, 1};2) 对19种代表性3DGS方法进行架构分析;3) 在mip-NeRF 360上构建四个受控变体E₀-E₃,隔离外观解码、空间共享、结构解码三种神经化形式,保持渲染器、初始化、密集化策略等不变。

Result: 五轴评分显示:神经属性解码最常见(14/14非零方法),空间共享(10种)、拓扑生成(13种)、摊销推理(6种)、视图条件化(4种)递减;受控实验中E₂(共享外观+不透明度+空间特征)取得最佳效果(PSNR 27.76, SSIM 0.820, LPIPS 0.196),相比E₀提升0.55dB;E₃进一步解码协方差和位置偏移后性能反而下降。

Conclusion: 3DGS并非回归NeRF,而是融合神经参数共享与显式溅射渲染;选择性神经化优于全面神经化——共享外观和不透明度有益,但额外解码几何结构无收益;神经组件最适用于共享独立高斯会重复的相关信息,显式参数对局部几何控制仍有价值;五轴评分N仅作为架构描述符而非性能预测器。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [117] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过在The Core竞争协同进化算法中引入"小生境（niching）"机制，将智能体限制在Xpilot游戏地图的特定象限内重新生成，从而进化出针对局部环境特征的专业化行为控制器。实验结果表明，与在整个地图上进化（全局小生境）的智能体以及来自其他象限的智能体相比，象限特化的智能体表现出更优的战斗力，尤其在低障碍密度象限中优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的The Core研究中，智能体可在整个Xpilot地图任意位置重生，进化出的智能体并未针对特定子环境形成专业化行为。本文旨在探索能否通过空间约束（小生境）促使智能体进化出适应各自象限独特地形特征（如障碍物密度和分布）的局部优化策略，从而验证在小生境机制下竞争协同进化的有效性。

Method: 1. 算法基础：采用The Core竞争协同进化算法，核心为一种多环循环遗传算法（Multi-Loop CGA），不含显式适应度函数。每个智能体拥有12个循环、每个循环含8个基因的二进制染色体，通过击杀事件触发交叉，3秒"青春期"后才有繁殖权。2. 小生境机制：将120个智能体分为4组（每组30个），分别限制在地图的4个象限内重生死亡后只能在本象限复活。3. 实验设置：连续运行72小时；性能评估通过两类基准测试：①16个本地象限智能体 vs 16个全局（非小生境）智能体，每场12小时、重复10次；②本地象限智能体 vs 相邻象限智能体（跨象限迁移测试）。

Result: 1. 本地 vs 全局小生境智能体对比（图5）：本地象限智能体累计击杀数显著高于全局智能体，验证了小生境机制的有效性。2. 本地 vs 跨象限访问智能体对比（图6）：低障碍密度象限（Q1和Q3）的智能体不仅在本象限击败访问者，作为"访问者"进入其他象限时同样占优，说明其在低障碍环境下更倾向于进化出以战斗为导向的策略（而非避障），使其在多种环境中具备更强的击杀能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过多环循环遗传算法结合The Core的竞争协同进化框架，配合空间小生境约束，能够有效进化出针对Xpilot特定子环境的专业化智能体控制器。小生境智能体在与其本地环境及跨象限对抗中均优于全局进化智能体，表明局部空间约束能够促使策略专门化。该方法具备良好的可扩展性（支持100+智能体并行进化），未来可推广至模拟赛车等其他复杂游戏环境中。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [118] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA 是面向生成式推荐（GR）大波束解码场景的线性注意力（GDN）解码框架，通过共享 prompt 后状态 + 紧凑追加式 GDN Transition Records（GTR） + 轻量级波束血缘索引，避免为每个波束物化完整的循环状态矩阵，实现 1.54–2.46× 端到端解码加速并显著降低显存与访存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐需要大波束解码生成数百个候选项，导致线性注意力的循环状态随波束宽度线性增长。现有方案（vLLM FullState / ReplaySSM 等）要么为每个波束物化完整 d_v×d_k 状态矩阵，要么让每个波束独立重放共享历史，带来巨大的显存占用与访存开销。

Method: 核心设计包含三点：(1) 每个请求只保存一份 prefill 后共享状态 S_ctx；(2) 用紧凑 GTR r_t=(α_t, δ_t, k_t)（仅 O(d_v+d_k) 大小）追加记录每个解码步的转移，而非 O(d_v·d_k) 的完整矩阵；(3) 通过投影重放（projection replay）沿血缘链在两个 d_v 维累加器 u_q、u_k 上直接计算 S·q_t 与 S·k_t，避免重建中间矩阵。配套轻量级 ancestry index h_t(b,j) 解耦逻辑波束树与物理 GTR 槽位，使 GTR 保持 append-only 与不可变。GPU 端采用 fused shared-context kernel，每个线程块加载一次 S_ctx 的 B_V×d_k tile 并在多个波束间复用，整段重放在片上完成。

Result: 在 0.8B Qwen3.5（6 全注意力层 + 18 GDN 层）上，R=4、W=256、prompt 1K/5K、O=3–7 条件下，端到端解码前向获得 1.54–2.46× 加速。算子层面 405 种 shape 上的中位加速比为：vLLM/SGLang FullState 40.5×/42.5×、两者 ReplaySSM 17.5×、FlashInfer 41.5×、TensorRT-LLM 46.3×；持久循环状态容量最高减少 42.5×；DRAM 流量在 O=3 时仅 161 MiB（较 FullState 减少 76.9×），算术强度达 40.0 FLOP/byte；最大绝对输出误差 4.88×10⁻⁴，相对 FP32 FullState 保持数值一致性。

Conclusion: OneLA 验证了对线性注意力循环状态采用"共享上下文 + 紧凑追加式转移记录 + 轻量级血缘"的表示方式，可有效支撑生成式推荐的动态大波束解码，在保持数值精度的同时大幅降低显存占用、数据搬移与端到端延迟，为线性注意力在工业级 GR 系统中的部署提供了新的系统级方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [119] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化的进化框架，为六足机器人每条腿独立进化CPG控制器参数，无需集中协调，通过涌现行为实现协调步态，在Webots仿真器中以Mantis机器人为平台验证，相比合作协同进化方法在收敛速度、最终适应度和腿部缺失鲁棒性方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式步态优化方法存在高维搜索空间的可扩展性问题，且对协调中断敏感；合作协同进化虽降低了计算负担，但仍需管理组件间交互的复杂性。因此需要一种更简单、可扩展且鲁棒的方法来生成六足机器人步态。

Method: 每条腿独立维护一个GA种群，优化其CPG正弦轨迹参数（振幅A、相位φ、偏移O），公式为θ(t)=A·sin(2πft+φ)+O。共享固定频率f。每代从各腿种群中各取一个个体组成完整步态进行仿真，以X轴最大位移作为适应度，并反馈给所有参与的腿部控制器。比较对象为合作协同进化基线。采用"有效代数"归一化评估成本，并进行单腿缺失的消融实验测试鲁棒性。

Result: 在全部6条腿正常运行及任意单腿缺失的消融场景下，去中心化方法在10次独立运行中均优于合作协同进化，表现为收敛更快、最终适应度更高、收敛曲线更平滑、性能方差更小，并展现出对结构损伤的更强适应性。

Conclusion: 完全去中心化的优化可通过涌现行为实现有效的全局步态协调，且天然具有更好的可扩展性和容错性；未来工作将在真实六足机器人平台上验证，并探索结合有限局部通信的混合模型及与完全集中式控制器的比较。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PLCD 是一个面向认知诊断的框架，用 LLM 生成的结构化语言证据（概念模式、认知过程图）替代传统的 ID 嵌入作为认知先验，并结合学生历史答题记录，通过目标条件语义记忆进行个性化校准，从而在标准、稀疏及冷启动场景下提升诊断性能与认知可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型依赖学生、习题、概念的离散 ID 嵌入，忽略了教育文本中蕴含的语义与认知结构，难以处理新习题/新概念等冷启动场景，且与人类知识以关系和概念相似性组织的认知机制不符。

Method: 1) 用 LLM 为每个概念构建类型化的概念模式（定义、先修、子技能、误解、认知操作），为每道题构建认知过程图（所需概念、认知操作、文本线索、解题步骤、潜在错误）。2) 目标条件语义记忆：基于图级语义相似度与 Q 矩阵概念重叠，对每个目标题检索与目标认知操作相似的历史答题记录。3) 过程感知的 DA-MoE 映射器：将专家绑定到预定义的认知过程类型（如语义-符号翻译、关系映射、算术计算等 8 类），门控同时受输入表征与题目过程先验约束。4) 过程级监督对比学习：在过程粒度上对齐"掌握"与"未掌握"证据，避免粗粒度正负样本造成的误判。5) 心理测量学的猜测-滑落响应头建模习题级响应噪声。整体损失由响应预测负对数似然、过程级对比损失与 MoE 过程先验正则与负载均衡损失组成。

Result: 在 Junyi、XES3G5M、MOOC 三个真实数学数据集上，PLCD 在 ACC/AUC/RMSE 上均显著优于 IRT、MIRT、NCD、RCD、ACD、KaNCD、TechCD、KCD、DMC-CDM 等 ID 基线及知识增强基线（p<0.05）；在新概念/新习题/缺失 Q 矩阵三类冷启动设置中显著领先；消融实验证实结构化证据、目标条件记忆、过程感知映射器、过程级对比均不可或缺；机制验证表明语义+Q 联合检索的 Res.Corr.@5 远高于标签打乱变体，证明记忆依赖真实答题模式；t-SNE 显示潜在表征呈主题模块化、技能连续过渡、共享过程聚类的认知支架结构；教师人工评估 Gemini 3 Pro 生成的过程图在概念相关性、操作正确性、教学有用性上评分约 4.4-4.6/5，过程标签 Top-1 一致率 0.78，Fleiss' κ=0.72。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 生成的结构化认知证据作为先验、由响应记录通过目标条件记忆进行校准，可在认知诊断任务中替代以 ID 嵌入为核心的范式，提升稀疏与冷启动场景下的鲁棒性，并提供与认知心理学一致的可解释潜在结构。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [121] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VRL-Bench评估框架与VEX²方法，揭示现有基于语言记忆的试错学习方法在有限试验预算下未必优于简单重试，并通过让语言模型充当探索-利用调度器来改善跨试验决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反思的verbal强化学习方法（如Reflexion及其后续工作）声称能让语言智能体从失败中学习，但缺乏在有限试验预算下的公平评估框架。作者质疑这些方法是否真正优于简单的无记忆重试。

Method: 1) 提出VRL-Bench：公平评估试错学习的评估框架，匹配试验预算和交互机会，共享T₁。2) 在3个模型（DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GPT-5.4 nano、GLM-4.7-FlashX）和2个环境（MiniWoB、WebShop）上对比Reflexion、DC-Cu*、ACE*与无记忆重试。3) 通过回放干预实验（控制反思使用频率）揭示探索-利用权衡。4) 提出VEX²：使用语言模型作为探索-利用调度器，联合选择策略并分配剩余试验预算。

Result: 1) 现有三种verbal更新方法在6个设置中均无法一致优于无记忆重试，存在模型依赖性。2) 回放实验显示反思的早期优势可能消失，不同调度恢复互补的失败案例，揭示探索-利用权衡。3) VEX²是唯一在所有6个设置中都取得相对于重试的正向SR@6增益的方法（MiniWoB +1.6至+6.6 pp，WebShop +8.0至+17.0 pp），且在所有设置中降低censored AvgT@6。

Conclusion: 现有基于verbal记忆的试错学习方法在有限试验预算下并不一致优于简单重试；语言模型可以通过显式调度探索-利用来更有效地利用失败经验。VRL-Bench提供了公平评估试错学习的研究框架，VEX²为该方向提供了有前景的思路。局限：评估仅涵盖3个模型、2个环境、6次试验预算，且token消耗并非匹配预算。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [122] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型（LLM）及智能体（Agentic）AI系统中越狱攻击与防御的系统化知识综述（SoK）。研究发现，现代LLM虽然具备较强的原生安全对齐能力，但其在面对对抗性越狱攻击、跨组件执行链路时仍存在显著脆弱性，且现有防御在效用与效率上代价高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从对话助手向智能体AI系统演进（具备推理、规划、记忆、工具调用及多智能体通信能力），传统针对早期模型研究的越狱攻击与防御结论是否仍然适用尚不明确。论文旨在重新审视现代LLM及智能体系统中的越狱安全问题，弥合现有研究的空白。

Method: 1. 提出围绕智能体完整执行流水线的越狱安全分析框架。
2. 构建统一攻击与防御分类法，覆盖用户交互、规划与推理、记忆、工具调用及智能体间通信等层面。
3. 提出安全-效用-效率三维评估框架，分离原生有害提示安全、对抗越狱鲁棒性及智能体级安全结果。
4. 在统一智能体框架下对代表性攻击与防御进行受控实证研究。

Result: 研究揭示三大核心差距：
1. 强大的原生对齐能力并不等同于对对抗性越狱攻击的鲁棒性；
2. 防御效果高度依赖于模型、攻击类型及智能体组件，且会导致显著的过度拒绝、效用下降及延迟增加；
3. 即使最终响应被成功过滤（低攻击成功率），智能体在规划、记忆及工具交互等中间环节仍可能已被严重攻陷（即“中间妥协”被掩盖）。

Conclusion: 结论呼吁安全防御范式从“响应中心”转向“跨层级、执行感知”的安全机制，即在保护智能体状态、组件转换及外部操作的同时，维持实用性与执行效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [123] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HORIZON，一种针对Lux AI Season 3挑战的分层零样本对手自适应智能体，融合对称性等变空间感知、双重记忆信念跟踪、神器中心图注意力、信息增益驱动探索和对手条件策略混合，在部分可观测多智能体导航任务中显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3要求智能体在部分可观测、动力学随机化、五局三胜的赛制下，既要执行战术又要快速适应对手。现有循环策略无法清晰区分战术记忆与跨局级元信念，也未能利用地图几何与对手行为中的结构信息，导致元自适应能力不足。

Method: HORIZON包含五个紧密耦合组件：(1)对称性等变空间骨干(SESB)结合FiLM条件化处理光栅观测；(2)双重记忆信念编码器(DMBE)分离快速局内GRU状态与跨局慢速状态以推断隐参数θ；(4)神器中心图注意力(RC-GAT)对隐藏计分瓦片进行贝叶斯信念更新并注入注意力；(5)信息增益内在奖励(IGIR)驱动早期匹配探索；(6)对手条件策略混合(OCPM)通过贝叶斯后验选择四种风格子策略。使用PPO结合世界模型、信念一致性、θ回归和对手分类辅助损失在JAX并行模拟器中训练。

Result: 在500场五局三胜评估中，HORIZON在所有指标上均优于基线：MWR 0.72、EWR 0.77、元自适应增益+0.16、TrueSkill 29.1，均显著超越RuleBot、FlatPPO、IMPALA-LSTM和RL²-Recurrent。消融实验表明去除DMBE或IGIR最损害元自适应能力，去除RC-GAT对MWR和TrueSkill影响最大，每个组件均有独立价值。

Conclusion: HORIZON证明了显式双重记忆元推理、隐式神器图推理和对手感知策略混合的组合，为部分可观测、非平稳多智能体博弈提供了一种实用方案，其设计原则可推广至Lux Season 3之外的更广泛场景。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style. Experiments show consistent gains in match win rate, episode win rate, adaptation gain, and league rating over strong recurrent and feed forward baselines.

</details>


### [124] [LifeFuse-Mem: Lifecycle-Aware State Fusion Against Temporary Overwriting for Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12436)
*Hanyu Zhao,Yuqian Feng,Zhenyu Song,Yuanchao Cheng,Yance Jiao,Tengfei Pan,Li Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifeFuse-Mem 是一种面向长运行 LLM Agent 的生命周期感知神经记忆框架，通过子空间隔离、永久路由和阶段感知受保护读出，解决临时信息覆盖持久知识的"临时覆盖"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行 Agent 的紧凑在线记忆机制将不同时间有效性的信息（持久事实与临时假设）压缩到同一共享潜在状态中，导致临时上下文覆盖持久知识，引发行为漂移——现有记忆系统普遍忽视了信息生命周期差异。

Method: 三阶段框架：①学习正交基将内存秩空间划分为可塑子空间与稳定子空间；②通过监督式永久路由（permanence router）控制写入令牌对稳定行的更新强度，并对稳定行键坐标进行衰减隔离；③在受控协议下使用受保护状态融合读出——将 Phase A 检查点的稳定行与 Phase B 后状态的可塑行融合，永久查询融合两个状态得分，临时查询直接使用覆写后状态。训练采用情景式 write-then-read 目标，结合路由监督损失、正交正则化、硬候选对比损失、保留排序损失和稀疏性惩罚。

Result: 在 Hard Attribution Anti-Overwrite 基准（Qwen3-4B / SmolLM3-3B）上：获取控制保留率从 δ-Mem 的 57.72%/60.27% 提升至 63.71%/69.39%，覆写率从 42.28%/39.73% 降至 36.29%/30.61%。在 LoCoMo 和 MemoryAgentBench 公共基准上整体保持竞争力（LoCoMo 全面提升，MemoryAgentBench 类别效果混合）。消融实验表明路由监督是最关键组件（去掉后保留率大幅下降）。

Conclusion: 显式生命周期信号可有效诊断和缓解紧凑在线记忆中的覆盖问题，路由条件隔离是获取控制保留增益的最可靠来源；然而该工作未实现从无标签交互历史中自主发现生命周期，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents require memory mechanisms that maintain coherent internal states across interactions. We study a lifecycle-labeled memory setting in which write episodes provide lifecycle metadata during training, and phase-aware readout is used during evaluation. This setting reflects the need to distinguish information that should remain influential across future interactions from information that should affect only the current context. A mismatch between these lifecycles can cause temporary information to overwrite durable knowledge, leading to behavioral drift in persistent agents. Within this setting, we introduce \textbf{LifeFuse-Mem}, a lifecycle-aware neural memory framework that separates information according to its temporal commitment. LifeFuse-Mem uses dedicated memory components and lifecycle-aware updates to allow stable and transient knowledge to evolve locally without converting temporary context into durable state. On the controlled anti-overwrite benchmark, LifeFuse-Mem improves acquisition-controlled retention and reduces temporary overwrite; on two public long-memory benchmarks, it remains broadly competitive. These results suggest that explicit lifecycle signals can help diagnose and mitigate overwrite in compact online memory.

</details>


### [125] [Do LLMs Trust the Accuser or the Accusation? Measuring Belief Shifts in Werewolf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12446)
*Yu-Yu Yang,Ti-Rong Wu,Hung Guei,Hsing-Yu Chen,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于狼人杀的"信念偏移(belief-shift)"评估基准,通过标注LLM自对弈游戏中1,224条怀疑/指控消息,衡量40个开源LLM配置(1B–120B)在观察指控消息前后对"指控者"与"被指控者"信念的变化,核心发现是LLM普遍过度信任被指控内容,即使指控者为狼阵营,且当指控者已被信任时几乎无条件接受指控;模型规模越大,角色判别能力和对已不信任源的抵抗能力越强,但仍难以有效整合信源信任与指控内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有狼人杀/Avalon等社交推理游戏中的LLM评估多依赖最终胜负等粗粒度结果,难以揭示具体的沟通行为(尤其是接收者侧信念更新)的成败原因;本文旨在用"信念偏移"作为中间指标,衡量LLM在战略沟通中的信源信任整合能力。

Method: 1) 用7个开源LLM自对弈生成200场7人狼人杀;2) 用Claude Opus 4.6标注1,224条含"suspect"或"accuse"的消息,记录指控者、被指控者及其真实阵营;3) 对每条消息,从1–2名存活村民方视角,先让待评估模型基于可观测历史输出对指控者与被指控者的先验信念(7级Likert),再在加入指控消息及自身先验信念后输出后验信念偏移;4) 评估29个开源检查点共40个(checkpoint, reasoning-mode)配置,覆盖GLM/GPT-OSS/Gemma/Llama/Mistral/Nemotron/Olmo/Phi/Qwen等家族,使用vLLM推理并以Spearman秩相关分析规模效应。

Result: 1) 先验角色判别:狼与村民的信念分离度随规模从≤4B组的0.80提升至≥10B组的~1.30(ρ=0.54,p<0.001);2) 指控后整体偏向:被指控者向"狼侧"偏移(村庄指控者下0.98,狼指控者下0.74),指控者向"村民侧"偏移(−0.51/−0.34),直接指控强于软怀疑;3) 先验信任调节:信任指控者时无论其真实阵营,被指控者偏移接近(0.94 vs 0.96);不信任指控者时偏移减弱但仍存在;4) 规模可缓解但不消除"轻信"模式:≥30B模型对不信任的狼指控者产生反向目标偏移(−0.12)且更怀疑指控者(+0.47,ρ=0.63,p<0.001);5) 初步对6个闭源前沿模型的扩展测试显示其在不信任源条件下出现明显负向目标偏移,表明前沿模型对此失败模式有更强抵抗力。

Conclusion: 当前120B以下的开源LLM在战略沟通中难以将"指控内容"与"信源可信度"整合进行信念更新,呈现显著的"权威/信任偏向";更大的模型虽改善了角色判别与对不信任源的抵抗,但仍会过度信任受信源发出的指控(即使其为狼阵营),因此该框架应作为更细粒度的沟通诊断工具,而非综合性沟通能力评测,未来工作可拓展至辩护、提问、角色声明等其他沟通子技能、说者侧分析、跨游戏泛化及人机混合评估。

Abstract: Social-deduction games such as Werewolf are increasingly used to evaluate LLM agents, but existing evaluations often rely on final game outcomes. We propose a belief-shift evaluation benchmark in Werewolf for analyzing communication skills through belief updating. Using LLM-played games, we annotate suspicion and accusation messages and measure how an observing village-side model's beliefs change after each message. We evaluate 40 open-weight LLM configurations on 1,224 annotated messages. Our results show that larger models better distinguish true wolves from villagers based on game history, but accusations still strongly influence their beliefs. Models become more suspicious of the accused target and less suspicious of the accuser, especially when the accuser is trusted, even if the accuser is wolf-aligned. Larger models better resist accusations from accusers they already distrust. Overall, our findings suggest that current open-weight LLMs up to 120B parameters still struggle to integrate accusation content with source trust in strategic communication. Our benchmark and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [EvoRS: On-Policy Self-Evolution of Reward Systems for Open-Ended Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12459)
*Weiyuan Li,Aili Chen,Xintao Wang,Yikai Zhang,Qingqing Dong,Jinghan Xu,Hongru Hou,Wenxuan Zhao,Chengkun Lang,Jun Gao,Yuanli Guo,Hongcheng Guo,Yanghua Xiao,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoRS框架，将强化学习中的奖励系统建模为可执行的Reward-DAG，并利用agentic designer基于在线策略经验对其进行持续演化，以解决开放任务（写作/角色扮演）中固定奖励系统在策略优化过程中出现的reward hacking、覆盖不足和信息量下降等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放性任务的RL训练中，策略与奖励系统形成动态反馈循环：随着策略优化当前奖励，初始有效的奖励系统可能因reward hacking、覆盖不足和奖励信号信息量下降而失效。已有动态rubric方法仅调整评估标准，但奖励失效还可能源于评分机制和信号组合方式，因此需要让完整的奖励系统随策略共同演化。

Method: 1. 将完整奖励系统抽象为可执行的Reward-DAG D=(V,E,Θ)，包含rubric节点、checklist/verifier节点、flaw-penalty/guard节点和组合算子，覆盖评估什么、如何评分、如何组合信号三个维度。2. 每N步由agentic designer分析在线策略rollout和奖励trace，诊断失败模式（validity/coverage/informativeness），从合法操作空间生成M个有界候选状态。3. 在匹配的replay案例上对比当前状态与候选状态的奖励行为，通过执行验证、guard检查、health阈值、informativeness阈值和review分数等门槛筛选支持性候选，选择最优者作为下一状态；否则保留当前状态。4. 维护run-local记忆和动态技能以持续改进搜索过程。

Result: 在写作和角色扮演任务上，EvoRS在三位评判者（GPT-5.6-Terra、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、GLM-5.2）下均取得最高质量，分别比base policy提升2.107和4.767分；降低reward hacking率和coverage failure率，保持组内奖励区分能力。消融实验表明：仅调整评分标准、固定最终Reward-DAG、去除候选选择/拒绝/记忆机制均导致性能下降，尤其在角色扮演任务上降幅更大。在Qwen3-8B策略和不同GRM上也验证了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 即使训练前精心设计的全面奖励系统也无法在开放性任务中始终保持可靠，奖励系统必须随策略共同演化。EvoRS通过将完整奖励系统表示为可执行Reward-DAG，基于在线策略证据进行诊断和有界搜索，验证了自演化奖励系统在策略学习全过程中的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Open-ended reinforcement learning often relies on rubric-based rewards for tasks without directly verifiable answers. Yet the policy and reward system form a dynamic feedback loop: as the policy optimizes the current reward, an initially useful reward system may become unreliable due to reward hacking or reduced response discriminability. The reward system should therefore evolve rather than remain fixed during training. Existing dynamic-rubric methods adapt evaluation criteria, but reward failures can also arise from scoring mechanisms or signal composition. We introduce EvoRS, a self-evolving RL framework that evolves the reward system from on-policy experience, representing it as an executable Reward-DAG. Specifically, an agentic designer updates this system from on-policy rollouts and reward traces to maintain train-time reliability. Across writing and roleplay, EvoRS achieves the best quality under all three judges, outperforming the policy by \(2.107\) and \(4.767\) points, respectively, while reducing reward hacking and coverage failures and preserving reward informativeness. Ablations confirm that a comprehensive fixed reward system cannot remain reliable in open-ended tasks and must evolve throughout training.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Vector Similarity: Hierarchical Context-Aware Graph RAG vs Standard RAG in Enterprise Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12464)
*Nilesh Jaiswal,Aniket Agrawal,Arjit Shukla,Divya Malhotra,Saurabh Garg,Suchit Puri,Suddhasatwa Bhaumik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于企业遗留代码迁移的层次化上下文驻留图（HCRG）Graph RAG 方法，与标准向量 RAG 进行对比。通过 tree-sitter 提取 AST、Spanner 属性图建模、Gemini Context Cache 缓存架构骨架，并提出 7 维 SE 评估框架，证实 Graph RAG 显著降低 API 幻觉率并提升依赖解析质量，但会因过度工程化导致圈复杂度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业将遗留 Java 单体系统迁移到 Python 微服务时，标准向量 RAG 因文本切片切断继承链，导致高编译失败率和 API 幻觉。传统词汇重叠指标（如 CodeBLEU）会掩盖结构性失败，需要架构感知的检索与评估方法。

Method: 1) 用 tree-sitter 解析多语言 AST，提取类、方法、INHERITS/CALLS/IMPORTS 等边；2) 将节点和边存入 Google Cloud Spanner Property Graph，同时嵌入 ARRAY<FLOAT64> 向量；3) 将"架构骨架"（JSON 序列化）通过 Vertex AI Context Cache 注入 LLM 工作记忆；4) 用 GQL 做 DAG 拓扑排序，先翻译父类再翻译子类；5) 提出 7 维 SE 评估指标（CMQ、PCC、THC、StaQ、DRQ、CCC、DP）与 Global Lookup 防误判机制；6) 在 spring-petclinic-genai 仓库进行 Java→Python 自动迁移对比实验。

Result: Graph RAG vs Standard RAG：API 幻觉率 56.4%→16.2%；DRQ 34.8%→65.9%；PCC 26.7%→45.5%；THC 51.4%→80.9%；MQ 65.5%→83.2%。但 CCC 因防御性过度工程从 71.6% 降至 46.7%，DP 从 67.0% 降至 61.0%。两者 CodeBLEU 几乎相同（约 91%），证明词汇指标失效。高耦合 Controller 类上 Graph RAG 优势最显著（MQ 从 3% 跃升至 77-80%），简单 POJO 上两者无差异。

Conclusion: Graph RAG 以代码复杂度上升为代价，换取关键的结构完整性与可编译性，是企业代码现代化更可行的路径。未来工作将开发 Hybrid RAG 架构以兼顾架构完整性与圈复杂度简洁性，并扩展对反射、泛型擦除和动态依赖的处理。

Abstract: As enterprises modernize legacy monolithic systems to microservices, Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily utilized for automated code translation. However, traditional vector-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Standard RAG) struggles to capture topological relationships. It fetches isolated chunks that sever inheritance chains, leading to high compilation failure rates. This paper introduces a Hierarchical Context-Resident Graph (HCRG) methodology to resolve these limitations. Our pipeline uses tree-sitter for Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) extraction, maps architectural edges into a Google Cloud Spanner Property Graph, and serializes this structure into a Gemini Context Cache for topological, parent-first code translation. We shift evaluation from naive text-overlap to a custom 7-metric Software Engineering framework. Traditional metrics like CodeBLEU (which scored 91% for both methods) effectively masked Standard RAG's structural failures behind syntactically plausible but broken code. Empirically, Graph RAG decisively mitigates dependency loss: API hallucination rates dropped from 56.4% to 16.2%, Dependency Resolution Quality improved from 34.8% to 65.9%, and Parent-Child Consistency rose from 26.7% to 45.5%. However, Graph RAG introduces specific trade-offs. The dense global context causes defensive over-engineering by the LLM, reducing Cyclomatic Complexity Consistency from 71.6% to 46.7%, and slightly degrades Docstring Preservation (67.0% to 61.0%). Ultimately, while trading code complexity for reduced hallucinations, Graph RAG provides a substantially more viable, architecturally sound path for automated enterprise codebase modernization.

</details>


### [128] [TripPattern: A Pattern-based Text Watermarking Method for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12472)
*Sangjun Moon,Dasom Choi,Jingun Kwon,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TripPattern 是一种基于模式匹配的大语言模型文本水印方法，通过将词表三分为一个中性组和两个模式组，在生成时交替选择两个模式组的 token 以嵌入可检测模式，中性 token 独立采样以保持文本自然度；检测端基于相邻模式组 token 的交替频率进行统计检验，理论证明其比 KGW 两分组框架具有更大的 token 选择灵活性。在英、韩、德、西四种语言上的实验表明，该方法在保持生成质量的同时实现了稳健的水印检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 KGW 框架的两分组（水印/非水印）水印方法通过对绿色 token 施加偏置来嵌入水印信号，但这种限制降低了 token 选择的灵活性，尤其在多语言场景下会显著损害文本质量和自然度。如何在保证水印可检测性的同时保留更大的生成灵活性，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出 TripPattern 框架，将词表划分为一个中性组 V_n 和两个模式组 V_{p1}、V_{p2}。生成时维护一个交替状态变量 s_prev：上一个非中性 token 若属于 V_{p1}，则当前允许集合偏向 V_{p2}∪V_n，反之亦然；对允许集合施加软偏置 δ。中性组按子词长度优先构建（短 token 优先），阈值设为 3。检测端重建每步的三分组，剔除中性 token，统计相邻模式 token 的交替次数 C，使用 Wald-Wolfowitz 游程检验在排列零假设下计算 z 值。理论上证明当 (1+γ_n)/2 ≥ γ_g 时，TripPattern 的允许 token 集合严格大于 KGW。

Result: 在 Llama-3.2-3B/3.1-8B 和 Qwen-2.5-3B/7B 上，使用 C4/mC4（英/韩/德/西）评测流畅度，GSM8K/MGSM 评测推理能力，CNN/DM/GovReport 评测摘要质量。TripPattern 在四语平均 PPL 上与 KGW 相当或更优（如 Qwen-2.5-3B：5.75 vs 7.60；Qwen-2.5-7B：4.80 vs 5.70），平均 AUC 达 0.984–0.995，TPR@5 达 0.960–0.989；推理任务上保持与无水印相近的准确率；摘要任务中人工评测在信息量、简洁性、事实一致性上均显著优于 KGW（p<0.01）。子词长度阈值设为 3 时效果最佳。对 WordDel/WordSub 攻击鲁棒，但对 Dipper 上下文释义攻击较敏感（AUC 降至 0.675）。

Conclusion: TripPattern 通过三分组（两个交替模式组 + 一个中性组）的词表划分方案，将水印嵌入建模为模式匹配任务，相比传统 KGW 两分组框架提供了更大的 token 选择灵活性和更好的文本自然度，尤其在多语言场景下实现了文本质量与水印可检测性的更优平衡。子词长度作为中性组构建的启发式规则进一步提升了流畅度。该方法的局限性在于对语义级释义攻击（如 Dipper）鲁棒性较差，且检测性能在多语言高确定性分布下会有所下降。

Abstract: Text watermarking techniques have gained significant attention for identifying machine-generated text and mitigating risks from large language models (LLMs). Existing methods typically divide an LLM's vocabulary into green and red tokens, but encouraging generation toward green tokens can reduce text quality and naturalness. To address this, we propose TripPattern, a watermarking framework that formulates text watermarking as a pattern-based matching task using three vocabulary partitions. TripPattern divides the vocabulary into one neutral group and two pattern groups. During generation, the model alternates token selection between the two pattern groups to embed detectable patterns, while neutral tokens are selected independently to improve flexibility and preserve naturalness. For detection, TripPattern uses pattern-based statistical tests that provide interpretable p-values by measuring how often adjacent tokens alternate between the pattern groups. Theoretical analysis and empirical evaluations on four multilingual datasets show that TripPattern maintains LLM generation quality while achieving robust watermark detectability.

</details>


### [129] [When Does AI Augment Work? A Workflow-Level Framework for Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12482)
*CIVIC-AI Collaboration,Jiaying Wu,Caleb Ziems,Raymond Chan,Nancy F. Chen,Corlyss Chua,Gerard Chung,Jungpil Hahn,Wee Sun Lee,Zhengyuan Liu,Jamie Ng,Desmond C. Ong,Jeryl Ong,Da Ren Soon,Tianqi Song,Zhi-Xuan Tan,Sixing Tao,Emily Yang,Yajing Yang,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前以"任务级自动化能力"和"AI 公众采纳率"来评估 AI 职场价值的做法，主张以"工作流（workflow）"作为分析单元，提出衡量 AI 是否实现"真正增强（genuine augmentation）"的六项条件框架，并通过 AI 介导的社会调查案例与新加坡劳动力市场数据进行应用说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以任务粒度衡量 AI 价值，忽略了人机协作对工作本质的重塑；同时现有治理框架虽强调以人为本与问责，但对工作流层面的评估方法、如何避免人类监督能力被侵蚀等问题缺乏指导。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出可操作的评估框架。

Method: 1) 概念框架法：构建六项增强条件（分两层：工作流完整性快照层 + 人类发展纵向层）；2) 案例研究法：以 AI 介导的社会调查（如 SparkMe 智能访谈员）说明人机任务划分与监督机制；3) 政策分析法：以新加坡劳动力统计数据检验框架适用性；4) 提出"工作流记录（workflow record）"作为落地工具。
