TL;DR: 提出QTrans模型，将参数化量子电路引入Transformer，用于小规模二分类情感分析。在MR、CR、MPQA数据集上以仅约2.4万参数超越参数匹配的经典基线2.88-3.79个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模二分类情感分析中，否定、转折、跨词依赖等导致情感线索非线性耦合，经典轻量模型难以充分建模上下文关系；经典Transformer的Q/K/V为线性映射，特征区分度受限，扩大规模又会过拟合。需要一种轻量级机制，在不显著增加参数的前提下增强投影表达能力。

Method: 1) 整体框架：序列嵌入→高斯投影量子多头自注意力→量子前馈网络→情感读出，含残差、LayerNorm、Dropout与因果掩码，用[SEP]位置表征分类；2) 量子多头自注意力：Q/K/V三个独立参数化量子电路（n量子比特，强纠缠ansatz，环纠缠CNOT+Dq层RY），测量Pauli-Z得到标量查询/键与n维值，注意力系数由查询-键高斯距离exp(-(q-k)²)经掩码归一化得到，值向量均分为两个头拼接融合；3) 量子前馈网络：独立参数化电路，n维测量经GELU+线性映射回残差空间；4) 损失为标签平滑交叉熵，端到端联合优化。实例化n=4、Dq=2、64个量子参数+经典参数共24382。

Result: 在MR/CR/MPQA上测试准确率72.13%/69.51%/63.45%，宏F1同步最优，较各自最强经典基线（参数差异<1%）分别提升2.88/3.17/3.79个百分点。消融显示量子多头自注意力在三数据集上一致提升（MPQA提升6.7pp），量子前馈网络提升因数据集而异（MR/MPQA提升，CR略降）。σ≤0.1高斯嵌入噪声下基本稳定，σ=0.3/0.5时MR/CR明显退化；实验为模拟器、固定种子单次运行，未涉及量子硬件噪声。

Conclusion: QTrans证明了将量子多头自注意力与量子前馈网络集成到轻量Transformer的可行性，为量子自注意力建模文本关系提供了实验基础。局限在于固定种子评估、数据集偏小、模拟器实现且缺乏门错误/退相干/读出噪声分析。未来工作包括多随机种子验证、更大更多样语料、替代测量头配置以及在含噪模拟器与真实量子硬件上的评估。

Abstract: In small-scale binary sentiment classification scenarios, factors such as negation, contrastive shifts, and cross-word dependencies lead to the non-linear coupling of sentiment cues, making it difficult for conventional lightweight models to fully capture the contextual relationships between tokens. To address this issue, we propose a model named QTrans, which uses parameterized quantum circuits to construct query, key, and value features and derives attention coefficients from Gaussian distances between quantum measurements. By further integrating a quantum feed-forward neural network, residual connections, and layer normalization, the model establishes an end-to-end trainable quantum-classical hybrid framework for sentiment classification. Experimental results on the MR, CR, and MPQA datasets show that QTrans achieves test accuracies of 72.13\%, 69.51\%, and 63.45\%, respectively, representing improvements of 2.88, 3.17, and 3.79 percentage points over the best-performing classical baselines for each dataset. Overall, QTrans expands the application of parameterized quantum circuits in lightweight sentiment analysis and lays an experimental foundation for further research into quantum multi-head self-attention for modeling textual relationships.

</details>


### [216] [Certified Safety Curation: Distribution-Free Guarantees for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12014)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"认证安全策展"(Certified Safety Curation),一种在仅能获得轨迹段比较偏好和偶尔的预算超支判断两种弱监督信号下,从离线数据集中筛选并克隆安全轨迹的filter-then-clone流水线。核心创新在于通过Learn-then-Test校准为所选训练集的组成提供(α,δ)无分布保证,并通过oracle受控分析证明轨迹级选择(而非逐转移重加权)是必要设计选择。在DSRL基准上达到11/15任务满足成本预算,与需要全程标签的BC-Safe的12/15相当接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全离线强化学习方法需要为每个转移提供成本函数,但实践中往往无法可靠地指定成本函数;若仅能通过比较短片段判断相对安全性,以及偶尔判断整条轨迹是否超出预算,则能实现何种安全离线RL是一个开放问题。作者希望探索比成本函数弱得多的监督信号下安全离线RL的可能性,并为所选训练集的组成提供统计保证——此前尚无工作对离线RL或模仿学习的训练集组成进行认证。

Method: 方法包含三个阶段:(1)用Bradley-Terry段比较偏好训练仅依赖状态的K=3个价值网络集成,得到轨迹评分g(τ)=平均V̄(s_t);(2)Learn-then-Test校准:在阈值网格{0.85,0.80,...,0.30}上进行固定序列超几何检验,以200条预算超支二元标签对阈值进行校准,以无分布(α,δ)保证所选训练集中不安全轨迹比例不超过α;(3)对所选轨迹集进行行为克隆。关键理论结果包括:引理1给出条件超几何分布;引理2证明p值在零假设下超均匀性;命题1给出组合保证;命题2给出认证二分律(若最小可达不安全比例>α则认证概率≤δ,若顶层阈值的不安全比例<α则n→N时认证概率→1);命题4给出认证概率闭合形式。设计合理性通过oracle受控分析(命题3走廊构造)证明轨迹级选择相对于逐转移重加权的结构性优势。

Result: 在DSRL 15个任务上:校准过滤器满足成本预算的11/15(包含未认证回退),未校准V-filter为12/15,BC-Safe(需全程标签)为12/15。CDT作为最强全标签基线仅在7/15任务上满足预算,且在8个Goal和Button任务上无论成本目标如何设置均违反。CDT在认证选择上重训练后变为安全(3任务的27/27运行)。BulletSafetyGym迁移测试中所有5个任务均满足预算。认证在α=0.25下触发3/15任务,α=0.40下触发8/9分析任务;无条件错误认证率在所有验证单元中均低于δ=0.1(最差0.070)。认证率由"纯度裕度"(α减去可达最小不安全比例)决定,Spearman相关0.99。

Conclusion: 工作表明:在所研究的领域中,安全可仅从状态在轨迹尺度上解码,从远比成本函数弱的监督中解码;全标签分类器在安全任务覆盖上与此路线相当,方法间的差别在于认证及其拒绝能力。认证选择可转移至任何离线学习器,使最强的全标签基线在原本违反的任务上变为安全。离线数据界定了任何方法所能达到的上限,在策略训练或运行之前对数据本身进行认证是一种可获得的保证。开放问题包括尾部鲁棒评分、组成到策略安全性的映射紧致化、以及标签预算与认证率之间的精确权衡。

Abstract: Safe offline reinforcement learning assumes a cost function on every transition. We ask what remains possible when safety can be judged only by comparing short clips and occasionally asking whether an episode exceeded its budget. Certified safety curation answers with a filter-then-clone pipeline: a state-only value trained from segment comparisons scores whole trajectories, Learn-then-Test calibration certifies a selection threshold under a distribution-free $(\alpha, \delta)$ bound on the unsafe fraction of the selection, and behavior cloning follows. We are not aware of prior work certifying the composition of a training set for offline RL or imitation. Oracle controls justify the design: reweighting individual transitions fails even with an exact value, so the value selects whole trajectories. The policies satisfy the cost budget on eleven of fifteen DSRL tasks, one short of cloning the ground-truth safe subset, which needs a label on every trajectory; the uncertified variant reaches twelve. Retrained on the certified selection, the strongest full-label method becomes safe where no setting of its own cost target rescues it. Refusal is predictable: the certificate's probability has a closed form in the purity the pool attains, which the calibration sample estimates and the scorer enters only through.

</details>


### [217] [Inverting Self-Triggered Control: Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Sparse Denial-of-Service Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12016)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Tristan Schuler,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性强化学习方法，反向求解对自触发控制（STC）系统最具破坏性的稀疏DoS/干扰调度。通过Lyapunov增长可接受性谓词镜像防御者的安全证书，证明了对满足Lyapunov契约的STC控制器，最小干扰次数的下界，并作为推论恢复了先前计数预算DoS调度的连续分组最优性的证书级类比。将DoS调度计数预算分析从周期/LTI控制器扩展到STC控制器。实验在Pendulum、CartPole和Quadrotor2D上针对四种固定防御者进行训练，学到的对抗者是唯一在每个植物上对每个防御者实现100%崩溃率的对抗者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自触发强化学习控制（RL-STC）通过学习最稀疏的控制调度来保证Lyapunov递减稳定性。然而，对此类系统最有效的稀疏DoS攻击调度（即攻击者侧的对偶问题）尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过对抗性RL学习对STC控制器最具破坏性的稀疏干扰调度。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 形式化对抗问题，使用Lyapunov增长可接受性谓词镜像RL-STC的安全证书；(2) 证明对满足Lyapunov契约的STC控制器的即时保持上次介质访问控制对手强制崩溃所需最小干扰次数的下界；(3) 恢复计数预算DoS调度的连续分组最优性的证书级类比；(4) 通过对抗性RL训练，生成针对四种固定防御者（一个LQR和三个RL-STC）的攻击调度；(5) 在Pendulum、CartPole、Quadrotor2D上进行实证评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 学到的对抗者是唯一在每个植物上对每个防御者实现100%崩溃率的攻击者；(2) 贪心基线在Quadrotor2D LQR上有42%的回合未能崩溃，周期基线在Pendulum LQR上有97%的回合未能崩溃；(3) 在每次失败的干扰时间指标上，学到的对抗者比基线最多好2.8倍；(4) 在Quadrotor2D LQR上表现出最大的绝对优势；(5) 鲁棒性消融实验表明，超过初始状态幅度的高斯观测噪声和仅位置观测均能保持100%的失败率。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将DoS调度计数预算分析从周期/LTI控制器扩展到自触发控制器，并提供了一种通过对抗性RL学习最优稀疏干扰调度的方法。学到的对抗策略在崩溃率和干扰效率两方面均显著优于基线，且具有鲁棒性。这为网络物理系统的安全分析提供了重要工具，特别是在自触发控制场景下。

Abstract: Self-triggered reinforcement learning control (RL-STC) learns the sparsest control schedule that preserves Lyapunov-decreasing stability under a Run-Time Assurance (RTA) override. We invert this: an adversarial RL agent learns the sparsest jamming or Denial-of-Service (DoS) schedule that destabilizes the closed loop, with a Lyapunov-increase admissibility predicate mirroring the defender's safety certificate. We prove a plant-property lower bound on the minimum jam count required for an immediate hold-last medium-access-control adversary to force a crash against a self-triggered controller (STC) satisfying a Lyapunov contract, and recover a certificate-level analog of the consecutive-grouping optimality of prior count-budget DoS scheduling as a corollary. This extends the DoS-scheduling count-budget analysis from periodic and linear-time-invariant to STC controllers. Empirically, we train against four fixed defenders per plant (one Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) and three RL-STC) on Pendulum, CartPole, and Quadrotor2D. The learned adversary is the only adversary that crashes every defender on every plant at $100\%$: greedy misses Quadrotor2D LQR on $42\%$ of episodes and periodic misses Pendulum LQR on $97\%$. On jam-time-per-failure it beats baselines by up to $2.8\times$, and shows its widest absolute margin on Quadrotor2D LQR. Robustness ablations show that Gaussian observation noise exceeding the initial-state magnitude and position-only observation both preserve $100\%$ failure rate and keep the learned adversary strictly ahead of both baselines on jam-time-per-failure.

</details>


### [218] [Toward Reliable Railway-Bogie Response Prediction Using Multifidelity TDNN and Physics-Informed Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12018)
*Gyeolhee Lee,Moosun Kim,Taewook Kwon,Jaehun Kim,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多保真度TDNN与物理信息残差学习方法，将多体动力学仿真作为低保真度信息、滚轮试验台测量作为高保真度证据，用于修正铁路转向架多通道响应预测。在385 km/h留出条件下达到平均R²=0.8197，NRMSE=4.6055%，NMAE=1.9297%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车辆动力学仿真与实测之间存在系统偏差（来自悬挂不确定性、轮轨边界差异、传感器行为等），有限工况的标定无法保证未测试工况的预测精度。需要一种能够利用仿真提供条件相关趋势、同时利用测量修正系统偏差的方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) TDNN基线模型——利用仿真历史和运行条件学习条件相关趋势，开发阶段拟合振幅对齐因子ρ*得到对齐基线；(2) 物理信息残差修正网络——通过有效动力学平衡方程（包含惯性、阻尼、刚度差异和外部力的差异矩阵）约束学习到的残差修正量。训练目标结合残差匹配、时间平滑度、选择性施加的位移-加速度一致性项和有效动力学残差项。开发条件为300–360 km/h，评估条件为385 km/h。

Result: 在385 km/h留出评估条件下，提出的差异修正方法达到：平均R²=0.8197，平均NRMSE=4.6055%，平均NMAE=1.9297%，四个通道R²均超过0.79。优于仅实验PINN（R²=0.5027）和TDNN辅助残差模型（R²=0.6515）。后验oracle时间对齐后R²可达0.9536，表明剩余不匹配主要来自时间未对准而非波形形状误差。

Conclusion: 该方法将低/高保真度多体仿真/实验数据与有效动力学约束相结合来学习仿真未能解释的响应分量，扩展了铁路多保真建模的范畴。385 km/h单一评估条件提供了超开发速度范围外推预测的初步证据，但不足以建立对更广泛运行域的泛化能力。未来工作需扩展独立评估集、执行受控消融实验、验证传感器-坐标映射并量化预测不确定性。

Abstract: Railway engineers need simulation models that predict vehicle responses across operating scenarios that cannot be tested exhaustively. Agreement with representative measurements provides essential evidence, but calibration at a limited set of conditions does not guarantee accuracy elsewhere. We present a multifidelity railway-bogie response-correction method that treats multibody simulation histories as low-fidelity information and roller-rig measurements as high-fidelity evidence. This method combines an experiment-anchored fidelity assignment with physics-informed discrepancy learning for multichannel bogie-response histories. A time-delay neural network (TDNN) represents the condition-dependent simulation trend, and development-fitted amplitude alignment defines the low-fidelity baseline. A residual-correction network then models the reproducible response component not explained by this baseline and adds it to the baseline. An effective dynamic-balance equation constrains the learned discrepancy by representing differences in inertia, damping, stiffness, and external forcing between the simulated and physical systems. The training objective combines this constraint with residual matching, temporal smoothness, and selectively applied displacement-acceleration consistency terms. For the evaluated reconstruction case, the corrected response gives a mean coefficient of determination of 0.8197, a mean normalized root-mean-square error (NRMSE) of 4.6055 %, and a mean normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) of 1.9297 %. These results provide initial evidence of accurate response prediction at the held-out 385 km/h condition.

</details>


### [219] [Reinforcement Learning for Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12020)
*John Zhuoyang Ye,Aarav Pabla,Jens Palsberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastSched利用强化学习（PPO）和重要性采样，自动搜索量子纠错码的症状提取调度，显著降低逻辑错误率（LER），在所有规模上优于AlphaSyndrome和PropHunt。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错中症状提取的CNOT门顺序组合呈指数级增长，不同调度在容错能力上差异巨大。现有自动调度工具（AlphaSyndrome使用MCTS，PropHunt使用MaxSAT）在方案质量与可扩展性之间存在权衡，难以同时兼顾。

Method: 将调度合成建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用Actor-Critic架构和PPO算法，状态编码包括历史CNOT顺序、校验矩阵、校验类型和权重等信息。针对LER估计中逻辑失败罕见的问题，采用重要性采样放大错误概率以提高失败观测频率，并使用似然权重校正恢复目标噪声下的无偏估计。使用有效样本量（ESS）作为自适应停止准则。通过三阶段流程（PPO训练、候选重评估、局部改进）输出固定调度。

Result: 在AlphaSyndrome全部11个电路上平均LER降低25.9%，在PropHunt全部8个电路上平均降低71.7%。在距离15的表面码上实现97.8%的LER降低（3.62×10⁻ vs 1.678×10⁷）。重要性采样节省40%-64%的训练采样开销。在低噪声条件下优势更显著。候选重评估是消融实验中贡献最大的组件（66.0%），局部改进贡献21.0%的最终优势。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合重要性采样有效解决了症状提取调度合成中的质量与可扩展性权衡问题。噪声放大使罕见逻辑失败对学习过程有用，重要性权重将放大样本转换为目标噪声水平下的LER估计。该方法为直接失败样本稀缺时的调度优化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: A key subtask of quantum error correction is to extract a syndrome that, if nontrivial, signals an error. The number of possible ways to extract a syndrome grows exponentially with the syndrome size, and these implementations vary greatly in fault tolerance, as measured by their logical error rates. This creates a natural search problem: find an implementation with a low logical error rate. Previous work solves this problem but sacrifices either solution quality or scalability. In this paper, we use reinforcement learning and importance sampling to outperform previous work at all scales. Compared with the state of the art automatic scheduling tools AlphaSyndrome and PropHunt, our tool reduces the logical error rate by 25.9\% and 71.7\% on average, respectively, culminating with a reduction of 97.8\% for a surface code with distance 15.

</details>


### [220] [Scalable Discrete-to-Continuous Channel Simulation for Compression and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12067)
*Joseph Rowan,Buu Phan,Ashish J. Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"排列方案"(permuted scheme)的方法,用于离散输入到连续输出信道的信道模拟,该方法使用固定数量的共享随机样本,运行时间独立于信道和输入。结合极化码和多级编码(MLC),可扩展至长块长度,时间复杂度为O(n log n)。应用包括VQ-VAE可变率压缩和中心差分隐私分布式均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道模拟算法(如PFR、GRS等)存在计算成本高、随机停止时间或最坏情况下需要生成无限多共享随机样本的问题。对于离散输入连续输出信道,缺乏一种使用固定样本数且运行时独立于信道与输入的通用模拟方案,且现有方法无法有效扩展到高维长块长度场景。

Method: 核心方法为"排列方案":首先生成每个潜在目标分布的一个(或固定多个)样本,施加一个潜在随机置换Π打乱索引,然后使用EFR(指数函数表示)中的指数竞赛机制进行样本选择和编码。具体包括:(1)单次方案使用2N个样本,通过Sinkhorn-Knopp近似处理大字母表;(2)多样本方案中证明通信成本可渐近逼近I(X;Y);(3)通过极化码框架将N=2情形视为带边信息的BSC;(4)利用多级编码(MLC)扩展到N=2^m情形,复杂度O(n log n)。

Result: 理论结果包括:(a)排列方案精确模拟P(Y|X)(命题1);(b)通信成本上界为I(X;Y|U^N)+log(I(X;Y|U^N)+2)+3(定理1);(c)二元输入下随每模式样本数m→∞,成本趋近I(X;Y)(定理2);(d)修正PolarSim算法(Algorithm 2)能以O(n log n)时间模拟带边信息的二元输出信道(定理4)。实验上:(1)VQ-VAE图像压缩中,单模型实现1.6-7.8 bits/latent的可变率压缩,优于IS基线;(2)中心DP分布式均值估计中,在4096维块长度下,速率显著低于PFR(例如ε=0.05时3.06 vs 58.7 bits/sample),编码时间约3秒处理128个块。

Conclusion: 本文成功为离散到连续信道模拟提出了一种使用固定数量共享随机样本的高效方案,通过引入潜在随机置换打破了朴素方案的局限性。借助极化码与多级编码的结合,使方法可扩展到长块长度。该工作将信道模拟技术有效应用于神经压缩和差分隐私两个重要机器学习场景,表明针对虽受限但实际相关的信道类别寻找可扩展构造是有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Channel simulation has recently emerged as a useful component in machine learning systems where samples from a prescribed probability distribution are to be compressed. Yet, general channel simulation algorithms often suffer from high computational costs, random stopping times or, in the worst case, can require generating an infinite number of shared random samples. We introduce a scheme for both exact and approximate simulation of discrete-to-continuous channels which conversely uses a fixed number of random samples, and therefore has a runtime independent of the channel and the input. Unlike existing channel simulation schemes which generate a sequence of independent samples from a proposal distribution, our approach generates one sample, or alternatively a fixed number of samples, from each potential target distribution. We then apply a latent permutation to the samples before performing sample selection using an exponential race. Our scheme provides a flexible tradeoff between the number of generated samples and the compression rate. Using polar and multilevel coding, we scale our approach to handle long blocklengths in $O(n \log n)$ time in order to benefit from reduced per-symbol overhead. We conclude by demonstrating applications to variable-rate compression with stochastic VQ-VAEs and communication-efficient differentially private distributed mean estimation via exact simulation of the Gaussian mechanism.

</details>


### [221] [Score-based Outlier Generation via Controlling the Radon-Nikodym Derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12113)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Tristan Milne,Kry Yik-Chau Lui,Stephanie Hazlewood,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于测度论的扩散模型异常值生成框架，通过控制 Radon-Nikodym 导数来调控似然分布，从而在无需重新训练扩散模型的前提下生成低似然样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常值生成方法多依赖启发式指标（重构误差、潜空间距离等），缺乏对似然本身的显式控制，且往往需要专门的架构或训练目标，缺乏统计可解释性。

Method: 1) 在分布层面定义异常值：通过对数似然的推前测度引入一阶随机占优和 Wasserstein-1 距离，定义 ρ-异常测度；2) 证明似然重加权诱导分数函数的乘法修正项，由 RN 导数给出；3) 利用 OU 半群的指数衰减性质，提出指数插值近似控制器 c̃_t = e^{-t} c_0，并给出误差界；4) 用平移卡方分布拟合参考似然分布 P_a 并构造目标 P_b，得到常数 c_0；5) 将控制器嵌入反向 PF-ODE 实现异常值采样。

Result: 高斯混合实验：W1 距离与目标 ρ 在各维度（n=1,4,16,64,256）上均高度吻合；CIFAR-10 实验：W1 距离随 ρ 增大单调递增（71.2→342.1），样本视觉上仍保持数据分布结构。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可被视为可控系统，RN 导数提供了无需重训练的分数修正机制；该框架开辟了分布引导和函数约束下生成建模的新方向。

Abstract: Outliers are important for stress-testing algorithms and understanding system behaviour under rare conditions. Despite being commonly described as low-likelihood events, existing generative approaches rarely control likelihood explicitly. In this work, we introduce a measure-theoretic notion of outliers based on the distribution of log-likelihood values, which is guaranteed to assign higher probability mass to low-likelihood events with a specifiable magnitude. Building on this formulation, we derive how likelihood reweighting modifies the diffusion score and use this relation to motivate a controlled modification of the reverse-time dynamics. In particular, likelihood reweighting implies a scaling of the score function with a control term derived from the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the likelihood distributions. Correspondingly, the updated score function can be obtained with no retraining of the diffusion model. We exploit the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck semigroup underlying diffusion models to motivate an exponentially interpolated controller which approximates the true control. Experiments demonstrate controlled generation of low-likelihood samples while remaining consistent with the data geometry.

</details>


### [222] [Almost Sure Convergence Analysis of Stochastic Gradient Methods with Clipping and Additive Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12119)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了带梯度裁剪与加性高斯噪声的随机梯度下降方法（SGD-CN）及其动量变体（随机重球法SHB-CN、Nesterov加速梯度SNAG-CN）在光滑性与一致有界随机梯度噪声假设下几乎处处（a.s.）收敛，并给出了最佳迭代收敛速率与末迭代收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度裁剪会引入随机梯度的偏差，加性噪声则带来额外方差，使得传统无偏SGD的收敛理论不再适用。尽管此类方法在鲁棒性与差分隐私优化中应用广泛，但其个体轨迹的长时间行为缺乏严格的理论刻画。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于超鞅收敛命题（Proposition 1）与辅助引理（Lemma 1、Lemma 2），通过构造Lyapunov型能量函数，利用期望裁剪梯度与真实梯度的对齐性质（Proposition 2，关键条件为裁剪阈值 q > σ_g），结合Lipschitz光滑性与一致有界噪声假设，对三种方法分别建立几乎处处收敛性与收敛速率分析，并通过控制振荡条件进一步证明末迭代收敛。

Result: 证明：（1）当 q > σ_g、步长 α_t = Θ(1/t^{1-θ})（θ∈(0,1/2)）时，SGD-CN、SHB-CN和SNAG-CN的加权和 ∑α_t Ψ_q(‖∇f(x_t)‖) 几乎处处有限；（2）最佳迭代满足 min Ψ_q(‖∇f(x_i)‖) = o(t^{-θ}) a.s.，梯度范数 min ‖∇f(x_i)‖ = o(t^{-θ/2}) a.s.；（3）在μ-强凸条件下，min(f(x_i)-f*) = o(t^{-θ}) a.s.；（4）通过振荡条件控制，所有三种方法均满足 ‖∇f(x_t)‖→0 a.s.（末迭代收敛）。

Conclusion: 本文为带裁剪与加性噪声的随机梯度方法提供了更强的逐路径（pathwise）理论保证，表明尽管存在裁剪偏差和持续高斯扰动，算法在凸与非凸情形下均保持稳定。开放问题包括：将有界噪声假设弱化为有界方差假设，以及精确刻画收敛性与裁剪阈值、噪声水平之间的定量关系。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with gradient clipping and additive noise has become a standard technique for training machine learning models, particularly in applications requiring robustness or privacy guarantees. However, clipping introduces a bias in stochastic gradients, while additive noise introduces additional variance, making the long-run behaviour of individual optimization trajectories difficult to characterize. In this work, we prove that SGD with clipping and additive Gaussian noise (SGD-CN) converges almost surely (a.s.) under smoothness and uniformly bounded stochastic-gradient noise assumptions, provided the step sizes satisfy some standard decaying conditions. Our analysis extends to momentum variants such as the stochastic heavy ball and Nesterov's accelerated gradient, where we show that careful energy constructions yield similar guarantees. These results provide stronger theoretical foundations for understanding the pathwise behaviour of clipped stochastic gradient methods and suggest that, despite the bias and noise introduced by clipping and perturbation, the algorithm remains stable in both convex and nonconvex regimes.

</details>


### [223] [Rank-Efficient LoRA via Joint Tangent-Space Optimization under Isotropic Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12123)
*Zihan Zhu,Zhehang Du,Xuyang Chen,Tim Tsz-Kit Lau,Jiayuan Wu,X. Y. Han,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Iso-LoRA，一种面向LoRA微调的切空间优化器，通过直接优化诱导的权重空间切向更新而非独立优化两个LoRA因子，提升LoRA秩利用率。在风格化分析下证明其一步有效秩上界，并配合因子分解实现，在0.1B–7B语言模型微调中取得优于AdamW和Muon的效果，尤其在中高秩时增益明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践将LoRA的名义秩r视为适配容量的主要旋钮，但忽略了优化器如何利用该容量。作者通过GPT-2+E2E案例发现：同一名义秩下，AdamW诱导的切向更新谱高度集中（有效秩低），而Muon使用更丰富的方向且能更稳定地从增大秩中受益。这表明"秩即容量"的视角不完整，优化器几何与LoRA秩的交互作用决定了诱导更新是否能充分利用切向空间。

Method: Iso-LoRA将LoRA的两个因子视为一个耦合对象，目标是优化诱导的权重空间切向更新X = AδB⊤ + δAB⊤。具体三步：(1) 将权重空间梯度G投影到LoRA切向空间𝒯_{A,B}中，得到H = 𝒫_{A,B}(G)；(2) 对H施加极化谱下降方向Z⋆ = -orth(H)；(3) 将Z⋆再投影回切向空间X = 𝒫_{A,B}(Z⋆)，并通过显式重构公式δB⊤=A†X、δA=(I-AA†)X(B†)⊤得到兼容的因子更新。实现上采用因子分解形式（Algorithm 4），从因子梯度直接恢复秩-2r的切向信号，避免实例化m×d稠密矩阵，计算复杂度为O((m+d)r² + r³)。

Result: 理论结果（Theorem 4.2/4.3/5.4/5.7）：在rank-k脉冲梯度模型下，标准LoRA初始化后，第一步诱导更新的稳定秩满足 Iso-LoRA = r（最大），Muon ≈ srank(A₀)，GD ≤ C·srank(G₀)；GD动态更新受环境梯度稳定秩控制（Theorem 5.4），而Muon在条件数和干扰常数受控时有Order(r)下界（Theorem 5.7）。实验结果：在GPT-2 Small + E2E NLG秩消融中，r=128时Iso-LoRA相比AdamW验证损失降低0.039，稳定秩利用率接近1.00（vs Muon 0.77，AdamW 0.04）；LLaMA-2-7B + MetaMathQA上r=128时GSM8K EM达61.87±0.64%，超过LoRA-Pro的59.20±0.39%；Qwen2.5-0.5B、Llama-3.2-1B/3B各规模上均取得最优或并列最优。在固定时间预算下，Iso-LoRA在E2E全部五项指标及GSM8K EM上均领先。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LoRA优化中一个被忽视的维度：优化器几何决定了名义秩能在多大程度上转化为有效秩更新。Iso-LoRA通过在切向空间中直接进行谱下降，将两个因子更新的耦合效果纳入优化目标，实现了更均衡的谱能量分布。该方法保持了标准LoRA参数化的兼容性，理论保证和实证结果均表明其在中等至高LoRA秩时优势最显著，为LoRA优化器设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is an effective approach for adapting large pretrained models by learning low-rank weight updates. In practice, the LoRA rank is used to control an adapter's parameter budget and representational capacity. We show that this view is incomplete: while the nominal rank determines the representational capacity, the optimizer shapes how much of that capacity is used in the induced weight-space updates. In a case study of GPT-2 adaptation with LoRA, we observe a strong rank-dependent optimizer effect. Despite using the same nominal rank, AdamW often produces per-step updates with concentrated singular spectra and low effective rank, whereas Muon uses a richer set of directions and benefits more consistently from increasing LoRA rank. These observations motivate ISO-LoRA, an optimizer that couples the LoRA factor updates through spectral descent on the induced tangent perturbation in weight space. ISO-LoRA promotes updates that distribute energy more evenly across singular directions, improving rank utilization while preserving compatibility with the LoRA parameterization. We complement this design with theoretical guarantees showing that ISO-LoRA can achieve higher effective rank than standard factor-wise optimizers through a one-step analysis under a stylized spiked-gradient model. We validate this design on language-model adaptation across 0.1B-7B-parameter models, where ISO-LoRA improves effective rank and downstream performance, with the strongest gains at moderate-to-large LoRA ranks. Our results highlight rank utilization as a key factor in LoRA optimization and suggest that optimizer design offers an important path toward stronger parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [224] [GUIDE: Generative Utility Inference and Decision Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12137)
*Anagha Tiwari,Alexander G. Gray,Nick Feamster,Brian Jabarian,Alex Imas,Alex Kale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUIDE是一个LLM辅助的贝叶斯偏好引导（PE）框架，通过结合贝叶斯自适应采样、符号表示学习和经验性领域初始化，解决多维用户偏好高效、精确引导的难题。它支持多种问题类型，提供可观察、可引导的架构，在投资组合优化任务中显著降低了冷启动遗憾并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好引导方法存在两大局限：(1) 贝叶斯方法通常局限于二元强制选择问题，信息增益受限，难以捕获复杂偏好；(2) 独立使用LLM作为PE系统时，缺乏对偏好不确定性的显式表征，且领域知识隐式存在，易产生幻觉和与领域脱节的推荐。因此需要一个支持多样化问题类型、基于经验领域知识、且透明可引导的PE架构。

Method: GUIDE采用粒子滤波效用模型维护偏好后验，通过扩展类型系统支持成对比较、规则引导、维度支配、自由文本和维度提议等多种"变换"，统一在预期信息增益（EIG）下竞争。其核心创新包括：(1) 双向两阶段控制器（发现-校准），先发现偏好维度再校准权重；(2) 基于ILASP的离线领域初始化流程，从人口数据中挖掘规则、构建维度参数化和人口先验；(3) 开发者引导检查点，允许审核、编辑或丢弃挖掘的规则；(4) 通过ESS折扣、成功率、曲线度奖金和力切换等机制增强EIG选择，避免粒子退化和局部最优。

Result: 在投资组合优化领域的50次模拟、5个投资者画像、15轮交互评估中，GUIDE在第1轮的初始遗憾为0.278，远低于OPEN（0.678）和PEBOL（0.651），并在第5轮降至0.038。在所有5个画像上，GUIDE的归一化遗憾减少率（NRR）均优于OPEN和PEBOL，并在4/5个画像上优于最强消融版本，展现了更快的收敛速度和更好的冷启动表现。

Conclusion: GUIDE将通常纠缠在提示词或模型权重中的偏好引导设计选择转化为可显式观察和编辑的组件，为系统化研究LLM辅助PE方法提供了框架。其实验证明了通过多样化查询、经验性领域初始化和结构化可观察性，能有效提升PE效率。局限在于多样化变换在后期可能引入不稳定性，且该工作为模拟评估，未来需通过真实用户研究验证。

Abstract: Measuring the preferences of human users remains a fundamental challenge of AI alignment. Existing elicitation approaches struggle to efficiently discover multidimensional preferences or accurately ground these inferences in domain knowledge. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, an LLM-driven elicitation architecture that infers user preferences through conversations by combining Bayesian adaptive sampling for question selection and symbolic representation learning to initialize domain-specific preference models. GUIDE generalizes adaptive sampling to diverse elicitation questions through an extensible type system of transforms on a parameterized preference state. GUIDE produces domain-specific preference representations through an initialization process using symbolic rule-based learning to capture world knowledge and set priors over preference dimensions grounded in data about decision alternatives. The architecture provides observability and steerability to facilitate deployment and analyze elicitation processes. In silico experiments on investment portfolio optimization demonstrate that GUIDE improves cold-start and minimizes recommendation regret consistently within early elicitation interactions across user personas compared to prior work, LLM-only baselines, and ablated GUIDE versions.

</details>


### [225] [Certifying Concept Unlearning in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12163)
*Mansi,Luca Marzari,Francesco Leofante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对文本到图像(T2I)扩散模型概念遗忘的认证框架，通过结合统计认证与概念相关嵌入方向的worst-case分析，在用户指定的置信水平下推导残余概念泄漏概率的显式上界，揭示了现有基于攻击成功率(ASR)的评估方法严重低估了真实安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型概念遗忘评估主要依赖自动对抗提示搜索的攻击成功率(ASR)，但ASR仅在有限查询集上提供经验证据，无法量化整个提示空间上的残余泄漏，可能高估遗忘效果、低估安全风险。低ASR仅能说明特定搜索方法未能找到攻击，并不能证明攻击不存在。

Method: 提出三阶段认证流水线：(1) 文本嵌入空间中的Steering Unit S，沿概念相关方向搜索对抗嵌入；(2) 嵌入分类器C_clf1，确认搜索是否到达有效的概念承载嵌入；(3) 像素分类器C_clf2，判断生成图像是否仍展示目标概念。基于单边Hoeffding不等式，通过联合界将四个独立经验估计的统计边际与两个分类器的泛化误差合并，得出端到端残余泄漏的高置信上界定理（Theorem 1）。在SD-Turbo和SDXL-Turbo上，针对艺术风格(10种)、NSFW(3类)、名人身份(10位)三类概念，使用CA、ESD、MACE、SSD、UCE、CoGFD六种遗忘方法进行评估。

Result: 在36个(遗忘方法, 类别)案例中，认证上界在32个(88.89%)案例中超过所有基线ASR，平均超出标准ASR 16.2%。最大差距出现在名人身份类别：SSD on SD-Turbo（认证0.652 vs 基线0.074-0.133）和ESD on SDXL-Turbo（认证0.542 vs 0.023-0.108），暴露了现有评估的重大盲区。运行时25分钟，少于P4D(114分钟)和MMA-Diffusion(40分钟)。

Conclusion: 经验性的基于攻击的评估会显著低估残余泄漏，认证框架应作为T2I扩散模型概念遗忘可靠审计的必要补充而非替代。Steering方向由Linear Probe识别（优于SAE），分类器选用Logistic Regression（在精度与泛化间取得平衡）。主要局限：当对抗提示无语义含义时（如P4D生成的乱码），认证边界可能略低于实际攻击成功率，此时需用对抗样本重新训练探针以扩展搜索空间。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of concept unlearning in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models primarily rely on attack success rates obtained through automated adversarial prompt search. However, these metrics provide only empirical evidence over a finite set of queries and leave residual leakage over the broader prompt space largely unquantified. This limitation can lead to overestimating unlearning effectiveness and underestimating safety risks. To address this gap, we introduce a novel certification framework for T2I concept unlearning that provides high-confidence guarantees with bounded error on residual concept leakage. Our approach combines statistical certification with worst-case analysis along concept-relevant embedding directions to derive explicit upper bounds on leakage probability under user-specified confidence levels. We evaluate our framework across three major concept categories namely NSFW content, artistic styles, and celebrity identities, and six state-of-the-art unlearning methods. Certified leakage bounds consistently exceed standard attack success rates by 16.2%, uncovering substantial residual risks missed by existing evaluation protocols. Crucially, our results demonstrate that empirical attack-based evaluations can significantly underestimate residual leakage and establish certification as a necessary complement for reliable auditing of concept unlearning in T2I diffusion models.

</details>


### [226] [Estimating Pedestrian Volumes from GIS-Derived Built-Environment Features: A Machine Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12173)
*Bahareh Golchin,Banafsheh Rekabdar,Sirisha Kothuri,Joseph Broach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对城市交通部门在行人量估计中面临的人工计数成本高、覆盖稀疏的问题，提出了一套基于开源GIS数据的机器学习流程，用于预测波特兰市101个交叉口晚高峰2小时行人量。作者从实践中常用的负二项GLM出发，系统性地引入了特征选择、计数型梯度提升（Poisson损失）、重复交叉验证和综合排名选模等组件，最终选出的最优模型（基于直方图的梯度提升+Poisson损失+L1 Lasso特征选择）相比基线在交叉验证RMSE上降低12%，留出集RMSE降低19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人量数据是城市交通安全投资、土地利用影响评估和碰撞风险建模的关键输入，但人工计数成本高昂、空间覆盖稀疏；现有的负二项GLM虽可解释但受限于强函数形式假设且难以刻画非线性交互，而高维特征（92个）配合小样本（101个）场景对模型选择与验证协议提出了严峻挑战，亟需一套系统、可复现的机器学习流程。

Method: 数据来源于波特兰市交通局PDF人工计数（101个交叉口PM高峰2小时进入量）及OSM、ACS、LEHD、Strava等开源GIS数据，在交叉口100英尺缓冲区和1/8、1/4、1/2英里缓冲区上构建特征。方法上以负二项GLM为基线，比较7种模型（Random Forest、HistGB-Poisson、HistGB-Squared、Extra Trees、Huber梯度提升、Poisson GLM、Bagged HistGB）；采用4种特征选择方法（L1 Lasso、互信息、RF重要性、F回归），在5种特征数（10/15/20/25/30）下通过RandomizedSearchCV进行40组超参数搜索，并采用4种交叉验证策略（5折、10折、5×10重复、3×10重复），以RMSE/MAPE/SMAPE的联合排名筛选最终配置；所有预处理仅在训练折上拟合以防泄露。

Result: 基线GLM留出RMSE为108.0；最优模型HistGB-Poisson+L1 Lasso（20个特征，学习率0.01，500轮，最大深度7，叶子最小样本10，ℓ2=1.0）在3×10重复CV下RMSE=78.7、MAPE=51.8%，相比基线CV RMSE降低12%、留出RMSE降低19%；特征选择前模型严重过拟合（GLM训练R²=1.0但测试预测为真实值的254倍）；20–30个特征为最佳区间；调优带来最大单步改进（RMSE从~79降至73.7）；对数变换目标（双重log-link）和Ridge堆叠均无改善。置换重要性显示Strava年度步行里程、信号化交叉口密度、距CBD距离、零售面积、零汽车家庭、人行道长度等为关键预测因子，与既有行人规划理论一致。

Conclusion: 该研究表明在n=101、p=92的小样本高维行人量预测场景中，特征选择、计数适配的损失函数和稳健的重复交叉验证协议是提升模型性能的关键，简单的模型架构更换即可获得14%的RMSE降幅，特征选择再降26%，而对数变换和模型堆叠等常见技巧在该设置下无效；最终选出的HistGB-Poisson模型兼具预测精度与城市规划理论上的可解释性，为交通部门以边际成本扩展全路网行人量估计提供了可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Transportation agencies need pedestrian volume estimates across entire road networks to prioritize safety investments, yet manual counts are expensive and cover only a small share of intersections. We present a machine learning pipeline that predicts 2-hour PM peak pedestrian volume at 101 urban intersections in Portland, Oregon, from built-environment, land-use, and street-network features drawn from open GIS data. Starting from the Negative Binomial GLM used in practice, we add feature selection, count-aware gradient boosting, and repeated cross-validation, selecting one configuration by a combined rank over RMSE, MAPE, and SMAPE across four cross-validation strategies. The winner, a histogram-based gradient boosting model with Poisson loss and L1 Lasso feature selection, reduces cross-validated RMSE by 12% over the GLM baseline (89.8 to 78.7) and holdout RMSE by 19% (108.0 to 87.9). Code is released on GitHub.

</details>


### [227] [Patient-Reported Survey Data Improve Prediction of Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12224)
*Xiyue Jiang,Zihan Ding,Grace Han,Yinan Liu,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于All of Us研究计划中267,747名有阿片类药物暴露史的参与者（含15,287例OUD患者），系统评估了患者自报问卷数据相对于纯电子健康档案（EHR）数据在预测首次OUD诊断中的增量价值。结果表明，在6、12、24个月回溯窗口下，8种模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM、MLP、LSTM、GRU、Transformer）的EHR+问卷模型PR-AUC均高于EHR-only模型，最佳24个月LightGBM模型PR-AUC从0.6219提升至0.6603。问卷特征在24个月窗口下成为第二重要的信息域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康档案（EHR）可能无法充分捕捉与阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）相关的患者自报因素（如社会决定因素、行为和功能状态）。这些在EHR中缺失或不完整的信息可能限制OUD风险预测模型的性能。本研究旨在量化患者自报问卷数据相对于结构化EHR数据的增量预测价值，并检验该价值是否在不同模型架构和时间窗口中具有一致性。

Method: 1. 数据来源：All of Us研究计划，267,747名有阿片类药物暴露史的参与者，OUD通过ICD-9/10编码定义（病例:对照≈1:16.5）。2. 时间设计：分别设置6、12、24个月回溯窗口，OUD阳性以首次诊断为索引日期，阴性以最近就诊为索引日期。3. 特征工程：EHR特征涵盖人口学、诊断、用药、检验、测量和临床观察六大类（按0.5%频率阈值筛选）；问卷特征来自The Basics、Lifestyle、Overall Health、SDOH四份问卷（按50%应答率筛选，经独热/多热编码）。4. 模型：8种模型——逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM、MLP（表格型）和LSTM、GRU、Transformer（序列型，K=20/30/50）。5. 类别不平衡策略比较后选择不调整。6. 评估指标：PR-AUC（主指标）、ROC-AUC、F1+；特征重要性通过测试集置换重要性分析（5次重复），分别在信息域和问题层级进行。7. 数据划分：68%训练/12%验证/20%测试，按OUD状态分层。

Result: 1. 问卷覆盖率：随窗口延长而上升，但OUD阳性参与者覆盖率显著低于阴性（24个月：21.7% vs. 60.7%）。2. 模型性能：在全部24种模型-窗口组合中，加入问卷特征均使PR-AUC提升，增益范围+0.0087至+0.0505；最佳模型为24个月LightGBM（EHR+S），PR-AUC=0.6603，ROC-AUC=0.9430。3. 最佳架构：梯度提升树（XGBoost、LightGBM）优于深度序列模型（MLP、LSTM、GRU、Transformer）。4. 特征重要性：在LightGBM和GRU中，问卷特征在24个月窗口下成为第二重要的信息域（仅次于检验测量），PR-AUC分别下降0.172和0.234。5. 关键问卷条目：娱乐性药物使用、注意力困难、独立完成日常事务能力、就业状态、酒精/烟草使用、疼痛、自评健康、收入和教育等在两个模型中反复出现在高排名位置。

Conclusion: 患者自报问卷数据在多种模型和时间窗口下均能为OUD预测提供超越EHR的互补预测信号，尤其在24个月长窗口下贡献最大。预测信号分布于行为、功能、社会经济和自评健康多个领域，与OUD风险的多因素特性一致。但问卷数据存在显著的选择性缺失和信息性缺失（OUD阳性参与者覆盖率更低），未来需分离问卷内容效应与可及性效应，进行外部验证、校准、公平性评估和前瞻性验证，才能推进临床转化。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) may incompletely capture patient-reported factors associated with opioid use disorder (OUD). We evaluated whether survey data improve prediction of a first recorded OUD diagnosis among 267,747 All of Us participants with documented opioid exposure, including 15,287 OUD cases. We compared EHR-only and EHR+survey models across 6-, 12-, and 24-month look-back windows using logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, multilayer perceptron, LSTM, GRU, and Transformer. Survey augmentation improved PR-AUC across all 24 model-window combinations by 0.0087-0.0505; the best 24-month LightGBM model improved from 0.6219 to 0.6603. Survey coverage increased with longer windows and differed by OUD status (24 months: 21.7% OUD-positive vs. 60.7% OUD-negative). Permutation analysis ranked survey features as the second most important information domain at 24 months in both evaluated models. Patient-reported data provide complementary predictive signals beyond structured EHRs while highlighting the importance of survey availability.

</details>


### [228] [PLSP (Pre-hoc Liminal Space Profiling): OOD Prediction over Detection -- An Anticipatory Approach for Machine Learning Model Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12225)
*Vipul Bansal,Himanshu Buckchash,Balasubramanian Raman,Deepak Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PLSP框架，将传统的后验OOD检测转变为先验OOD预测，核心是引入与数据集无关的可信度评分（CREDS）指标，在部署前评估模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法均为后验式（post-hoc），依赖推理时的准确率和AUC-ROC等指标间接评估模型对OOD数据的响应，无法在开发阶段提前预判模型在分布偏移下的可靠性，导致部署后可能发生灾难性故障。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) CREDS指标——通过计算训练数据与新数据在各特征、各类别上的KL散度（假设高斯分布），再用模型特征重要性加权聚合，衡量模型对新分布的泛化能力；(2) 可信度曲线（Credibility Curves）——分析均值μv和方差σv变化时可信度的变化，估计模型可达到的最大可信度；(3) 可信度热力图与体积下表面（VUS）——在μv和σv同时变化时评估整体模型脆弱性。使用随机森林作为基础模型，在MNIST/SVHN、CIFAR-10/CINIC-10/STL-10上进行跨域评估，并加入10%–40%高斯噪声测试。

Result: 实验显示：同分布测试CREDS高达0.97–0.99，跨域测试降至0.44–0.94；CREDS与准确率随噪声增加呈单调下降趋势，二者强相关；噪声主要通过方差偏移而非均值偏移削弱模型鲁棒性；MNIST模型比SVHN模型对分布偏移更敏感；CINIC-10（因含ImageNet增强且样本量大）泛化能力略优于CIFAR-10和STL-10。

Conclusion: PLSP成功实现从OOD检测到OOD预测的范式转变，CREDS作为先验指标可在无OOD数据的情况下评估模型鲁棒性，为部署前主动调整模型提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) data poses a significant threat to machine learning models, often leading to model failure during deployment. All existing OOD detection methods are post-hoc, relying on evaluation metrics such as accuracy and AUC-ROC during inference to indirectly assess the model's response to OOD data by measuring deviations. In contrast to existing approaches, the proposed work shifts the paradigm from OOD detection to OOD prediction by proposing a pre-hoc anticipatory framework called PLSP for OOD prediction. We make several key contributions: (a) a dataset-independent metric called the CREDibility Score (CREDS) is proposed for OOD prediction; (b) credibility curves are introduced to study the maximum credibility a model can attain; and (c) credibility heat maps (and volume under surface) are introduced to characterize pre-hoc model behavior across different datasets. This work provides a novel perspective on signal processing under distributional shifts. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed metric serves as a valuable measure for improving the robustness of machine learning models toward OOD prediction.

</details>


### [229] [CRFCAN: A Complex-Valued Cross-Domain Residual Network for Joint Channel and Phase Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12244)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRFCAN，一种复数值交叉域残差网络，用于亚太赫兹OFDM系统中联合信道与相位噪声估计。该网络通过FFT/IFFT残差组实现时频域交替细化，结合物理感知的相位旋转模块和软归一化输出层，在端到端单次推理框架下显著优于传统LMMSE/迭代LS和现有级联/混合深度学习方法，且具备跨未见过PN模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚太赫兹通信中超大带宽与严重相位噪声耦合，导致传统联合信道与PN估计计算复杂度极高且存在明显误差平台。现有深度学习方法多采用级联架构或与迭代估计器混合，未能真正实现端到端联合恢复；同时主流实值卷积网络无法显式建模PN在复平面上的乘性旋转特性，限制了估计精度与跨PN模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CRFCAN（Complex-valued Residual FFT Convolutional Attention Network），核心设计包括：(1) 复值神经网络（CVNN）框架，使用ℂReLU激活和复卷积核保持I/Q耦合；(2) FFT/IFFT驱动的交叉域残差组交替细化时频域特征，IFFT-RG采用五个CRCAB侧重频域线性信道响应，FFT-RG在中心位置插入PRCAB显式建模乘性相位旋转；(3) RCAB-PhaseRotation模块通过Euler公式 e^{jθ̃} 生成相位旋转因子，并以可学习门η控制旋转步长实现残差相位补偿；(5) 双输出头（ℂ̂、p̂），PN分支使用基于sigmoid的软幅度约束稳定训练；(6) 基于不确定性的自适应加权联合损失函数与循环warm-up+余弦退火学习率策略。

Result: 在100 GHz载频、64子载波、3GPP TDL-C信道及三种PN模型（RAN4/RAN1 Set1/Set2）下的仿真表明：(1) CRFCAN在NMSE、PN-MSE、BER和EVM上均优于迭代LS、级联三网络（Cascade 3 nets）和DeepSRX等基线，并有效消除高Eb/N0处的误差平台；(2) 仅在RAN4训练下直接泛化至未见的RAN1 Set1/Set2 PN模型，性能保持同步下降趋势，无灾难性退化；(3) 消融实验验证FFT/IFFT交叉域主干贡献最大，移除后高Eb/N0 BER退化最严重，PRCAB与软归一化PN尾分别提供有意义的归纳偏置和正则化；(4) 网络约4.7×10^6实值参数，单次前向推理计算复杂度固定且确定，与迭代方法相比具备显著延迟优势。

Conclusion: CRFCAN通过复值交叉域学习与物理感知模块的结合，为亚太赫兹OFDM接收机提供了一种有效且实用的联合CSI与PN估计方案。其单次推理、跨PN模型泛化能力以及端到端训练简便性，使其成为宽带亚太赫兹系统在严重振荡器硬件损伤下的有前景的接收解决方案。

Abstract: In sub-terahertz (sub-THz) communications, the coupling of ultra-wide bandwidth and severe phase noise (PN) impairments renders conventional joint channel and PN estimation highly complex and computationally prohibitive. To address this, we propose CRFCAN, a complex-valued residual FFT convolutional attention network designed for joint channel and PN estimation. Unlike existing deep learning schemes that rely on cascaded networks or hybrid frameworks combining neural networks with conventional iterative estimators, CRFCAN performs joint recovery in a truly end-to-end fashion through a physics-inspired cross-domain structure. Specifically, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and inverse FFT modules are embedded within residual groups to enable iterative feature interaction across the time and frequency domains, thereby capturing both frequency-selective fading and time-varying phase distortions. In addition, two dedicated residual blocks are introduced for complex feature extraction and multiplicative phase-distortion modeling, respectively. A physics-aware PN output tail with soft normalization is further employed to improve estimation stability while preserving the physical characteristics of the effective PN process. Simulation results demonstrate that CRFCAN significantly outperforms conventional algorithms and state-of-the-art deep learning models in terms of normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER). Notably, CRFCAN achieves superior performance with single-shot, fixed-complexity inference and generalizes well to unseen PN models without fine-tuning, highlighting its robustness and practicality for sub-THz receivers.

</details>


### [230] [The Rank the Task Demands: A Causal Rank Law for Matrix Memories Trained on Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12259)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"秩定律"（Rank Law）：在矩阵记忆模型中，梯度下降会精确征募任务代数所要求的秩。通过在组合状态追踪任务上的因果干预实验证明，征募的秩等于群的最小忠实实表示维度d_min，且秩本身是因果必要的（少一秩则恢复率为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值记忆的秩是其表征的自然预算，但先前工作要么描述性地测量秩，要么手工构建秩，未回答一个几何表征理论需要回答的核心问题：当任务代数固定了最小表示维度时，梯度下降是否征募恰好该维度，且该维度是否因果承载？

Method: 方法包括：(1) 硬性单状态瓶颈（解码器仅读取一个矩阵Z，经梯度消隐验证）；(2) 固定解码器无法"洗白"秩；(3) 五个有限群（S3, S4, A5, S5, A6）上的词问题作为组合状态追踪任务，目标为正交的最小忠实实表示；(4) 预注册力秩干预（截断至d_min-1, d_min, d_min+1）；(5) 熵有效秩估计器与去规范化的余弦相似度恢复率rec@0.9；(6) 预注册等价性检验（S4/A5维度匹配对）。

Result: 关键结果：(1) 征募秩与d_min的Spearman ρ=0.9747（设计所能达到的最大值），19个种子全部落入预注册的[0.7, 1.3]·d_min带内；(2) 维度匹配的S4/A5在±0.5秩单位容差内统计等价（差异+0.019）；(3) 力秩因果剃刀：d_min-1时所有群所有种子的rec@0.9均为0（几何强制上限≤0.894），d_min时五个群四个种子全部通过预注册阈值；(4) 秩的必要性是几何强制结果，充分性则是经验性因果确认。

Conclusion: 该工作将秩定律从标量容量边界（来自姊妹论文的K对绑定测试平台）推广到表示论层面，建立了"任务需求多少秩，梯度下降就征募多少秩"的因果版本。局限：结果限于亚百万参数的合成测试平台，非预训练语言模型；S3存在软收敛标记，应方向性解读；S5步数钉从8k升至20k（预注册重新测试）。

Abstract: Matrix-valued memories make rank the natural budget of a learned representation: the number of independent directions a state spans bounds what it can bind, compose, and track. We report causal evidence, on a group-composition testbed trained under a hard single-state bottleneck with a fixed decoder that cannot launder rank, that gradient descent recruits precisely the rank the task's algebra demands. A companion paper [Larson, 2026a] establishes the analogous recruitment and causal necessity pattern on a $K$-pair associative-binding testbed, where exact recovery provably requires state rank at least $K$; this paper inherits that instrument and extends the rank law from a scalar capacity bound to a representation-theoretic one. We train toward chosen minimal faithful reference representations embedded in larger matrices. On group-composition state tracking over five finite groups spanning the solvable/non-solvable divide, the recruited rank equals the group's minimal faithful real representation dimension $d_{\min}$ (Spearman $\rho = 0.9747$, the design's tie-capped maximum), the dimension-matched solvable/non-solvable pair $S_4$/$A_5$ is statistically equivalent under a pre-registered test, and a pre-registered force-rank test separates a guaranteed similarity ceiling from empirical recovery at the target dimension: one rank below $d_{\min}$, cosine similarity is capped by the target's tied unit spectrum at $\sqrt{(d_{\min}{-}1)/d_{\min}} \le 0.894$, below the $0.9$ threshold in every group by construction, with observed cells at 86-95% (mean 91%) of that ceiling; at $d_{\min}$, not guaranteed a priori, recovery clears the pre-registered anchor-relative bar at four seeds per group in all five groups. Within this testbed, measured effective rank tracks representation dimension; the matched-dimension $S_4$/$A_5$ comparison establishes equivalence within the pre-registered tolerance.

</details>


### [231] [Adaptive Chemotherapy Control under Tumor Heterogeneity via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12264)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的自适应化疗给药框架，在高维异质性肿瘤模型上比较了连续动作空间（TD3）和离散动作空间（DQN）的闭环策略，并与庞特里亚金最大值原理（PMP）推导的开环最优控制基准进行了对比，通过100名虚拟患者的队列验证了策略在参数异质性下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤异质性和耐药性使得传统的最大耐受剂量（MTD）策略和基于单一标称模型的最优控制难以推广到多样化患者群体；现有DRL研究多局限于单一患者、缺乏与控制论最优解的基准对比，且未解决队列级泛化问题。

Method: 将肿瘤建模为n=21维亚群ODE系统，以最优控制代价泛函直接推导奖励信号；分别采用DQN（离散7级动作）和TD3（连续动作）进行训练，并以PMP打靶法求解的开环策略作为基准；通过±10%参数扰动构建100名虚拟患者队列评估泛化性能，并基于PMP最优性条件重构协态轨迹进行诊断验证。

Result: 在标称模型上，TD3肿瘤缩减率89.1%，略优于PMP的87.7%，DQN为86.93%；在100名患者队列中，TD3平均缩减率（84.07%）显著高于DQN（82.78%，p=0.0078），但DQN给药一致性更优（95%CI±0.23 vs ±0.40），呈现明确的"效能-一致性"权衡。

Conclusion: 仿真训练的DRL可学习状态依赖的反馈给药策略，作为开环最优控制基准的补充；TD3适合追求平均疗效，DQN适合追求给药可预测性；未来工作需引入POMDP公式和状态估计以应对临床稀疏噪声观测。

Abstract: Designing effective chemotherapy regimens is hindered by tumor heterogeneity and drug resistance, which complicate the deployment of patient-specific model-based optimal control across diverse populations. We develop and compare closed-loop deep reinforcement learning (DRL) dosing policies with continuous (TD3) and discrete (DQN) action spaces trained on a high-dimensional heterogeneous tumor model. The DRL policies are benchmarked against a Pontryagin's Maximum Principle (PMP)-derived open-loop benchmark. We assess generalization under parametric heterogeneity using a 100-patient virtual cohort with plus or minus 10 percent uniform perturbations in growth and drug-sensitivity parameters. Across this cohort, TD3 achieves higher average tumor reduction, while DQN yields tighter inter-patient dosing consistency, revealing a clear efficacy-consistency trade-off in this study. Our simulations assume full observation of all tumor subpopulations; translation to sparse and noisy clinical measurements will require partial-observability formulations and/or state estimation. Overall, the results show that simulation-trained DRL can learn state-dependent feedback dosing policies that complement open-loop optimal control benchmarks.

</details>


### [232] [FRIST: FMRI Representation Informed Shared-space Training Improves EEG-only Individual-Finger BCI Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12298)
*Jintao Zhang,Yidan Ding,Joshua Kosnoff,Maxim Karrenbach,Hanwen Wang,Bin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FRIST框架，利用fMRI的高空间分辨率来指导EEG解码，实现非侵入式脑机接口的精细手指动作解码。FRIST通过两阶段训练，从同步EEG-fMRI数据中学习fMRI信息引导的频谱投影，并利用fMRI推导的类别几何来指导EEG预测的残差细化。在12名健康受试者的实验中，FRIST显著提升了个体手指级别的EEG解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体手指级别的运动解码对于自然化脑机接口（BCI）控制至关重要，但从头皮脑电图（EEG）中解码单个手指动作仍面临挑战，因为手指表征在感觉运动皮层空间上接近且受到容积传导的模糊化影响。本文希望利用fMRI的空间特异性来改善EEG解码性能。

Method: 本文提出了fMRI表征引导的共享空间训练（FRIST）框架，这是一种两阶段EEG解码方法：1）第一阶段从同步EEG-fMRI记录中学习fMRI信息引导的频谱投影；2）第二阶段利用fMRI推导的类别几何来指导EEG预测的残差细化。FRIST通过共享的手指标签在记录间传递信息，无需配对试验，在推理时仅使用EEG。该方法支持跨受试者迁移（即使目标受试者缺乏自身的fMRI数据也能工作），并可与多种EEG解码骨干网络（如EEGNet、EEG Conformer）结合使用。

Result: 在12名健康受试者的运动执行（ME）和运动想象（MI）任务中，使用时间顺序会话留出的解码方案模拟在线场景：使用EEGNet作为特征提取器时，两类ME从66.93%提升至74.53%，三类ME从44.83%提升至56.58%，两类MI从80.78%提升至85.63%，三类MI从60.93%提升至69.90%。使用EEG Conformer时，两类MI达到87.40%，三类MI达到72.54%。当目标受试者自身的fMRI数据不可用时，FRIST仍能改善EEG解码。

Conclusion: FRIST验证了fMRI提供的空间约束对EEG表征学习具有重要价值。该框架通过多模态策略整合了fMRI的空间特异性和EEG的实时适用性，显著提升了非侵入式EEG手指级别BCI解码性能，并具有良好的泛化能力，可适用于不同的EEG解码架构。

Abstract: Finger-level motor decoding is important for naturalistic brain-computer interface (BCI) control, yet individual-finger decoding from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains challenging because finger representations are spatially close in the sensorimotor cortex and blurred by volume conduction. Leveraging the high spatial resolution of functional MRI (fMRI), we introduce fMRI Representation-Informed Shared-Space Training (FRIST), a two-stage EEG decoding framework that first learns fMRI-informed spectral projections from simultaneous EEG-fMRI recordings and then uses fMRI-derived class geometry to guide residual refinement of EEG predictions. FRIST transfers information across recordings through shared finger labels without requiring paired trials and uses only EEG at inference. We evaluated 12 able-bodied participants during movement execution (ME) and motor imagery (MI) under two-class and three-class chronological session-held-out decoding simulating the online scenario. Using EEGNet as the EEG feature extractor, FRIST increased group average accuracy from 66.93% to 74.53% for two-class ME, from 44.83% to 56.58% for three-class ME, from 80.78% to 85.63% for two-class MI, and from 60.93% to 69.90% for three-class MI compared with the EEG-only EEGNet baseline. FRIST is also shown to improve EEG-only decoding when the target participant's own fMRI data were unavailable. FRIST also generalized across multiple EEG decoding backbones, reaching 87.40% in two-class MI and 72.54% in three-class MI with EEG Conformer as the EEG feature extractor. These findings indicate that fMRI provide useful spatial constraints for EEG representation learning. FRIST improves noninvasive EEG-based finger-level BCI decoding, offering a multimodal strategy for integrating the spatial specificity of fMRI with real-time applicability of EEG.

</details>


### [233] [Sampling via Decision-Flow: Training-Free Extraction of Improved Latent Reasoning Paths in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12317)
*Zhendong Mi,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、无需数据的推理时采样框架 DF-Sample（Decision-Flow Sampling），通过构建分层推理树、评估叶节点效用并反向传播全局质量信号，从基础模型中挖掘低概率但高质量的潜在推理路径，在多个基准上超越 GRPO 等 RL 微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）微调是否真正赋予 LLM 新能力，还是仅重新分配概率质量（即"分布锐化假说"）？基于此假说，高质量推理路径已潜在存在于基础模型中，问题在于如何通过采样而非训练来找到这些路径。现有 MCMC 风格方法依赖每步局部接受-拒绝，无法保证全局轨迹最优，因为局部合理的推理步骤仍可能导向错误结论。

Method: DF-Sample 包含四个阶段：(1) 分层推理树构建——按分支因子 K 逐层采样候选推理步骤；(2) 叶节点能量评估——结合归一化对数似然（温度系数 α）和质量评分 R（由 GPT-4o 评判）计算终端能量 E，进而导出效用 U=exp(-E)；(3) 决策流反向传播——自底向上以先验加权方式将效用递归聚合到中间节点；(4) 后验路径选择——按 π* ∝ p_prior · U 的后验概率递归采样完整推理路径。当总深度 N 超过块大小 B 时采用块式采样以控制计算开销。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B、Qwen2.5-7B、Phi-3.5-mini-instruct 三种模型与 MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA-Diamond、AlpacaEval 2.0 四个基准上，DF-Sample 在多数设置下超越 Base、Low-temperature、GRPO 与 Power Sampling 基线。其中 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 在 GPQA-Diamond 上达 45.6%（GRPO 39.9%、Power Sampling 38.9%），MATH500 上达 81.8%（GRPO 78.5%）。分析表明 DF-Sample 的 token 似然和置信度分布更广，能进入基础模型低置信度但全局正确的区域；K=4 时比 K=2 准确率提升约 18%，α≈4–5 时效果最佳。代价为推理时间略长（384s vs 340s/题）。

Conclusion: DF-Sample 证明无需参数更新的训练免费采样方法即可从基础模型中挖掘出高质量推理路径，性能可媲美甚至超越 RL 微调方法，支持"分布锐化假说"，即 RL 并非注入新能力，而是重塑已有知识的表达。局限在于纯推理时方法未将发现的推理模式蒸馏回模型参数，未来可探索与学习式方法的结合。

Abstract: A central question in LLM reasoning is whether reinforcement learning (RL) instills genuinely new capabilities or merely reshapes how existing knowledge is expressed during inference. Building on the distribution-sharpening hypothesis, which holds that RL reallocates probability mass toward high-reward trajectories already latent in base models, we ask: can we unlock those latent paths without costly RL fine-tuning? We present Decision-Flow Sampling (DF-Sample), a training-free, data-free inference-time framework that constructs a hierarchical reasoning tree, scores terminal nodes for quality, and back-propagates utilities to inform each intermediate branching decision. Unlike conventional sampling strategies that make purely local step-wise choices, DF-Sample performs explicit global trajectory evaluation before committing to a path, recovering high-quality but low-probability reasoning chains that standard decoding overlooks. On GPQA, DF-Sample achieves 45.6% accuracy, surpassing power sampling (38.9%) and GRPO (39.9%), showing that a training-free method can outperform a trained one. Across three models and four benchmarks, DF-Sample consistently outperforms baselines, indicating substantial latent reasoning potential in pretrained base models.

</details>


### [234] [Simulating Disengaged Students to Evaluate LLM-based Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12331)
*Xianghui Meng,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Disengagement-Aware Student Simulators (DAS2)，一种模拟五种学习者参与状态（专注、刷系统、原地打转、分心、混合）的可复现预部署评估协议，用于在部署前评估AI导师在不同学习者状态下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学生模拟器大多只模拟积极参与的学习者状态，忽略了刷系统（gaming）、原地打转（wheel-spinning）、分心（off-task）等脱离行为状态。然而真实学习场景中这些状态普遍存在，且不同状态需要导师采取不同干预策略。缺乏对AI导师在异质学习者状态下的鲁棒性评估方法。

Method: （1）基于ASSISTments09数据集构建规则标注体系识别五种参与状态；（2）两名标注员对100个采样辅导会话进行独立标注，84%一致率（Cohen's kappa=0.78）；（3）对比微调Qwen2.5-7B与提示驱动的GPT-4o模拟器；（4）评估五个AI导师（Claude、Llama、Gemini、Qwen、GPT）的跨状态表现；（5）进行人类评判验证。

Result: （1）人类共识标签与DAS2规则标签一致率达81%（kappa=0.75）；（2）模拟器与真实会话正确率差距从0.54降至0.20（刷系统）、从0.51降至0.18（原地打转）；（3）微调Qwen2.5-7B更好捕捉真实响应时间分布，GPT-4o生成的学习者状态更具可区分性；（4）AI导师的相对排名在不同状态和交互长度下保持稳定，但绝对性能存在状态特异性差异；（5）自动评估与人类判断不完全一致。

Conclusion: DAS2为AI导师在部署前评估其对多样化学习者参与状态的响应提供了实用框架。该研究表明导师的相对性能稳健但绝对表现因状态而异，同时揭示了自动化评估与人类判断之间的差距，呼吁开展结合参与状态感知的更全面的AI导师评估方法。

Abstract: Simulated students generated by computational models provide a practical way to evaluate tutoring strategies and pedagogical approaches used by human and AI tutors. However, such simulations should account for disengaged behaviors, including gaming the system, wheel-spinning, and off-task behavior, because tutors may need different responses for different learner states. We present Disengagement-Aware Student Simulators (DAS2), a reproducible pre-deployment protocol that models five learner-engagement states: engaged, gaming, wheel-spinning, off-task, and mixed, and evaluates AI tutor performance across these states. Using ASSISTments09, two coders independently labeled 100 sampled tutoring sessions based on anonymized interaction-log summaries. They achieved 84% agreement (Cohen's kappa = 0.78), and among agreed cases, human consensus labels matched DAS2 rule-based labels in 81% of cases (kappa = 0.75). Conditioning simulations on intended learner states reduced the correctness-rate gap between simulated and authentic sessions from 0.54 to 0.20 for gaming and from 0.51 to 0.18 for wheel-spinning. Fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B better matched authentic response-time distributions, while prompt-only GPT-4o generated more distinguishable learner states. Evaluation of five AI tutors from the Claude, Llama, Gemini, Qwen, and GPT families showed that relative rankings remained stable across learner states and interaction lengths, while absolute performance varied, revealing state-specific differences in tutor support. Human validation further showed that automated tutor evaluation does not fully align with human judgment. DAS2 provides a pre-deployment framework for evaluating how AI tutors respond to diverse learner-engagement states before deployment.

</details>


### [235] [Theoretical Guarantees for One-Shot Magnitude Pruning and Compute-Adaptive Early Exit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12337)
*Erdem Koyuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"部分计算 vs 全计算"的统一视角,研究神经网络中的计算缩减问题,涵盖静态的一次性幅值剪枝和自适应提前退出两个场景。文章在单神经元层面证明了剪枝相似度的集中定理,并提出了"条件感知机"模型分析提前退出;在深度网络层面,通过高斯过程极限刻画了剪枝失真随深度累积的规律,并在冻结骨干网络设定下推导了计算-精度的幂律折中关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件计算(如提前退出、MoE)虽能大幅节省推理计算,但缺乏理论解释:何时以及为何部分计算能维持精度。本文旨在从第一性原理出发,为静态剪枝和自适应提前退出提供可处理的计算-精度折中理论,覆盖从单神经元到深度网络的全谱。

Method: 方法分为四个层面:(1)静态单神经元:利用Gamma随机变量序统计量的条件独立性,结合Berry-Esseen定理和Hoeffding不等式,证明剪枝余弦相似度以O(n^{-1/2})速率集中到确定性极限;(2)自适应单神经元:引入条件感知机,在学生-教师设定下通过球面几何和角距离三角不等式分离师生重叠与剪枝-学生重叠,推导超额泛化误差的幂律上界;(3)静态深度网络:采用无限宽NNGP极限,耦合未剪枝与重归一化剪枝前向传递,通过多元Lindeberg CLT和确定性阈值代理建立交叉核递归;(4)自适应深度网络:在冻结骨干设定下,利用类内高斯耦合假设和次高斯残差控制推导幂律折中,并通过正交分解精确计算NNGP中的蒸馏相关性。

Result: 主要结果包括:定理2.1给出剪枝相似度的显式浓度界(含O(n^{-1/2})和exp(-Cnε²)项);定理3.1表明条件感知机超额误差按计算缺口的A次幂衰减,A=0.5/(1-ρ²),ρ→1时A→∞;定理4.1建立深度网络交叉核递归,MSE=2(1-α_L),α_ℓ遵循符号激活的arcsin递归或ReLU的κ_R递归;定理5.2将幂律折中推广到深度网络,A'=0.5/(1-ρ_ℓ²);推论5.4证明NNGP极限下ρ_ℓ→χ_φ^{L-ℓ}。数值实验在DistilBERT(SST-2)和ViT(CIFAR-100)上验证了提前退出的幂律标度行为及深度依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文为神经网络计算缩减提供了统一的理论框架,从单神经元到深度网络、从静态剪枝到自适应提前退出全覆盖。核心洞察是:部分计算与全计算的对齐性是决定计算-精度折中指数的唯一参数;剪枝失真在深度上以激活函数相关的方式累积;幂律标度和指数趋于无穷的阈值现象共同解释了大量预训练模型在不同计算预算下能保持精度的经验观察。未来工作包括将分析扩展到训练化骨干和多个退出点的场景,并在同一框架下比较不同的一次性剪枝规则。

Abstract: We study compute reduction in neural networks through a unified partial versus full computation view, captured by one-shot magnitude pruning in the static regime and early exit in the adaptive regime. In an asymptotic single-neuron model, we prove a concentration theorem for one-shot magnitude pruning with explicit rates. We also introduce the conditional perceptron for early exit and show that its excess generalization error decays as a power of the compute gap, with an exponent that grows to infinity as the alignment between partial and full computations tends to one. We then extend the analysis to deep networks, characterizing how pruning-induced distortions accumulate with depth and deriving a corresponding compute-accuracy tradeoff for frozen-backbone early exit under a neural network Gaussian process model. Numerical simulations corroborate the predicted scaling laws.

</details>


### [236] [When Connected Does Not Mean Similar: Charting the Homophily Boundary of SNAP-KG for Streaming Entity Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12356)
*Jui-Chien Lin,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAP-KG 是一种面向流式知识图谱实体集成的归纳式聚类框架,其性能依赖于至少存在一个同质性(homophilous)关系视图。该论文通过在三个异质性(heterophilous)图(Texas、Wisconsin、Chameleon)上的实验,系统地揭示了该方法及其同类方法的同质性边界:当没有任何同质性关系时,聚类质量急剧下降,且这种下降源于关系本身的异质性,而非关系数量。论文进一步提出了通过蒸馏异质性感知的教师模型来扩展 SNAP-KG 的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNAP-KG 在其原始评估中使用的五个多视图基准都满足同质性假设(相连节点倾向于同类),但其在缺乏同质性关系的真实知识图谱中的适用性尚未得到验证。本文旨在探究这一关键的结构性假设失效时,SNAP-KG 及其同类方法的性能边界。

Method: 1) 在三个标准异质性图(Texas、Wisconsin、Chameleon)上运行 SNAP-KG(五个随机种子);2) 测量全部八个数据集各视图的边同质性(edge homophily);3) 利用原始 SNAP-KG 论文中的单视图消融实验固定关系数量,仅改变关系本身以分离异质性与视图数量的影响;4) 在异质性图上对比 SNAP-KG 与四个归纳式基线(AGE、O2MAC、BMGC、DuaLGR);5) 回顾异质性多视图聚类相关工作(NGCE、HeNCler、SMHGC),提出用异质性感知教师蒸馏至投影器的未来路线。

Result: 1) 异质性图上 SNAP-KG 的 ACC 急剧下降至 31–37%,NMI 跌至个位数;2) 同质性假设成立的数据集 h_max ≥ 0.64 时 ACC 多在 90% 以上;3) 单视图消融证明性能取决于关系同质性而非数量(DBLP APVPA h=0.67 → 90.07% ACC vs APTPA h=0.32 → 56.42% ACC);4) 所有归纳式基线同样表现崩溃,说明限制为整个方法家族共有;5) DuaLGR 在异质性图上相对最佳(Wisconsin 58.57%),但仍远低于其在同质性基准上的表现。

Conclusion: SNAP-KG 的有效性以"至少存在一个同质性关系视图"为前提;当该条件不满足时,聚类结果不可信。这一限制并非 SNAP-KG 所独有,而是整个同质性假设方法家族的共性问题。异质性多视图聚类是一个独立的研究问题,当前所有异质性感知的图聚类方法仍局限于归纳式设置。未来工作可通过将异质性感知的多视图教师蒸馏至 SNAP-KG 的投影器 f_ϕ, 统一归纳式部署与异质性表征,实现对同质性与异质性知识图谱的统一支持。此外,实践者可在部署前通过小样本标签或特征相似度估计各关系的边同质性来预判 SNAP-KG 的适用性。

Abstract: SNAP-KG is a framework for assigning newly arriving entities to semantic communities in a growing knowledge graph (KG) using only their raw features, with no graph access and no retraining at inference time. It was evaluated on five multi-view benchmarks and a 2.4M-node OGB-WikiKG2 KG. In each of these datasets, at least one graph view is homophilous, meaning that connected nodes usually belong to the same class, and SNAP-KG performs well on all of them. This paper asks what happens outside that setting. We extend the evaluation to three heterophilous graphs (Texas, Wisconsin, Chameleon) and measure the edge homophily of every view. When no homophilous view is available, clustering quality drops sharply for both SNAP-KG and the transductive baselines used in its original evaluation. What decides this is the homophily of the relation, not the number of relations. Multi-view fusion still helps, but only when at least one homophilous relation provides a reliable foundation. The homophily assumption is therefore shared by the whole method family, not specific to SNAP-KG. We argue that heterophilous multi-view clustering is a separate research problem, outside the scope of this work. As future work, we outline how a heterophily-aware teacher could be distilled into SNAP-KG's projector to serve both homophilous and heterophilous KGs.

</details>


### [237] [LatentVerse: A Framework for Understanding Shared and Modality-Specific Information in Multimodal Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12364)
*Majd Alafrange,Samuel Friedman,John Kitonyo,Sana Tonekaboni,Mahnaz Maddah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentVerse 是一个模型无关的潜在表示分析框架，通过 Web 可视化分析平台与命令行界面相结合，统一了潜在表示质量的多维诊断指标，并扩展到多模态场景，将融合嵌入分解为共享与模态特定组件进行独立评估，主要面向生物医学基础模型嵌入的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在生物医学领域广泛用于编码多模态数据（临床文本、医学图像、组学、生理信号等），但现有嵌入分析工作流存在三大局限：依赖碎片化的自定义脚本、缺乏统一的诊断指标、以及最重要的——缺乏对多模态嵌入中信息结构的系统性分析工具。这使得从业者难以在昂贵微调前比较表征、评估标签受限或域偏移下的可靠性，以及理解多模态融合中信息的分布与纠缠。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 五个互补的表示质量维度——聚类性（Clusterability）、解纠缠性（Disentanglement）、表达性（Expressiveness）、下游可预测性（Downstream Predictability）、鲁棒性（Robustness），每个维度集成多个成熟指标（如 Silhouette、NMI、DCI、MIG、SAP、TC 等）；(2) 三步统一评估工作流（选择测试→上传数据→生成报告），通过 Web 应用与 CLI 共享同一管线，并具有 10⁻⁹ 相对容差的数值一致性；(3) 多模态分析模块，集成 MultiLoReFT 框架将融合嵌入分解为共享与模态特定组件，对各组件独立施加同样的诊断指标族。报告整合指标表、交互可视化与基于 Gemini 2.5 Flash 的模板受限自然语言摘要。系统采用三层 Web 架构（React + FastAPI + 独立 ML 库），部署于 Google Cloud Run，支持异步作业、TUS 可恢复上传与硬件自适应计算。

Result: 通过三重验证确认框架有效性：(1) 控制变量模拟——单模态仿真（5000 样本、64 维、四组各三难度等级）证明各诊断指标能准确恢复已知的聚类性、可预测性、解纠缠性与表达性结构；多模态仿真（6 个隐藏变量、2 共享+2 各模态私有，含共享/私有/联合四类标签）证明分解后共享标签集中于共享空间、私有标签集中于对应私有组件；(2) 真实生物医学发现——基于 UK Biobank 的 ECG 与 cMRI 预训练嵌入分析显示：PQ 间隔预测在 ECG 空间中更直接可及；肺动脉高压预测中 AUROC 比准确率更具信息量；生物性别信息最强分离出现在共享潜在空间，符合预期；(3) 用户研究——3 名来自基因组学/蛋白质组学/临床 ML 领域的研究人员提供形成性反馈，肯定了指标即时可用性、引导示例与作业进度报告，同时指出结果需可追溯到具体样本、需区分无监督聚类与标签类别、需脚本化版本控制复现路径，这些反馈均已被纳入后续开发。

Conclusion: LatentVerse 通过统一报告驱动的工作流整合了多样化的潜在空间诊断指标，支持系统化的单模态与多模态表征分析，弥合了表示学习研究与实际应用之间的鸿沟。其最突出贡献是支持多模态表示分析——通过将融合嵌入分解为共享与模态特定组件，使用户能够分析预测性或生物学相关信号驻留在何处，而非将多模态表示视为单一不透明向量。框架仍为研究原型，未来需扩展更大规模基础设施支持、并发用户管理、计算密集型分析与跨更广泛科学和应用领域的用户评估。

Abstract: Latent embeddings have become a central data abstraction in modern machine learning, especially in biomedicine, where foundation models are increasingly used to encode multimodal data like clinical text, medical images, omics, and physiological signals. However, the utility and value of these representations depends on understanding their quality, structure, and the information they encode. Existing analysis workflows for evaluating representations remain fragmented across custom scripts, isolated metrics, and most importantly lack multimodal analysis, limiting accessibility and reproducibility. We present LatentVerse, a representation analysis resource that combines a web-based visual analytics platform for accessible, report-driven exploration with a command-line interface for scalable technical workflows. LatentVerse unifies diagnostics for various representation quality metrics and extends to multimodal settings by decomposing embeddings into shared and modality-specific components. We evaluate LatentVerse through controlled unimodal and multimodal simulations, discovery-oriented analyses on real biomedical embeddings, and a user study across diverse use cases. By supporting thorough and interpretable evaluation of latent spaces, LatentVerse makes foundation model representations more understandable in biomedical and data science applications.

</details>


### [238] [Certified AI Triage of ICU Alarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12365)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed,Rozhin Yasaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将ICU心电监护误报抑制重构为"保留/抑制/延迟"三分诊策略,使用Learn-then-Test框架在VTaC基准上对"抑制真报警"这一有害事件提供有限样本统计保证。核心发现:网格多重性成本使更细的策略网格反而认证更少,通过在选择集上自适应选网格可将认证折叠运行数从1/15提升至8/15;此外,学到的可靠性信号并未改善认证前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU监护仪产生大量心律失常警报(71%为假阳),但抑制真实室速(VT)警报会延误对危险心律失常的识别。VTaC基准上71%的VT警报为假,但现有系统未报告对有害抑制率的有限样本保证。本文将告警减少重构为三分诊决策(retain/suppress/defer),并寻求在用户设定的预算内对"被抑制中真报警占比"提供统计保证。

Method: 1. **数据与协议**:使用VTaC v1.0数据集(5,037个VT警报事件,1,441真/3,596假,2,260波形记录,250Hz,ECG+脉搏波),分两个实验臂——开发臂(5折记录不相交,15次折叠运行)和官方分割臂(遵守原发布协议,482测试事件)。2. **模型Sentry**:可用性感知的多模态编码器,共享trunk+槽位token,4层自注意力融合,可用性掩码m,带模态dropout;附加可靠性头r,基于z、掩码、槽位质量得分和跨通道兼容性置信度预测证据可靠性;输出真报警概率p和可靠性r。3. **分诊策略族**:策略三元组(τ_sup, τ_rel, τ_ret),若p≤τ_sup且r≥τ_rel则抑制,若p≥τ_ret则保留,其余延迟;有害事件=真报警被抑制;不安全抑制风险=Pr(y=1|suppress)。两个策略族:RC(885候选,59概率×15可靠性阈值)和P-only(59候选,τ_rel固定为0)。4. **认证**:采用Learn-then-Test(LTT),对网格Λ中每个候选用Clopper-Pearson精确上界CP⁺(k_λ,n_λ;δ/|Λ|)检验≤α(用户预算,α=0.05或0.10,δ=0.05);通过Bonferroni控制在i.i.d.采样下总风险≤α的可信度≥1-δ。额外用记录聚类bootstrap作为敏感性分析(因同一波形记录内事件非独立)。5. **自适应选网格**:Adaptive规则——在选择分区上对每个网格按最佳可候选的假报警抑制率评分,选最高且候选数最少者。

Result: **基础预测**:官方分割AUROC 0.953[0.922,0.970],AUPRC 0.875,Challenge Score 83.33[78.5,86.8],F1 0.799;与FCN等十一个已发布系统在记录聚类区间内相当。**认证分诊**(α=0.05,P-only,官方分割):3/3种子认证,假报警抑制74.8%±1.3%,风险0.008,真报警保留98.5%。**关键发现——多重性代价**:固定885格在held-out校准下仅认证1/15折叠运行,而自适应选网格可达8/15,代价仅1.4%真报警被抑制。无网格能在α≤0.02时认证。校准需求:α=0.05时885格需约4,000事件,59格需约2,000;α=0.10时约800事件(开发臂1,555可用)。**可靠性门控无效**:P-only在所有已认证预算上匹配或超过RC;在三种降级(pulsatile丢失、ECG突发伪影、基线漂移)队列中17/18 cohort×budget×mode单元上匹配或超过RC;r响应r降级0.317而p只+0.003,确实区分可用性,但未改善前沿。**子群问题**:缺可用脉搏通道的子群(6%评估)实际风险为完整模态的2.6倍(α=0.05,16/213 vs 180/6,133);边缘保证不蕴含子群保证,但VTaC无患者标识,无法按患者分层。**对比程序**:naive阈值在α=0.05时15/15运行中有10/15超出预算;无多重性校正CP在3/14中超出;split conformal控制不同泛函;Bonferroni LTT是唯一每个已接纳运行风险都在宣称预算内的程序。

Conclusion: 风险控制的三分诊将分类器分数转为带有限样本保证的策略,并对保证标价:每预算需多少标注警报,每网格多少候选。后者出乎意料——校正按策略数收取,过细的网格可能太贵而无法认证任何策略;在选择集上选网格将认证折叠从1/15提升至8/15,故应将|Λ|视为预算量。在VTaC上5%预算在多数开发折叠和全部官方种子上成立,沉默1.5%真报警;可靠性门控未锐化前沿。本研究为回顾性研究,defer为待审类别而非警报,未经床旁使用验证。限制:仅官方分割臂与文献可比且依赖3种子;记录不相交非患者不相交;边界是边缘且假设i.i.d.事件;一个子群超出;聚类bootstrap是近似的;无外部队列评分;保留阈值无保证;网格发现是探索性的。

Abstract: In the VTaC benchmark 71% of ventricular-tachycardia alarms are false, but silencing a real one can delay recognition of a dangerous arrhythmia. We reframe alarm reduction as three-way triage (retain, suppress, or defer) and bound the decision this analysis treats as harmful: among suppressed alarms, the fraction that were genuine stays below a user-set budget with 95% confidence, under i.i.d. event sampling. Alarms sharing a waveform record are dependent, so the clustered analysis is a sensitivity check. On the official split a 5% budget certifies in all three seeds, suppressing 74.8% of false alarms while silencing 1.5% of genuine ones, at AUROC 0.953 and Challenge Score 83.33, numerically comparable to the strongest of the eleven published systems. Our central finding measures what multiplicity costs: the correction charges for every candidate, so a finer grid can certify strictly less. Under held-out calibration the 885-cell grid we declared certifies 1 of 15 fold-runs, while choosing the grid on a separate selection partition certifies 8. We project the calibration volume each budget needs, making an uncertifiable budget a design parameter. Finally, adding a learned reliability dimension to the policy grid did not sharpen the certified frontier.

</details>


### [239] [Split Conformal Prediction with Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12386)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Juyeon Kim,Erkhembayar Jadamba,Seungjin Choi,Hyunjin Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LSA score(标签偏移调整贝叶斯得分),一种针对标签偏移下分拆共形预测的非一致性得分,通过后验预测倾斜恒等式推导,直接在贝叶斯得分中加入标签偏移修正,结合贝叶斯岭回归实现闭式自适应预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签偏移破坏了共形预测的可交换性假设,现有方法存在两个不足:(1)基于残差的非一致性得分产生等宽区间,忽略预测不确定性;(2)贝叶斯共形方法在源预测分布下评估一致性,在标签偏移下与目标域不对齐,导致加权分位数膨胀、区间过宽。本文旨在同时解决对齐性与自适应性的不足。

Method: 核心方法基于后验预测倾斜恒等式(Proposition 2.1):在标签偏移下,目标后验预测是源后验预测按密度比 r(y)=p_t(y)/p_s(y) 的加权变换。通过指数倾斜模型参数化密度比(log r(y) 线性),用逻辑回归(基于源标签与目标伪标签)估计密度比参数。在贝叶斯岭回归(BRR)下,源预测为高斯,密度比 log 线性,故归一化常数 Z(h) 与倾斜预测分布均有闭式解——均值平移 β̂₁σ²(h),方差不变。LSA score = 源 score − log r̂(y) + log Ẑ(h),等价于目标对齐预测的负对数密度。结合 Tibshirani 等的加权分位数(伪标签估计权重),预测区间为 [μ*±σ√(2 max(q̂−½logσ²,0))],中心为 μ*(h)=μ(h)+β̂₁σ²(h)。

Result: 在 AqSolDB(9982 化合物水溶性数据,BART 编码 768 维冻结特征)1000 次随机试验,β∈{0,-0.1,...,-0.5} 控制标签偏移强度。β=0 时三方法均达 90% 标称覆盖且区间长度几乎相同;β=-0.5 时 LSA 平均区间长度 4.977,相对 Source score(5.744)和 Residual(5.820)分别缩短 13% 和 14%,配对 t 检验 p<0.001;覆盖均略优于基线(0.8842 vs 0.8795/0.8797)。得分分布分析表明,LSA 的加权 CDF 左移、加权分位数下降,从而缩短区间,且优势随 |β| 增大而扩大。Lipophilicity 数据集上趋势一致(β=-0.5 时缩短约 4.6–5.3%)。高斯均值密度比估计器下结果一致,验证方法对估计器不敏感。强偏移下所有方法覆盖率均下降,源于伪标签密度比估计误差,而非得分本身问题。

Conclusion: LSA score 通过后验预测倾斜恒等式提供对源贝叶斯得分的直接修正,在贝叶斯岭回归下实现闭式、样本自适应、无额外计算开销的预测区间。在保持目标域覆盖相当的同时,显著缩短区间长度。该框架适用于任何具有可处理 log 密度的源预测分布,未来可扩展至深度集成、MC-Dropout 等更丰富模型。局限:不声明偏移下的精确有限样本有效性(因依赖伪标签密度比估计);目前仅在 BRR(高斯预测)下实例化。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification under exchangeability. However, this assumption is violated by label shift, where the marginal distribution of labels changes while the conditional distribution of inputs given labels remains stable. Under such shifts, standard conformal procedures no longer maintain their intended coverage behavior. Existing approaches address this via importance weighting. They pair the reweighting with residual-based nonconformity scores that ignore predictive uncertainty. The resulting intervals have uniform width. Bayesian conformal methods produce adaptive intervals by leveraging predictive distributions. They evaluate conformity under the source predictive, which is misaligned with the target domain under label shift. We propose the \emph{Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Score} (LSA score), a nonconformity score derived from a posterior predictive tilting identity. This identity shows that the target predictive is an importance-weighted transformation of the source predictive. We use it to derive a direct correction to the Bayesian score. We evaluate the method on molecular property prediction under controlled label shift. The LSA score consistently yields shorter intervals than residual-based and source-based Bayesian scores. Coverage in the target domain remains comparable. Under stronger shift, all methods incur some coverage loss due to pseudo-label-based density-ratio estimation. The LSA score is defined for any source predictive with a tractable log-density. We instantiate it with Bayesian Ridge Regression, where the correction admits a closed form.

</details>


### [240] [RiPPLE: Cross-Space Performance Prediction from Early Training for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12418)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiPPLE提出了一种跨搜索空间的神经架构性能预测方法，通过将早期训练的局部信号作为覆盖锚点(anchors)的标签而非每个候选架构的特征,在极低训练预算下实现全空间排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS性能预测方法存在两难:零成本代理(ZCP)虽覆盖全空间但跨空间泛化能力差且排序质量有限;全监督预测器每个标签需完整训练一个架构,成本高昂;将部分训练信号作为特征的方法(如OMNI)在大规模空间上无法扩展。本文的核心观察是:部分训练信号不必作为特征(需要对每个候选计算),而可作为标签(只需对少量锚点计算),从而解耦训练成本与排序覆盖范围。

Method: RiPPLE框架包含四个阶段:(1)为整个空间计算无标签特征,包括零成本代理(z)与架构编码(φ);(2)通过编码空间中的最远点采样(FPS)选择少量覆盖锚点,具有k-center覆盖保证;(3)仅训练锚点到短前缀(π·T epochs),通过曲线读取器r(基于schedule形状选择:长干净schedule用对数线性外推,噪声schedule用EMA,早饱和用末段均值)将部分曲线映射为代理标签ŷ;(4)用极端随机树(ExtraTrees)等传播器在锚点对上拟合,将标签传播到全空间s(a)=g(x(a))。整个管道无需逐空间调参,各选择规则均可在训练前确定。

Result: 在12个benchmark cell(4个搜索空间族:拓扑/尺寸/循环/迁移)和DARTS空间(~10^18架构)上评估。150 FTE预算下,RiPPLE-prefix在12个cell中的11个优于所有外部基线(GCN、SemiNAS、GP-WL等),Kendall τ在NAS-Bench-201 CIFAR-10达0.851,在NB201 CIFAR-100为0.846。在监督预测器自有协议下,RiPPLE-full在NAS-Bench-201所有预算列均为最强;RiPPLE-prefix仅用30 FTE即超过最强基线在1%预算下的表现。选出的架构在NB201 CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上距最优分别仅0.08和0.26个点。DARTS空间上选出的架构测试误差2.61%,进入近期可微分搜索的水平。消融实验证实:聚合代理(无锚点)仅达0.593相关度,而两阶段读取-传播设计达0.902;两特征模块在不同族上各有优势,拼接最佳。

Conclusion: RiPPLE证明了"将部分训练信号作为标签而非特征"这一设计选择的有效性:训练成本随锚点数量缩放,排序覆盖全空间。在12个cell上以统一不变的管道达到接近oracle的排序,在监督预测器协议下匹配或领先图transformer基线。其选出的架构与全训练搜索竞争。局限性在于:不专门优化单架构选择(与多保真搜索可比但不统计等价);曲线读取器假设schedule可外推,对混乱schedule保守;覆盖分析限定于保守的近邻传播器而非实际部署的树模型。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) evaluates candidate networks, but fully training enough architectures to rank an entire space is expensive. Zero-cost proxies score architectures at initialization, yet their ranking quality varies across search spaces. Learned predictors reduce evaluation cost but typically require fully trained labels or partial-training features for individual candidates. We introduce $\textbf{RiPPLE}$, $\underline{\textbf{R}}$anking v$\underline{\textbf{i}}$a $\underline{\textbf{P}}$refix-$\underline{\textbf{P}}$ropagated $\underline{\textbf{L}}$abel $\underline{\textbf{E}}$xtrapolation, which treats partial training as a source of labels for a small coverage set of anchors. RiPPLE trains these anchors to an early prefix, extrapolates their learning curves to surrogate labels, and propagates the labels over label-free architecture features. The early-training signal remains a label on the anchors rather than a per-candidate feature. Feature, readout, and encoding rules are selected without held-out accuracy and reused across search spaces. We evaluate the method on twelve benchmark cells from four search-space families and on the larger DARTS space. The results examine ranking quality, label efficiency, architecture selection, and the roles of readout, coverage, and propagation. RiPPLE provides a whole-space ranking from a fractional anchor-training budget, with comparisons interpreted under their respective evaluation and cost protocols.

</details>


### [241] [Granularity-Adaptive Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12424)
*Taoran Liang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Yingguang Yang,Rongrong Zhang,Yingzong Min,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GACA提出一种无需critic的优势估计器，将长视野LLM智能体RL中"步级"与"回合级"优势的混合权重设计为随每步策略不确定性（NLL）连续自适应变化的粒度，从而在关键决策处信任细粒度信号，在常规步骤处信任低方差信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO只能广播单一回合级标量，无法指认驱动成败的具体决策；GiGPO虽以anchor state恢复步级信号，却用固定权重对所有步一视同仁，未区分"关键分叉步"与"近确定性步骤"，在高不确定性步上低估了步级信号的价值，在低不确定性步上又放大了其估计噪声。

Method: 以rollout已记录的逐token负对数似然均值NLL作为每步关键性得分c_t（相对轨迹均值归一），用关于c_t的分段线性、夹紧至[0,1]的门函数λ_t(c_t)将步级优势wA^S_t与回合级优势A^E进行凸混合：A^GACA_t=λ_t wA^S_t+(1−λ_t)A^E；额外融合项可加入折扣回报变化的幅度（α_c控制），但纯熵默认最佳；理论给出命题1（高anchor-条件期望NLL认证动作分歧下界与局部动作价值方差下界，从而认证误差最优步权重下界λ̲_z(h_z)关于h_z单调不减）以及命题2（按状态自适应严格优于任一固定权重当且仅当per-state最优权重不全相等）。

Result: 在ALFWorld与WebShop上以Qwen2.5-Instruct 1.5B/7B为基座，GACA在四个设定下均取得最高成功率，较GiGPO在ALFWorld上+10.2/+2.4、在WebShop上+13.6/+8.4；匹配协议的消融显示：将熵得分随机置换后性能从96.88降至88.54（差于不做自适应的93.05），证明增益来自"权重放在何处"而非"权重是否变化"；扫λ_0呈凹形、最佳0.5；调制强度α_λ=β_λ=0.5最优、升至1.0反退1.44点；奖励门控项在稀疏奖励下无帮助；诊断显示c_t标准差0.31/0.27、λ_t标准差0.08/0.07、夹紧步<1%，门工作在线性区。

Conclusion: 粒度应作为状态的属性而非全局常数；GACA用零额外成本的NLL代理实现状态自适应混合，并在两个理论命题下得到认证与最优性保证；其作为两层级估计器的自然推广（关掉调制即退化为固定混合）在两个agent基准上一致超越GRPO与GiGPO；开放问题包括将误差最优权重在线估计、动作独立去偏的leave-one-out变体选择，以及自适应粒度如何转化为端到端样本效率的完整刻画。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now the standard way to train large language model agents on long-horizon tasks, where dozens of interdependent actions precede a single sparse reward. Critic-free, group-relative methods such as GRPO suit this regime, but they broadcast one trajectory-level scalar to every step and cannot say which decision drove the outcome. GiGPO recovers a step-level signal by grouping time steps that share an anchor state, yet it merges the step- and episode-level estimates under one fixed weight, spending the same resolution on a pivotal branching decision as on a routine, near-deterministic transition. We argue that the right resolution is state-dependent, and propose GACA, a critic-free estimator whose granularity follows an uncertainty-based criticality proxy. GACA scores every step by the negative log-likelihood its own rollout already records, then blends the two advantages with a per-step weight that grows with that score, so the gradient places more weight on the fine-grained signal at above-average NLL and on the episode-level signal below it. We derive an exact risk decomposition for the implemented mixture and show that sufficiently small modulation improves on fixed mixing under positive directional alignment. A separate conditional result bounds local action-value variation using expected NLL, while an error-projection analysis characterizes when mixing adds value beyond scalar uncertainty reweighting. On ALFWorld and WebShop, GACA improves task success over GRPO and GiGPO at both 1.5B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [242] [SAGE-Loop: Reliable Closed-Loop LLM-Driven AutoML with Trial-and-Correction and Adaptive Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12455)
*Junquan Gu,Shibo Cui,Xiangfeng Luo,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE-Loop是一个面向表格数据的可靠闭环LLM驱动的AutoML框架，其核心创新在于将"执行级试错修正"与"基于证据的自适应集成"统一在同一反馈循环中，在20个公开数据集的分类、回归和聚类任务上均取得了优于主流AutoML系统和LLM增强基线的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML系统（如H2O、Auto-sklearn、TPOT）普遍采用"预设搜索空间→一次性求解"的单向流水线范式，存在两个根本缺陷：（1）缺乏"边运行边修正"的执行级纠正机制——一旦中间步骤失败或次优，运行通常被终止或绕过；（2）模型多样性的利用方式不基于证据——固定的集成模板或单一最佳模型选择导致结构多样性无法转化为受控的协同增益。随着LLM被引入AutoML，流水线的可靠性问题变得与自动化效率同等重要，亟需一种具备试错-修正-改进闭环能力且能基于验证证据利用模型多样性的新框架。

Method: 框架由三个核心组件构成：(1) 可反馈的特征生成——基于任务元数据和数据模式条件式生成候选特征，并通过任务感知准则（分类用互信息/特征重要性、回归用F检验、聚类用方差/结构评分）筛选保留可解释的Top-N特征子集；(2) 试错修正的模型生成——采用多轮差异化提示（每轮要求LLM生成结构不同的架构），结合错误驱动修复（注入异常日志）和性能驱动修订（基于当前与历史最佳分数），形成轻量级提示-执行闭环更新公式 M^(r+1)=Update(M^(r), L^(r))；(3) 自适应集成——监督任务从{stacking, bagging, voting}中依据验证证据选择，其中stacking的二级学习器由LLM针对当前基模型集合和任务动态合成；无监督任务通过共生矩阵(co-association matrix)+多数投票/特征间隙估计k+谱聚类+退化结果过滤来融合异构基聚类器。系统配置：R=5轮，每轮生成最多10个候选学习器，各候选最多10次超参数试验，使用OpenAI API（temperature=0.5, max_tokens=200）。

Result: 在20个公开数据集（主要来自FinBench仓库）上：(1) 分类（8个数据集）——SAGE-Loop在所有AUC和ACC指标上均取得最佳或次佳，balance-scale达100% AUC/ACC，credit-g AUC达81.08%±0.24（最佳基线H2O为78.36%）；(2) 回归（4个数据集）——MAE/RMSE/RMSLE均最低，boston的RMSE为2.73（XGBoost 2.89, AutoGluon 2.97）；(3) 聚类（10个数据集）——在20项数据集-指标对比中17项取得最高分，iris ARI达90.39%, seeds ARI达87.59%。完成率分析显示SAGE-Loop在7个数据集上均达5/5自主完成，DS-Agent在boston和california上为0/5，CAAFE在多个数据集上仅3/5。消融实验验证了特征工程和自适应集成两个模块的累加贡献（如seeds ARI从87.59降至64.96/75.34）；残差分布和PCA投影回归曲面可视化证实了特征增强对可解释性和泛化的改善；机制分析表明验证驱动的闭环能自适应地为不同数据集选择最优集成配置。

Conclusion: SAGE-Loop证明了LLM驱动的AutoML可以通过闭环试错修正和基于证据的自适应集成来同时提升执行可靠性和模型多样性的利用效率，从而将"如何生成"与"如何使用模型"统一在同一反馈循环中。论文提出了方法、机制和范式三个层面的贡献：多轮LLM引导的模型合成方法、统一的自适应集成机制（同时支持监督和无监督任务）、以及提示-执行闭环工作流。局限与未来方向：代码和复现脚本将在发表时发布；后续计划将框架扩展到多模态场景、研究闭环建模的理论性质（收敛性与一致性），并优化大规模部署的效率。

Abstract: Automated machine learning (AutoML) is reshaping data-driven science and industrial practice, and as large language models are introduced into AutoML, pipeline reliability becomes as important as automation efficiency. However, existing AutoML still struggles to realize instant feedback and adaptive optimization during execution, so once a run drifts into a suboptimal or failed state, it lacks a process-level correction mechanism. The fundamental pathology lies in its one-way pipeline: intermediate failures are typically terminated or bypassed, while fixed paradigms often strengthen model generation but leave ensemble decisions static, weakening both execution reliability and the controlled use of structural diversity. This indicates that LLM-driven AutoML needs a closed-loop ability for trial-correction-improvement together with evidence-based use of model diversity. To this end, we propose SAGE-Loop, a reliable closed-loop, self-adaptive, LLM-driven AutoML framework that performs multi-round generation and validation for trial-and-repair, and adaptively selects ensemble strategies in both supervised and unsupervised tasks, thereby unifying how to generate with how to use models. Across 20 public datasets, SAGE-Loop consistently improves performance and stability on classification, regression, and clustering tasks. Additional results further show its ability to recover from execution failures and maintain robust pipeline behavior.

</details>


### [243] [A Differentially Private Federated Proximal Optimization Framework for Customer Churn Prediction in Heterogeneous Federated Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12470)
*Joydeb Kumar Sana,Subrata Chakraborty,M M Manjurul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向电信行业客户流失预测的差分隐私联邦近端优化（DP-FedProx）框架，结合 FedProx 聚合算法与 DP-SGD 隐私机制，在非独立同分布（non-IID）异构联邦环境下进行客户流失预测。通过在两个公开电信流失数据集上的实验，证明所提框架在提供强隐私保护（ε=1, δ=10⁻⁵）的同时，仅带来极小的预测性能下降，并在 FedProx 系列模型中表现稳定。SHAP 分析揭示差分隐私与联邦聚合会改变模型依赖的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 电信客户流失预测传统上依赖集中式机器学习，但客户数据集中存储引发 GDPR 等隐私法规合规问题。2. 联邦学习虽无需共享原始数据，但仍存在两个关键挑战：（a）现实电信数据多为 non-IID（异构），标准 FedAvg 在此场景下收敛慢且性能下降；（b）模型更新与训练模型本身仍可能遭受梯度反演、成员推断等隐私攻击。3. 已有 FL 客户流失研究或仍依赖原始数据集中，或未引入差分隐私，缺少同时解决"统计异构性"与"隐私泄露"两问题的统一框架。

Method: 1. 数据集：两个公开电信流失数据集（Dataset-1：100000 样本/101 特征；Dataset-2：IBM 7043 样本/21 特征）。2. 非IID 划分：按客户 tenure（合同月数）将每个数据集划分为 4 个客户端（≤12、13–24、25–48、>48 个月），模拟真实异构联邦场景。3. 特征工程：自适应 WoE（Weight-of-Evidence）编码，独立应用于每个客户端，避免跨客户端信息泄露；高基数特征采用自适应分箱。4. 模型架构：ChurnNet（两层前馈神经网络，隐藏层 128/64，含 BatchNorm、ReLU、Dropout）。5. 联邦算法：对比 FedAvg、FedProx（μ=0.01）、DP-FedAvg、DP-FedProx；DP 通过 Opacus 的 PrivacyEngine 在本地训练时注入（基于高斯机制的 DP-SGD），梯度裁剪范数 C=1.0，隐私预算 ε=1, δ=10⁻⁵。6. 评估：七项指标（Accuracy、Precision、Recall、F1、Specificity、ROC-AUC、PR-AUC），20 轮通信，每轮 5 个本地 epoch，batch size 64。7. 解释性：使用 SHAP（GradientExplainer）对集中式模型与 DP-FedProx 全局模型分别分析 Top-10 特征。8. 基线：6 种集中式模型（RF、LR、XGB、GB、AdaBoost、ChurnNet）+ 4 客户端本地 LR。

Result: 1. Dataset-1：FedProx 较 FedAvg 准确率高 0.26 pp、F1 高 0.22 pp；DP-FedProx（91.61% Acc, 91.19% F1, 97.35% ROC-AUC）与非 DP FedProx 相比仅下降 0.34 pp Acc / 0.31 pp F1；性能逼近最佳集中式 LR（92.10% Acc）。2. Dataset-2：FedProx 较 FedAvg Acc +0.78 pp、F1 +3.79 pp；DP-FedProx 反而略优于非 DP FedProx（+1.0 pp Acc, +1.06 pp F1）；集中式 LR 仍最优（83.51% Acc），联邦与集中式差距更明显。3. 雷达图显示 Dataset-1 上 FedProx 与集中式 LR 形状几乎重合，Dataset-2 上差距扩大，提示性能损失与数据集特征及异构程度相关。4. 隐私效用分析：DP-FedProx 与 DP-FedAvg 性能相近，原因在于（a）tenure 划分造成的异构程度温和；（b）小 μ=0.01 使近端正则项的修正小于 DP 噪声。5. SHAP 分析：集中式模型 Top 特征以 usage/revenue 变量（Dataset-1）和 tenure（Dataset-2）为主；DP-FedProx 模型则更依赖 billing 与 service 相关特征，tenure 重要性下降，提示联邦聚合 + DP 噪声会改变模型依赖的关键特征。6. 与已有研究对比：优于 Huh & Lee [16]（86.50%）与 Krishnan [22]（84.40%），且是首个在电信客户流失场景中同时引入 FedProx 与 DP 的工作。

Conclusion: 1. 提出的 DP-FedProx 框架在 non-IID 异构联邦场景下，能以极小的预测性能损失（≤0.34 pp 准确率）提供强差分隐私保证（ε=1）。2. FedProx 在异构客户端数据上一致优于 FedAvg；差分隐私的引入未显著降低模型性能。3. SHAP 分析揭示联邦聚合与差分隐私会重新分配特征重要性，集中式模型的特征解释不能直接迁移到实际部署的隐私保护联邦模型。4. 该框架为多电信运营商在不交换原始客户数据的前提下协作构建客户流失预测模型提供了实用解决方案，兼顾预测性能与隐私保护。5. 未来工作方向：自适应隐私预算分配、个性化联邦学习、基于 Transformer 的联邦模型。

Abstract: Customer churn is one of the major issues in the telecommunication industry. To predict customer churn, conventional centralized machine learning approaches have been widely used.
