This centralized approach requires customer data to be stored in a central repository, which raises privacy concerns and may violate data protection regulations. Federated learning addresses this problem by allowing multiple telecom operators to collaboratively train a global model without transferring their raw customer data. However, real-world customer data are often heterogeneous (non-IID), which may negatively affect the performance of standard federated learning. Trained models can also suffer from privacy attacks. To address those issues, we propose a Differentially Private (DP) based Federated Proximal optimization (FedProx) framework. All experiments were performed on two publicly available telecom churn datasets. We trained Federated Averaging (FedAvg), DP-FedAvg, FedProx, and the proposed DP-FedProx framework. For baseline comparison, we also used several centralized and local models. To evaluate the models, we employed seven widely used evaluation metrics. The experimental results show that the FedProx based models consistently outperform the FedAvg based models. Compared with the best centralized model, the proposed DP-FedProx framework achieves competitive prediction performance with only a small reduction in accuracy while providing privacy guarantees. To explain our model, we conducted SHAP analysis which shows that DP-FedProx method priorities revenue group features. These results indicate that the proposed DP-FedProx framework provides a practical balance between prediction performance and data privacy protection.

</details>


### [244] [Temporal Recurrence Favors Fewer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12531)
*Ivan Anokhin,Johan Obando-Ceron,Irina Rish,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时间循环（temporal recurrence）是否改变流式任务中模型的最佳计算分配，特别是步内深度（layers）的需求。作者提出"并行专家"（Parallel Experts）框架，统一控制三个分配轴：步内深度L、每层专家数E、专家宽度d。在Sokoban强化学习和FineWeb语言建模上实验表明：在近似匹配的每步计算量下，循环模型的最佳分配显著偏向更少的层数（L=2–4），且性能可比或优于非循环模型（后者需要L=16甚至更多层）。循环降低了减少层数的性能代价，而并行容量（专家数、宽度）成为有用的替代扩展维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习通过堆叠层实现逐步的序贯计算精化。在流式任务（语言建模token序列、视频帧、强化学习环境步）中，循环模型可跨时间步累积隐状态，使每步更新基于先前步骤的表征。这种"时间循环"与"深度循环"（如looped Transformer对同一输入多次迭代）不同，能将序贯计算从单步内分散到时间维度上。然而，当时间循环已经提供跨步的序贯计算后，每步内仍需要多少层（步内深度）尚不明确。已有研究表明循环可使浅层模型具有竞争力，但本文将问题重新定位为计算分配问题：固定总计算量下，应如何在步内深度与并行容量之间分配？

Method: 提出Parallel Experts框架作为探针：在每个外部步内，多个专家模块并行读取当前输入和共享隐状态，输出聚合后更新共享状态；循环版本将最终状态传递至下一步。三个分配轴：步内深度L（顺序阶段数）、每层专家数E、专家宽度d（隐藏维度）。总工作量近似为 W ≈ L·E·C(d)。在固定W下，比较循环与非循环变体的最佳分配。两个实验域：(1) Sokoban（完全可观测规划任务，使用ConvLSTM/ConvGLU专家，actor-critic训练，50M步）；(2) 流式FineWeb语言建模（自回归下一token预测，约1B token，使用Transformer专家，通过top-down反馈使前一步最终残差流进入下一步第一层）。通过空间探测（Sokoban）分析浅层模型如何在时间步间弥补单步内减少的规划深度。

Result: Sokoban：小预算下，循环ConvLSTM性能在L=4即饱和，得分高于L=8才饱和的非循环版本；大预算下循环模型在L=2–4最佳，非循环需L=16才接近。ConvGLU架构同样呈现循环下更早的深度饱和。FineWeb：小预算下循环Transformer在L=2即可达到约3.51验证损失，与非循环L=16最佳分配相当；中预算下循环在L=2或4最佳，非循环需L=16或32。共同结论：(1) 时间循环将最佳计算分配显著推向更少层数；(2) 增加每层专家数对循环与非循环均有益，而深度增益在循环下更早饱和；(3) 探测分析显示浅层循环模型初始规划不如深层完整，但经过几步环境步后赶上，性能相当。延迟测量初步表明L=1、E=16的循环Transformer推理延迟最低（每token 0.108ms，对比L=16、E=1的非循环0.565ms）。

Conclusion: 时间循环不消除序贯计算，但改变了其发生的位置：非循环模型必须在当前步内完成所有必要计算，而循环模型可在新输入到达时继续精化中间表征。在共享问题跨多步贡献的流式任务中，最佳计算分配从步内深度转向并行容量。循环降低了使用更少层的性能代价，专家数和宽度成为重新分配计算的有去处。该假设有望推广到其他流式场景。实践意义：将计算从深度重分配到并行容量可缩短每步串行路径，有望降低延迟、提高训练吞吐量，使一般非线性循环模型在大规模预训练中更可行。局限性：实验覆盖两个领域、有限架构和预算；最佳分配仅为已评估中的最佳而非全局最优；截断BPTT可能使深度循环更新的信用分配更难；并行容量带来的实际硬件加速取决于实现细节。

Abstract: In streaming tasks, recurrent models can carry latent computation across time, allowing each update to build on representations produced earlier. This raises a basic question: once temporal recurrence provides sequential computation across steps, how much depth is still needed within each step? Prior work has shown that recurrence can make shallow models competitive. We instead study this question as a compute-allocation problem, varying within-step depth, expert width, and the number of parallel experts per layer across several compute budgets. For each budget, we compare the best observed recurrent and non-recurrent allocations and the performance they achieve under approximately matched per-step computation. Across Sokoban and autoregressive FineWeb language modeling, we find that temporal recurrence shifts the best observed compute allocation toward substantially fewer layers, with comparable or better performance.

</details>


### [245] [$\text{GSF-}χ$: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12532)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GSF-χ 是一种面向分子手性建模的图 Transformer，通过在所有原子对上施加由立体化学单元（中心或轴向）调制的全局反射偶相位场，并利用手性赝标量 χ 控制查询-键块上的相对旋转方向（Chiral-RoPE），使镜像反射操作反转而非保持该旋转；再结合 C₂ 投影将 ECD 读出分离为偶数（峰数、峰位）与奇数（峰符号）流，从而在保持置换等变与刚体运动不变性的同时，精确区分对映异构体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对映异构体在原子、键和成对距离上完全一致，仅因手性不同而在手性环境中表现出不同的药理/化学性质（如 (S)-沙利度胺与 (R)-沙利度胺对 cereblon 的结合力差异约十倍）。现有欧几里得群不变的分子编码器无法区分镜像异构体；而基于"中心查询"的方法将单一立体中心设为特权观察者，不适用于无唯一中心的轴向或多手性中心情形。该研究旨在解决：手性信号应如何嵌入图注意力机制，使每个原子对都能参与、且对置换和正常旋转保持等变/不变性的同时，区分对映异构体。

Method: 1) 全局反射偶场：每个中心或轴向立体化学单元 s=(τ_s, A_s, χ_s, ρ_s) 对所有原子产生反射偶相位场 ψ_is；2) Chiral-RoPE：手性赝标量 χ_s 决定潜在查询-键块上相对旋转方向，旋转角度 θ=χ_s·Δψ·ω 满足反射反转律 R(-χ)=R(χ)ᵀ=R(ji)；3) 作为残差叠加到普通图注意力之上（不替换），保留标准 Q-K 路径且值向量不旋转；4) C₂ 投影 ECD 读出：将 F(χ) 与 F(-χ) 分别前向，分解为偶流 z⁺=(F(χ)+F(-χ))/2（用于 Number/Position）与奇流 z⁻=(F(χ)-F(-χ))/2（用于 Symbol），保证对映体对的精确互补关系。证明包括：偶奇分解、注解反转、单位顺序无关性、刚体不变性、原子置换等变性、无手性单元时退化为普通图 Transformer。

Result: 在 ChIRo（中心手性）和 ACMP（轴向手性）基准上达到 SOTA：中心 R/S 99.9%、Ranking 72.9%，中心 ECD 全部三个输出（Position、Number、Symbol）均领先所有基线；轴向 Rotation 和 Symbol 分别比最强基线提升 12.6% 和 7.9%。配对 Bootstrap 检验在两个奇性目标上置信区间不含零（p<0.02）。在 RotA 构象偏移测试中优势进一步扩大至 +15.4 和 +10.7。等预算控制表明全局有符号交互的贡献来自放置位置（global signed support）而非参数或边数。85 项算子属性测试与坐标反射审计在所有 1,192 个 ACMP 分子上验证至机器精度。C₂ 投影在完全监督下以约 5% 原始精度换取精确对映体互补，在镜面监督稀缺时变为预测性增益。

Conclusion: GSF-χ 提供了一种将手性信息整合到图 Transformer 中的通用表示：偶数上下文场确定手性作用于哪些原子对，赝标量 χ 确定交互如何反转。模型保持精确的镜像对称性保证（结构级）并在无立体单元时退化为标准无手性编码器。经验结果表明全局有符号交互的放置策略（而非模型容量）是性能提升的关键。该方法为手性分类与 ECD 预测建立了新基准，并为镜像监督稀缺场景提供了归纳偏置保障。

Abstract: Enantiomers share atoms, bonds, and pairwise distances yet can behave differently in chiral environments, so molecular encoders must respect atom relabelings and proper rotations without becoming blind to reflection. We introduce GSF-$\chi$, a graph transformer in which stereogenic units modulate all pairwise interactions rather than single out one atom as special. Each central or axial stereogenic unit creates a reflection-even phase field over all atoms, a handedness pseudoscalar $\chi$ sets the direction of a relative rotation on latent query--key blocks, giving a \textbf{Chiral-RoPE} that reflection inverts rather than leaves fixed. A $C_2$ projection separates mirror-even ECD peak counts and positions from mirror-odd peak signs. We prove the operator's even--odd decomposition and its annotation-inversion, permutation, and unit-order identities under explicit canonical-role conditions; property tests and a coordinate-reflection audit verify the laws end to end. GSF-$\chi$ leads every central-ECD output and improves axial Rotation and Symbol by $12.6\%$ and $7.9\%$ over the strongest baseline. Equal-budget controls attribute the Rotation advantage to global signed support rather than parameter or edge count; the $C_2$ projection yields exact enantiomer-pair consistency at a small raw-accuracy cost under complete supervision and becomes predictive when mirror supervision is scarce.

</details>


### [246] [Quality-Constrained Routing over a Fixed Pool of Quantized Mixture-of-Experts Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12550)
*Zhenghong Huang,Hongfan Wu,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定量化MoE实例池中，依据请求特定质量风险（FWP信号）进行路由分配，以在类级质量预算下最大化吞吐量。核心贡献包括：固定池问题建模、FWP风险度量、LP路由的KKT一致性保证，以及完整Qwen实例上1.284×的离线分配增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化MoE服务中，量化损伤在不同请求和位宽间差异显著（88个Qwen提示上W2/W3/W4的平均ΔNLL分别为0.9437/0.1832/0.0513）。实例物化和副本计数消耗内存且需慢速重新配置，因此应作为上游供给决策；本文聚焦于固定驻留池内的路由问题，即在池边界内最大化吞吐量。

Method: 方法包含三个层次：(1) 理论框架——两专家稀疏MoE模型的精确亲和性-脆弱性分解，以及条件多层top-k扩展；(2) FWP信号——基于路由器范数倒数权重计算提示词的脆弱性加权困惑度，并通过离线校准映射预测候选实例退化；(3) LP路由——窗口级线性规划最大化服务令牌，对偶变量产生带符号缩减奖励得分，在最优价格和原始可行平局分配下与LP最优解KKT一致。

Result: 在88个扩展Qwen提示、完整W2/W3/W4实例（量化全部6,144个专家块）上，τ=0.1513时FWP分配达到1.284×离线模型乘数，优于请求无关混合的1.253×和静态W4的1.000×。FWP相对请求无关混合的相对增益为2.5%（0.031乘数点），并闭合了到受限真实损失排序差距的约62%。跨实例提示FWP预测W3风险的Spearman为0.586、AUROC为0.747；前缀相关性从64令牌的0.238到1,024令牌的0.999。

Conclusion: 论文证明了固定池条件下的路由问题可解且有意义：异构预算分配贡献了0.253的乘数提升（占静态W4之上总增益的大部分），FWP在请求特定层面贡献了增量2.5%。局限性包括：提示长度依赖性、短前缀行为、保持合规性不足、目标再预填充成本以及缺失的等资源副本实验。证据支持固定池分配研究，但尚不支持端到端部署保证。

Abstract: Quantized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) services can hold several pre-materialized instances of one base model, but quantization damage varies sharply across requests and bitwidths. Because instance materialization and replica counts consume memory and require slow reconfiguration, we treat them as upstream provisioning decisions and study routing within a fixed resident pool. Within this fixed-pool boundary, we route each request to maximize modeled throughput under a class-level expected quality-degradation budget and measured instance capacities. To predict this request-specific risk, we introduce FWP (Fragility-Weighted Perplexity), computed from prompt tokens on a reference-instance prefill and calibrated to candidate-instance degradation. Underlying FWP is an exact two-expert affinity--fragility decomposition and a conditional multi-layer top-$k$ expansion whose bias, interaction, route-change, separability, and higher-order terms remain explicit. Using these calibrated risks, a window-level linear program yields a signed reduced-reward score that is KKT-consistent with the LP optimum under optimal prices and primal-feasible tie allocation. On 88 extended Qwen prompts, complete W2, W3, and W4 instances quantizing all 6,144 expert blocks incur mean $\Delta$NLL of $0.9437$, $0.1832$, and $0.0513$. Under the same population and $\tau=0.1513$, FWP allocation reaches a $1.284\times$ offline model-based multiplier versus $1.253\times$ for request-agnostic mixing and $1.000\times$ for static W4, an incremental $2.5\%$ relative FWP gain.

</details>


### [247] [Where Decoder Cosine Similarity Fails for SAE Feature Flow Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12591)
*Hendrik Droste,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Kathrin Korte,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征流发现问题，构建了MLP更新的转移图谱，发现成对解码器余弦相似度无法恢复大多数强因果特征转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通过微调、编辑等手段持续演进，理解内部计算机制对持续学习至关重要。SAE提供可解释特征字典，但残差状态特征与MLP更新特征如何交互产生下游残差特征尚不清楚。成对解码器余弦相似度作为常用的特征流匹配基线，其有效性需要严格检验。

Method: 三阶段管线：(1)在残差流和MLP输出上训练TopK SAE，提取稀疏激活事件；(2)构建候选三元组图谱 $s_k + u_j \rightarrow t_\ell$，基于交互分数（条件期望减去边际期望之和加均值）排序；(3)对解码后的更新特征进行消融验证，测量归一化目标特征变化。在Pythia-160M L7→L8（完整图谱，38,125个强效应三元组）和Gemma-3-4B L21→L22（前30,000排名子集）上实验，比较成对解码器余弦相似度的召回能力。

Result: Pythia完整图谱中，38,125个强消融效应三元组中88.0%在 $c_{s,t}<0.7$ 且 $c_{u,t}<0.7$ 区域，即使阈值降至0.4仍有62.1%被遗漏。高update-target余弦（≥0.7）确实捕获直接写入关系（70.2%有效），但仅覆盖小子集。Gemma交叉模型检查方向一致但更弱：53.6%强效应三元组双余弦低于0.7，且update-target余弦恢复了大多数最强效应。多数强因果转换发生在低余弦区域，表明其为状态条件交互而非成对几何匹配。

Conclusion: 成对解码器余弦相似度不足以恢复大多数因果特征转换，需要三元组级别的转移图谱。状态特征可强烈条件化MLP更新特征（91.9%的(s,u)对消融后效应显著，中位数40.6个标准差）。特征流图谱可作为表征更新机制的诊断工具，未来工作将验证更多层、模型和SAE家族中的复杂模式，并探索其在持续学习分析中的应用。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted through fine-tuning, model editing, and alignment procedures while retaining previously acquired capabilities. Understanding the internal computations that support these adaptations is therefore becoming increasingly important for continual model evolution. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide interpretable feature dictionaries for residual-stream activations and sublayer outputs, but it remains unclear how state features and update features interact to produce downstream residual features. In this work, we focus on MLP updates as a first test case. We construct a transition atlas of triples $s_k + u_j \rightarrow t_\ell$, where a residual-state feature and an MLP-update feature jointly predict a target residual feature, and validate candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature. In a 20M-token Pythia-160M $L_7 \rightarrow L_8$ run, we find 38,125 strong ablation-effect transitions, but 88.0% have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. As a preliminary cross-model check, a run of 20M-token Gemma-3-4B $L_{21} \rightarrow L_{22}$ causally validates only the top 30,000 ranked candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature, and 53.6% of strong-effect triples have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. The Gemma result is directionally consistent with Pythia, but weaker, since update-target cosine recovers many of the strongest Gemma effects and the run is not a full-atlas causal validation. Ultimately, our results suggest that feature flow atlases can serve as diagnostics of representation-update mechanisms and thereby inform tools for steering model updates. Future work will validate more complex patterns across layers, models, and SAE families.

</details>


### [248] [Poisson-Corrector Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12594)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对MYULA算法（Moreau–Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm）用于复合非光滑对数凹分布的采样问题，通过结合离散Poisson修正器（discrete Poisson corrector）、活跃迹估计（active-trace estimates）以及共享噪声曲率反馈（shared-noise curvature feedback），在固定平滑参数下证明了不变测度偏差的Wasserstein误差界为 $O(h)+\widetilde{O}(h^{3/4})$，并由此推导出端到端的迭代复杂度 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样高维非光滑对数凹分布 $\pi(dx)\propto e^{-f(x)-g(x)}dx$ 是统计学和机器学习中的基础问题。MYULA将不可微的 $g$ 用其Moreau包络 $g_\lambda$ 平滑后做Euler离散，但存在三类误差的耦合：Moreau近似偏差、Euler不变测度偏差、初始分布收敛偏差。已有方法（直接用Pedrotti–Whalley的全局光滑性界）只能给出 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的复杂度，作者希望更精细地控制曲率项以改进复杂度。

Method: 方法核心是Poisson修正器框架：(1) 将MYULA与共享Brown运动的精确Langevin扩散同步耦合，定义两点漂移矩阵 $A_\lambda(x,y)=A^f(x,y)+A_\lambda^g(x,y)$；(2) 构造离散Poisson修正器 $\chi_h=\varphi_h(\mathcal{A}_\lambda)\nabla U_\lambda$ 分解局部漂移误差 $D_k$ 为伸缩增量加鞅增量；(3) 通过活跃迹（Moreau Hessian的迹）弱积分估计控制矩；(4) 证明共享噪声下两点曲率迹的一步界 $\mathbb{E}\,\tr A_\lambda^g(X_h,\widehat{X}_1)\le CG\sqrt{d/h}$（通过分段对称性、Jacobian下界与高斯分部积分）；(5) 用Hölder乘积引理从一步界推出反馈估计；(6) 由Lyapunov迭代得出 $\sqrt{m}\,W_2(\pi_\lambda,\widehat{\pi}_{\lambda,h})\le O(h)+\widetilde{O}(h^{3/4})$；(7) 结合Moreau偏差 $\sqrt{m}\,W_2(\pi_\lambda,\pi)\le G^2\lambda/4$，选取 $\lambda=\varepsilon/G^2$ 和相应 $h_\varepsilon$，得到端到端 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ 复杂度。

Result: 主要定理（Theorem 5.1）：在 $0<hL_\lambda\le c$ 下，$\sqrt{m}\,W_2(\pi_\lambda,\widehat{\pi}_{\lambda,h})\le C[R(\sqrt{m}+\sqrt{L_f})+L_f\sqrt{d}]h+C[G\sqrt{R}+K\sqrt{G}\,d^{1/4}\ell_{h,\lambda}^2]h^{3/4}$，其中 $\ell_{h,\lambda}=1+\log(e+\sqrt{h}/(G\sqrt{d}\lambda))$，系数 $R,K$ 显式依赖模型参数但与 $h,\lambda$ 无关，对 $\lambda^{-1}$ 仅呈对数依赖。端到端定理（Theorem 5.2）：取 $\lambda_\varepsilon=\varepsilon/G^2$，步长 $h_\varepsilon$ 满足三个约束，迭代次数 $N(\varepsilon)=\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$，改进此前直接应用Pedrotti–Whalley的 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$。

Conclusion: 本文通过Poisson修正器结合活跃迹与共享噪声曲率控制，为MYULA提供了更精细的不变测度误差分析，在固定模型参数下实现了 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ 的Wasserstein迭代复杂度，优于已有 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的结果。改进源于在修正器分析中用平均曲率代替最坏情形曲率界 $1/\lambda$，但仍保持对 $\lambda^{-1}$ 的对数依赖和标准步长限制。算法本身不变（无Metropolis校正），复杂度以精确proximal求值次数计。

Abstract: We study the classical Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for $\pi(\,\mathrm{d} x)\propto e^{-f(x)-g(x)}\,\mathrm{d} x$, where $f\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ is $m$-strongly convex with $L_f$-Lipschitz gradient and $g:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ is convex and globally $G$-Lipschitz. For the Moreau-smoothed target $\pi_\lambda$ and the MYULA invariant law $\widehat\pi_{\lambda,h}$, we prove \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(\pi_\lambda,\widehat\pi_{\lambda,h}) =O(h)+\widetilde O(h^{3/4}) \] under $0<h(L_f+\lambda^{-1})\le c$, with only logarithmic dependence on $\lambda^{-1}$ in the error coefficients. Combining this estimate with the Moreau approximation bias yields $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ iterations to achieve $\sqrt m\,W_2(\mu_N,\pi)\le\varepsilon$, for fixed model parameters and initialization. The proof combines a discrete Poisson corrector with active-trace estimates and a shared-noise bound for the exact--Euler two-point curvature.

</details>


### [249] [Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning for Cortical Sulci Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12627)
*Saeb Tounsi,Joël Chavas,Pietro Gori,Vincent Frouin,Denis Rivière,Jean-François Mangin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deep learning on cortical surfaces faces a dilemma: capturing the complex topology of over 60 nomenclature-dependent sulci per hemisphere requires high-capacity models, yet the extreme scarcity of expert annotations ($N=62$ subjects) inevitably causes overfitting. Standard supervised approaches fail to generalize in this data-scarce regime, particularly for variable and small sulci where topological ambiguity is high. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning framework. First, we leverage massive unlabeled data (UK Biobank, $\approx$30,000 subjects) to pre-train a spherical encoder using a locally-optimized strategy. By relying solely on continuous surface features (curvature and depth), the relevance of this pre-training is confirmed by the model's ability to detect localized and rare topological traits, such as sulcal interruptions. The downstream labeling task, however, introduces extracted sulcal fundi (lines) as an explicit semantic input. To bridge this dimensional domain gap (from purely geometric to semantic) without causing catastrophic forgetting, these anatomical lines are integrated into the pre-trained backbone via a soft-initialized Topological Prior Injector. Our experiments demonstrate that this approach outperforms fully supervised baselines trained from scratch, achieving a mean Dice of 0.77. Crucially, a local analysis reveals that the self-supervised geometric priors yield the largest performance gains on variable and tertiary sulci (up to 14.8%), confirming that learning the cortex shape is highly beneficial for identifying its rarest parts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Deep learning on cortical surfaces faces a dilemma: capturing the complex topology of over 60 nomenclature-dependent sulci per hemisphere requires high-capacity models, yet the extreme scarcity of expert annotations ($N=62$ subjects) inevitably causes overfitting. Standard supervised approaches fail to generalize in this data-scarce regime, particularly for variable and small sulci where topological ambiguity is high. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning framework. First, we leverage massive unlabeled data (UK Biobank, $\approx$30,000 subjects) to pre-train a spherical encoder using a locally-optimized strategy. By relying solely on continuous surface features (curvature and depth), the relevance of this pre-training is confirmed by the model's ability to detect localized and rare topological traits, such as sulcal interruptions. The downstream labeling task, however, introduces extracted sulcal fundi (lines) as an explicit semantic input. To bridge this dimensional domain gap (from purely geometric to semantic) without causing catastrophic forgetting, these anatomical lines are integrated into the pre-trained backbone via a soft-initialized Topological Prior Injector. Our experiments demonstrate that this approach outperforms fully supervised baselines trained from scratch, achieving a mean Dice of 0.77. Crucially, a local analysis reveals that the self-supervised geometric priors yield the largest performance gains on variable and tertiary sulci (up to 14.8%), confirming that learning the cortex shape is highly beneficial for identifying its rarest parts.

</details>


### [250] [Distortion of AI Alignment Revisited: RLHF is a Decent Utilitarian Aligner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12651)
*Kazusato Oko,Annie Ulichney,Nika Haghtalab,Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了RLHF在用户偏好异质场景下的失真度（distortion），证明先前工作中关于RLHF失真度随Bradley-Terry温度参数β呈指数级恶化并非算法固有缺陷，而是源于偏好数据生成分布μ与KL参考策略π_ref之间的分布失配。通过引入"有效效用"概念和技术性奖励截断，作者在多个KL正则化强度区间内建立了紧致的上界与下界Θ̃(Bβ+β)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Gölz等人(2025)的研究表明，RLHF在异质用户偏好下的失真度可随Bradley-Terry温度参数β呈指数级增长，引发了对RLHF是否适合处理多样化用户偏好的质疑。然而这一悲观理论结果与RLHF在实践中(如GPT-4)的良好表现存在明显矛盾，需要深入理解RLHF失真度的真实根源。

Method: 作者采用理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法：(1)引入"有效效用"概念，捕捉仅保留可从成对比较中恢复的有用信号部分；(2)通过奖励截断技术处理奖励估计中的非线性问题；(3)在社会选择场景(无KL约束)和AI对齐场景(有KL约束)下分别建立失真度的上下界；(4)定义分布失配度量B = max_x log(μ(x)/π_ref(x))来量化偏好数据分布与参考策略的偏离程度；(5)通过合成实验和实际奖励模型分析验证理论发现。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：(1)在社会选择场景下，RLHF失真度上界为O(β)，优于Gölz等人的O(β²)；(2)在AI对齐场景下，失真度上界为C₂·(min{e^Bτ, B, B/τ}+1)β+4；(3)当μ=π_ref时(B=0)，失真度达到O(β)的算法无关最优下界；(4)匹配的RLHF特定下界为Ω̃(min{Bβ/log(Bβ), Bβ/τ})；(5)即使KL预算τ呈指数级小，失真度Θ̃(Bβ)仍不可避免。实验显示实用奖励模型的最大奖励差可达108.8，确认β在实际中处于显著非线性区域。

Conclusion: 研究表明RLHF本质上是合理的功利主义对齐器，其失真度主要受两个因素控制：Bradley-Terry模型的非线性(不可避免，乘以β)和偏好数据分布与参考策略之间的失配(可通过实践缓解，乘以B)。实际启示包括：(1)在线(on-policy)偏好数据采样优于离线方法；(2)在RLHF前使用来自接近μ的源数据对基模型进行微调可缓解分布失配；(3)当分布失配不极端时，RLHF是平均效用最大化的合理选择；(4)未来工作应关注离线偏好数据的预处理方法以及RLHF与更显式的多元对齐方法(如NLHF)的选择标准。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard paradigm for aligning large language models with human preferences, its effectiveness in pluralistic settings has been called into question. Notably, recent work by Gölz et al. (2025) demonstrated that the \textit{distortion} -- defined as the multiplicative gap between the average user utility of the RLHF policy and the optimal average utility -- can scale exponentially with the Bradley-Terry temperature parameter $\beta$ when users have heterogeneous preferences. In this work, we present a fine-grained analysis of the distortion of RLHF with reward clipping and demonstrate that such exponential degradation is not a fundamental property of the algorithm but rather a consequence of distribution mismatch between the distribution generating preference data ($\mu$) and the KL reference policy ($\pi_{\mathrm{ref}}$). To this end, we establish tight upper and lower bounds on the distortion of RLHF across multiple regimes of the KL regularization strength. We show that in a representative regime, under the Bradley-Terry model, the distortion is $\tilde{\Theta}(\beta B + \beta)$, where $B$ is an upper bound on the log density ratio between $\mu$ and $\pi_{\mathrm{ref}}$. In particular, when there is no distribution mismatch (i.e., $\mu = \pi_{\mathrm{ref}}$), RLHF achieves the optimal distortion of $O(\beta)$ up to a constant. Our results suggest that, to reasonably maximize average utility with RLHF, it is preferable to use on-policy sampled preference data or to fine-tune before RLHF on data from a source close to $\mu$.

</details>


### [251] [ProactiveBench: Can Streaming Video Models Really Interact Like Humans?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12658)
*Kaixuan Du,Xin Wan,YuKun Wang,Hang Zhang,Meng Cao,Dai Guan,Ming Chen,Ni Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProactiveBench 是一个面向流式视频模型的主动交互评测基准，以每秒一次的粒度评估模型在持续视频流中的响应时机决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频评测多为"被动式"——评测方选定时间戳再查询，无法衡量模型是否应该在何时主动响应。人类般的主动交互要求模型在"待命请求"下持续监控视频流，在目标事件出现后的合理时间窗内做出回应，其余时刻保持静默。这本质上是一个时序决策问题，需要专门基准来衡量。

Method: 1) 协议：每秒一个间隔、每个样本包含一段连续视频流与一条待命请求，无显式响应提示，前置间隔不参与评测，最多回看30秒上下文。2) 六个子任务，沿两个轴变化——时间容差（零容差/固定3秒/事件持续时长）与语义清晰度（单一定位秒→进行中的活动→通用异常句）。子任务包括：Event Sensitivity (ES)、Onset Response (OR)、Window Response (WR)、Anomaly Alert (AA)、Sustained Silence (SS)、Duplicate Counting (DC)。3) 数据：911个独立视频、流长4–207秒，共5.4小时、18,146个评测决策点；SS含前缀静默60–194秒的延长段；每个视频只用于一个子任务。4) 指标：窗口类子任务将首次非静默响应分为early/in-window/missed；ES在同一条视频上配对响应率R_ES与静默率Q_ES，以几何均值聚合以抑制极端策略；DC采用精确计数匹配准确率。5) 实验：基于196k序列微调Qwen3-Omni-SFT，评测六个系统（M4、ROMA、MOSS-VL-Realtime、AURA、JoyAI-VL-Interaction、Qwen3-Omni-SFT）。

Result: 平均分AVG在5.3–41.0之间，但前三名差距仅0.34分，排名对聚合方式敏感。在OR/WR/AA/SS共628个例子中，四个系统的"过早响应"是"漏检"的2.1–28倍，表明过早响应为主导错误；窗内响应的平均延迟为0.52–1.08秒。MOSS-VL-Realtime响应率最高(82.9)但静默率最低(50.4)，AURA则相反(34.3/99.2)；几何聚合相比算术聚合可使M4下降26.3分，AURA下降8.4分，强调需配对评估响应与静默。

Conclusion: 流式视频模型在主动时序决策上与人类水平仍有显著差距，过早响应是主要失败模式，几何聚合相比算术聚合能显著改变模型排名，因此需联合报告响应率与静默率以准确刻画响应策略。整体上，基准揭示了现有系统在"何时该说话、何时该沉默"这一核心能力上的不足。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to process continuous multimodal input while maintaining temporal context. Existing evaluations are predominantly reactive: they query a model at a selected timestamp and therefore do not assess when it should respond. Proactive interaction instead requires monitoring a standing request, responding within an appropriate interval after the target event, and otherwise remaining silent. We introduce ProactiveBench, which evaluates models at one-second stream intervals without an explicit response cue. Its six subtasks vary trigger ambiguity and timing tolerance. Event Sensitivity geometrically combines response and silence rates on the same recording; four window-based subtasks distinguish early, in-window, and missed responses; and Duplicate Counting penalizes omissions and repetitions. Premature responses outnumber missed responses for four of the six evaluated systems, revealing a substantial gap in the temporal decision-making required for human-like interaction.

</details>


### [252] [SIFPBPNet: A Dual-Path Network for Wearable and Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation via Individualized Steady-state Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12690)
*Shuailong Tang,Xiaoyu Li,Donglin Xie,Wei Chen,Guangpu Zhu,Yelei Li,Yali Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SIFPBPNet的双路径网络架构，用于基于光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号的可穿戴无袖带血压估计。该网络通过稳态特征路径（SFP）和瞬态特征路径（IFP）分别建模个体特异性长期生理稳态和当前短时信号动态，解决了传统群体模型面临的"一对多映射"问题，在OPPO HBPM大规模数据集上取得了优于现有方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高血压是全球重大心血管风险因素，但传统袖带式血压测量具有间歇性不适等问题。基于PPG的无袖带连续监测成为研究热点，但现有群体化深度学习模型面临"一对多映射"挑战——相似PPG波形在不同个体间对应显著不同的血压水平（最高达33%），导致模型倾向于回归群体均值，精度和泛化性受限。核心动机在于引入个体生理稳态先验，将"瞬时映射"问题转化为"个体先验估计"问题。

Method: 1）**双路径架构**：SFP使用图注意力网络（GATv2）从个体多日历史PPG轨迹中提取稳态特征，节点为各历史PPG片段的潜在表示，通过任务引导的注意力池化聚合；IFP从当前PPG片段提取瞬态特征。2）**PPG编码器**：共享的编码器结合多感受野卷积神经网络（MRF-CNN，核大小3/5/7）、卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）和Transformer（含正弦位置编码）。3）**特征融合**：IFP通过门控融合将瞬态表示与人口统计特征和形态学特征结合，再通过交叉注意力机制（瞬态为Query，稳态为Key/Value）注入个体先验。4）**两阶段训练**：SFP在40%个体数据上通过年龄回归、高血压检测、高血压变异性（BPV）检测三个辅助任务进行预训练；IFP在剩余60%个体上以4:1:1比例按个体划分训练。5）**数据集**：OPPO HBPM数据集，790名受试者，每人选10天历史数据+后续预测数据，共20342个历史样本和98258个预测样本，PPG采样率250Hz，标签由Omron袖带血压计同步采集。6）**预处理**：0.5–12Hz四阶Butterworth滤波，模板匹配质量检测，选取12秒高质量片段。

Result: 1）**主性能**：SBP MAE=8.57 mmHg，DBP MAE=5.98 mmHg，R²分别为0.44和0.34，ME±SDE接近零（−0.02±10.76 SBP；−0.10±7.70 DBP），无系统偏差。2）**对比基线**：相比最强基线，SBP MAE降低8.5%，DBP MAE降低4.0%，优于ResNet18、VGGNet、BiLSTM、SEM-ResNet。3）**消融实验**：MRF-CNN→+CBAM→+Transformer逐步改善，但SFP模块带来最大性能跃升（SBP MAE从9.07降至8.57 mmHg）。4）**预训练任务分析**：高血压检测为最佳单任务（8.61 mmHg）；年龄+高血压组合取得最佳综合性能（DBP 5.98 mmHg），验证年龄相关血管老化信息对DBP的补充价值。5）**迁移性**：SFP模块即插即用地集成到各基线架构后，SBP MAE均获得2.8%–13.1%的相对降幅，VGGNet+SFP获得最大改善（13.1%），证明稳态先验对不同特征提取器具有普遍增益。

Conclusion: SIFPBPNet通过引入基于GAT的个体稳态特征路径，有效缓解了无袖带血压估计中的群体异质性和"一对多映射"问题，在大规模可穿戴数据集上达到当前最优精度，同时其SFP模块展现出强大的跨架构可迁移性。该工作验证了个体级表征学习是实现可靠、个性化血压估计的关键步骤，并为其他面临个体间变异性的生理信号建模任务提供了可推广的框架。未来工作方向包括纵向研究验证、自监督/对比学习预训练以及PPG窗口长度的部署优化。

Abstract: Continuous and cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) is of great interest for low-cost and personalized cardiovascular health management. However, significant population heterogeneity and the "one-to-many mapping" problem, where similar waveforms across individuals correspond to different BP levels, limit the accuracy of conventional population-based models. To address this challenge, we propose a dual-path architecture termed SIFPBPNet, which separately represents steady-state and instantaneous features, through a Steady-state Feature Path (SFP) and an Instantaneous Feature Path (IFP). The SFP employs a Graph Attention Network (GAT) to extract individual-specific and long-term characteristics from multi-day historical PPG trajectories. In parallel, the IFP captures short-term dynamics from current PPG segments and incorporates the steady-state prior via a cross-attention mechanism. Experiments on a large-scale wearable dataset demonstrate that SIFPBPNet achieves a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 8.57 and 5.97 mmHg for systolic and diastolic BP, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art models. Furthermore, the SFP module consistently improves performance when integrated into various backbone architectures, yielding 2.8-13.1% relative MAE reductions for systolic BP. These results highlight the strong generalizability and plug-and-play transferability of the SFP module, underscoring its great potential for accurate cuffless BP monitoring.

</details>


### [253] [Write on Paper and Get the Online Digital Trace:\newline A New Era for Handwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12702)
*Florent Imbert,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil,Tanja Harbaum,Alexey Serdyuk,Fabian Kress,Tim Hamann,Peter Kampf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合传感器数字笔（Digipen）、深度学习与嵌入式AI优化的完整硬件-软件系统，用于在普通纸张上书写时实时重建手写轨迹的数字墨迹。该系统通过IMU信号采集、TCN混合专家模型（MOE-CI）、硬件感知的神经架构搜索压缩方法，实现将AI模型部署于笔内微控制器，在资源受限条件下完成实时轨迹推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字化手写方案（如EMR数位板、电容屏或Anoto点阵纸）成本高、需专用基板，无法兼顾传统纸笔书写的触感和自由度。纸笔书写在学习与发展中具有不可替代的优势，但缺乏低成本、广泛兼容的笔迹数字化方案。本文动机在于突破"无外部参考系统、纸面自由书写"这一核心难题，连接传统纸笔书写与数字轨迹捕获。

Method: 1. 硬件层面：STABILO Digipen搭载三轴加速度计、三轴陀螺仪及笔尖压力传感器，通过BLE与移动设备通信；2. 数据采集：通过Wacom EMR模块与Digipen PCB融合的双采集系统，同步记录IMU信号与真实笔迹，采用动态时间规整（DTW）进行时序对齐以构建训练数据库；3. 软件算法：采用基于时间卷积网络（TCN）的混合专家模型（MOE-CI），分别建模"落笔"与"抬笔"两个专家；4. 模型压缩：提出硬件感知的神经架构搜索（NAS），结合NASWOT零成本代理与多目标贝叶斯优化，对FLOP数与精度进行联合寻优，将专家模型参数量从467,452压缩至10,952。

Result: 在Fréchet距离指标下，直接将基于平板数据训练的MOE-CI模型应用于纸笔数据时性能显著退化（0.566→0.738）；在纸笔数据上重新训练后性能大幅提升（0.429）；Kalman滤波仅带来边缘改善（0.542）。压缩模型虽然整体Fréchet距离上升（落笔轨迹重建接近原始模型，但抬笔间跨笔画重建退化），但其仅占用74 KB RAM与66 KB ROM，满足嵌入式微控制器资源约束，可部署于Digipen内部实现实时推断。

Conclusion: 研究证实，IMU信号的噪声特性因书写介质（纸vs.平板）差异显著，仅靠滤波不足以跨越域差异，必须在目标介质数据上重新训练模型。所提出的硬件-软件协同设计方案（传感器笔 + 混合专家深度学习 + 硬件感知NAS压缩）成功在严苛资源约束下实现了从普通纸张到数字笔迹的实时重建，为教育场景中纸质手写与数字分析的融合提供了可行路径。压缩模型在抬笔阶段重建仍有改进空间，未来可针对该专家进一步优化。

Abstract: Capturing the digital trace of handwriting usually requires a specific stylus and a compatible substrate, be it a capacitive touchscreen, an ElectroMagnetic Resonance (EMR) tablet as used in Wacom systems or special paper. While writing on regular paper offers rich haptics, no latency and is well known for improving information retention, no low-cost and widely accepted, effective solution exists to digitize such a pen trace. The challenge is to accurately track the pen's trajectory without an external reference system while allowing unrestricted freedom of pen movement across a surface. We propose an innovative solution that combines a digital pen, advanced artificial intelligence algorithms, and adaptive AI techniques to reconstruct the digital trace of handwriting. Our approach integrates hardware development, focusing on a sensor-equipped pen, with software innovations to optimize trajectory reconstruction and processing in real time using an embedded AI. This work aims to advance the state-of-the-art in automated trace reconstruction of handwriting, enabling a seamless connection between traditional handwriting on paper and capturing the trace digitally.

</details>


### [254] [Physics-Guided Synthetic High-Frequency Ultrasound Generation for Skin Layer Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12735)
*Junkyung ju,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理引导的高频超声（HFUS）合成数据生成框架，利用k-Wave仿真构建多层皮肤声学模型，自动生成配对的合成HFUS图像与密集8类皮肤层标注（含表皮、SLEB、真皮、皮下组织、筋膜、肌肉等），用于缓解皮肤层分割任务中标注稀缺的问题。下游实验表明，合成预训练+真实微调在四种可训练架构中有三种实现了优于纯真实训练的Dice/IoU性能，证明合成数据具有可迁移的解剖与纹理监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频超声（HFUS）能无创可视化皮肤表层结构，但自动皮肤层分析受限于密集标注数据稀缺：现有公开HFUS数据集通常仅标注表皮与SLEB，而真皮、皮下组织、筋膜、肌肉等深层结构的密集标签几乎不可得。现有合成超声工作多基于数据驱动风格迁移，缺乏针对多层皮肤的物理引导生成，难以提供密集的解剖标签监督。

Method: 1) 构建多层声学皮肤体模，为空气、耦合介质、表皮、SLEB、真皮、皮下组织、筋膜、肌肉八类分别赋声速、密度、散射参数；2) 通过k-Wave时域声学仿真生成配对的灰度/绿通道合成HFUS图像（2067×1555像素，20 MHz，96扫描线，7.0 mm轴向深度）；3) 引入三类结构/外观变化——层厚变化、边界形态扰动、组织异质性变化，并结合标签保持的数据增强；4) 自动导出密集8类原始整数掩码及元数据；5) 下游采用合成预训练+真实Mendeley数据集微调策略，在Fresh SegUNet、U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer四种架构上评估表皮/SLEB分割性能。

Result: 生成200组合成样本，可控地实现层厚、边界不规则度、组织异质性变化，并保持像素级标签对应。在Mendeley真实HFUS测试集上，合成预训练+真实微调在四种架构中有三种（Fresh SegUNet、U-Net、SegFormer）mean Dice/IoU优于纯真实训练，最佳结果为Fresh SegUNet：mean Dice 83.40%、mean IoU 71.79%；DeepLabV3+略有下降。表明合成HFUS可提供可迁移的解剖与纹理线索，但合成-真实外观差距（散斑分布、衰减、边界锐度、反射伪影）仍限制了性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 基于物理引导的合成HFUS生成框架可生成配对图像与密集多层标签，为皮肤层分割在标注稀缺场景下提供了可行的预训练来源。合成预训练可使下游真实域性能与纯真实训练相当，并在多数架构上带来小幅增益。该工作被视为合成HFUS可用性的初步验证而非最终解决方案，未来需进一步缩小合成-真实外观差距以推动多层HFUS分割的可靠应用。

Abstract: High-frequency ultrasound (HFUS) enables noninvasive visualization of superficial skin structures, but automated skin-layer analysis is limited by the scarcity of densely annotated data. Existing real HFUS datasets commonly provide annotations for superficial targets such as the epidermis and subepidermal low-echogenic band (SLEB), while dense labels for deeper structures such as dermis, subcutaneous tissue, fascia, and muscle are rarely available. We propose a physics-guided synthetic HFUS generation framework for skin layer segmentation. The framework constructs multilayer acoustic skin phantoms, assigns layer dependent acoustic properties, and uses k-Wave simulation to generate paired synthetic HFUS images, dense layer masks, and simulation metadata. To evaluate whether the generated data provide transferable supervision, we use it for downstream segmentation pretraining and fine-tune the models on real Mendeley HFUS data. Synthetic pretraining followed by real fine-tuning achieved real-domain performance comparable to real-only training and improved mean Dice/IoU in three of four evaluated trainable architectures. These results suggest that physics-guided synthetic HFUS images contain transferable anatomical and textural cues for real-domain skin layer segmentation, although further reduction of the synthetic-real appearance gap is needed to enable greater gains. The code and data are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [255] [Optimizing for the decision not the prediction: an exploration of Smooth Net Benefit as a training objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12752)
*Koen M.F. Gorgels,Lasai Barreñada,Maarten van Smeden,Ben Van Calster,Ewout W. Steyerberg,Wouter A.C. van Amsterdam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Smooth Net Benefit (σNB) 的可微分 Net Benefit 近似值，作为临床决策阈值相关的训练目标。作者在 Framingham 数据集和 TabZilla 基准的 44 个数据集上评估了 σNB 用于逻辑回归、GAM 和 XGBoost 的效果，结果显示 σNB 训练并未带来一致的 Net Benefit 提升，不能作为 NLL 训练的通用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的预测模型通常使用伯努利负对数似然（NLL）进行训练，但下游临床决策往往依赖于特定的风险阈值，这导致训练目标与决策效用之间存在脱节。因此需要一种能够与阈值特异性临床效用对齐的训练目标。

Method: 提出 Smooth Net Benefit (σNB)，作为 Net Benefit 的可微分近似值。在 Framingham 心血管风险数据集和 44 个 TabZilla 数据集（72 个数据集-阈值组合）上，将 σNB 作为训练目标应用于逻辑回归、广义可加模型（GAMs）以及三种 Hessian 实现的 XGBoost，并与传统的 NLL 训练进行对比。

Result: 在 Framingham 数据集上 σNB 训练未带来 Net Benefit 的一致提升。在 TabZilla 基准上：逻辑回归的标准化 Net Benefit 从 NLL 的 0.5669 提升到 σNB 的 0.5765（均值差 0.0096，95% CI -0.0001 至 0.0193）；GAM 从 0.5921 下降到 0.5625（均值差 -0.0296）；XGBoost NLL 为 0.6745，σNB 各实现为 0.6723-0.6735。逻辑回归中 σNB 增益与 XGBoost 相对于 NLL 训练逻辑回归的性能优势正相关。

Conclusion: σNB 的效果具有上下文依赖性，收益主要集中在逻辑回归中，对更灵活的模型类别几乎没有益处。这表明当模型灵活性有限时，决策聚焦优化（decision-focused optimization）最有价值。研究结果不支持将 σNB 作为 NLL 训练的通用替代方案，但支持在传统基于似然的训练可能无法充分捕捉决策相关结构的情形中进一步研究决策聚焦目标。

Abstract: Objective Prediction models are commonly trained using objectives such as Bernoulli negative log-likelihood (NLL), although downstream clinical decisions may depend on specific risk thresholds. We introduce Smooth Net Benefit ($\sigma$NB), a differentiable approximation of Net Benefit designed to align model training with threshold-specific clinical utility. Materials and Methods We evaluated $\sigma$NB as a training objective for logistic regression, generalized additive models (GAMs), and XGBoost with three Hessian implementations. Experiments used the Framingham cardiovascular risk dataset and 44 TabZilla datasets comprising 72 dataset-threshold combinations. Results $\sigma$NB training did not consistently improve Net Benefit in Framingham. Across the TabZilla benchmark, mean standardized Net Benefit for logistic regression increased from 0.5669 with NLL to 0.5765 with $\sigma$NB (mean difference 0.0096, 95% CI -0.0001 to 0.0193). For GAMs, mean standardized Net Benefit decreased from 0.5921 to 0.5625 (mean difference -0.0296, 95% CI -0.0721 to 0.0129). For XGBoost, NLL achieved 0.6745 compared with 0.6723--0.6735 across $\sigma$NB implementations. In logistic regression, $\sigma$NB gains were positively associated with the performance advantage of XGBoost over NLL-trained logistic regression. Discussion The effect of $\sigma$NB was context dependent, with modest gains concentrated in logistic regression and little benefit for more flexible model classes. This suggests that decision-focused optimization may be most useful when limited model flexibility leaves greater scope for improvement. Conclusion Our results do not support $\sigma$NB as a general replacement for NLL training, but support further investigation of decision-focused objectives in settings where conventional likelihood-based training may not adequately capture decision-relevant structure.

</details>


### [256] [Curriculum-Based Adversarial Heterogeneous Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Quad-Copter Landing in Maritime Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12758)
*Allan Minh-Tam Nguyen,Sree Showrya Kotala,Stefan Banioi-Crijman,Kurt Driessens,Rico Möckel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARL-AC框架，将课程学习与异构多智能体对抗强化学习相结合，用于海上环境中四旋翼无人机被船载机械臂空中捕获的协同控制策略学习，在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中通过HAPPO算法训练，相比课程域随机化方法在分布外高海况下显著提升了泛化性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机降落方式（悬停后切断动力自由落体）在小型船舶甲板或强风环境下可靠性差，易损坏设备。本文探索用机械臂进行空中捕获的替代方案，但该方案需要无人机与机械臂之间高度协同的异构多智能体控制策略，且海上风浪扰动为策略鲁棒性带来了严峻挑战。

Method: 1) 采用HAPPO（异构智能体近端策略优化）作为基础多智能体学习框架，配合团队分离的critic防止异构任务中的价值崩溃；2) 引入零和对抗风智能体，其动作通过双曲正切缩放并经一阶低通滤波平滑后，与Dryden湍流叠加形成复合风扰动；3) 风力大小通过指数滤波驱动Pierson-Moskowitz海浪谱，进而在五个自由度上生成船舶平台运动，实现风浪耦合；4) 设计四阶段课程逐步引入风扰与海浪，并采用非对称EMA（成功时β=0.02缓慢上升，失败时β=0.10快速回落）控制阶段进退；5) 设计双向对抗课程，将对抗智能体胜率锚定在30%阈值附近，通过乘性更新Dryden湍流标准差σ，维持有效对抗压力。

Result: 在分布内海况0/4/5评估中，HARL-AC与课程域随机化（DR）方法成功率相近（最高97.5%），但HARL-AC训练收敛略快。在分布外海况7/8/10测试中，HARL-AC显著优于DR：在海况10上中位成功率提升达16%（60% vs 45.9%），且崩溃率降低达14%。代价是HARL-AC策略更为谨慎，超时率略有上升（<3%），因多花数秒完成降落。该策略表现出对不可预测风扰更强的抗干扰能力，优先保证稳定性而非速度。

Conclusion: HARL-AC框架通过对抗风智能体在训练中暴露协同团队到更广阔的搜索空间，使其学得更为通用的扰动抑制策略。所得策略虽以稍长降落时间为代价，但在分布外高海况下具有显著更优的鲁棒性和安全性，为实际自主无人机降落应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。预期在仿真到真实的迁移中，若环境与仿真存在偏差，HARL-AC将表现出更强的适应性。

Abstract: Recovering unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in maritime environments is challenging due to wind turbulence and ship-deck motion, making it a valuable test case for alternative control and learning approaches as conventional landing approaches often become unreliable. We study simulated mid-air capture of quadrotor UAVs by a ship-mounted robotic arm, learning robust cooperative control policies with Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) Reinforcement Learning. We train with HAPPO using a curriculum and an adversarial wind agent (HARL-AC) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, and compare the obtained control policies against those generated through curriculum-based domain randomization and a benchmark trained on a single sea state. In-distribution evaluation on sea states $0/4/5$ shows comparable success for HARL-AC and domain randomization of up to $97.5\%$. On out-of-distribution sea states $7/8/10$, HARL-AC generalizes better, achieving up to $16\%$ higher median success rate at sea state 10, and substantially lower crash rates of up to $14\%$ compared to the domain randomization policy. Furthermore, we show that the adversarially trained policy shows more cautious behavior, slightly increasing timeouts by $<3\%$, but yields safer recovery behavior in severe, unseen conditions.

</details>


### [257] [Convergence of Stochastic Gradient Methods under Heavy-Tailed Noise and Hölder Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12785)
*Misbah Uz Zaman,Anirbit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸随机优化问题中，同时存在Hölder光滑性(s∈(0,1])和重尾噪声(α∈(1,2]有限α阶矩)条件下的随机梯度方法的收敛性。论文建立了三个主要收敛结果：(1) 标准SGD在α≥1+s时达到O(T^{-s/(1+s)})的收敛速率，推广了经典的非凸SGD速率；(2) δ-正则化梯度裁剪(δ-GClip)在相同条件下达到O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})的平稳性速率；(3) 标准梯度裁剪(G-Clip)在α≥1+s时恢复上述速率，而在α<1+s的重尾区域首次给出O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中两个重要的实际现象：(1) Simsekli et al. (2019) 和 Zhang et al. (2020b) 等研究表明深度网络训练中梯度噪声常呈重尾分布（α<2，可能具有无限方差）；(2) 许多实际损失函数（如q范数hinge损失和回归损失，q∈(1,2]）只满足s-Hölder连续梯度而非Lipschitz光滑。现有文献对这两个挑战往往分开处理，本文旨在同时在Hölder光滑目标和重尾噪声条件下下分析随机梯度方法的收敛性，并首次为"α<1+s"的极重尾区域提供收敛保证。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 基于(L,s)-Hölder光滑的广义下降引理，引入Young不等式处理分数阶项；(2) 对标准SGD直接采用Lyapunov不等式将α阶矩控制转化为(1+s)阶矩；(3) 对δ-GClip算法（Tucat et al. 2025提出），将状态空间分为小梯度和大梯度两个区域，对每个区域分别建立偏置和方差的界并推导统一单步下降不等式；(4) 对G-Clip进行类似的区域划分分析，关键创新是在α<1+s区域使用"clipping region上界"技巧处理噪声矩（因Lyapunov不等式不再适用）；(5) 通过精细的γ和η的优化选择平衡偏置、方差和下降项得到最优收敛速率。技术上采用Markov不等式控制尾区域概率，反向三角不等式建立几何缓冲区，以及Cutkosky-Mehta内积下界。

Result: 三个主要收敛结果：(1) 标准SGD：当α≥1+s时，以η=O(T^{-1/(1+s)})的步长运行T次，达到期望平方梯度范数的速率O(T^{-s/(1+s)})；(2) δ-GClip：当α≥1+s时，达到平稳性度量min{‖∇f‖², γ‖∇f‖}的速率O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})；(3) G-Clip：当α≥1+s时与δ-GClip同速率；当α<1+s（极重尾）时达到O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})，是任何随机梯度方法在该区域的首个收敛保证。实验上：SGD收敛速率确实与α无关（验证定理4.1）；在s=0.8, α=1.3的极重尾设置中，SGD无法收敛，而G-Clip的实测斜率-0.29接近理论斜率-0.30。

Conclusion: 本文建立了在(L,s)-Hölder光滑目标和重尾噪声同时存在条件下，SGD、δ-GClip和G-Clip的首个收敛保证。特别地，G-Clip在α<1+s的极重尾区域的收敛保证填补了现有文献的空白。比较分析显示：经典设置(s=1, α=2)下SGD和G-Clip均达到最优O(T^{-1/2})；在α<1+s区域，G-Clip是唯一有保证的方法。开放问题包括：(1) 推导这些速率的信息论下界；(2) 发展Hölder光滑条件下的高概率收敛保证；(3) 研究动量与梯度归一化在分数Hölder景观下的交互作用。

Abstract: Classical convergence guarantees for stochastic gradient methods typically assume Lipschitz-smooth objectives and finite-variance gradient noise, both frequently violated in practice. In contrast, we study nonconvex stochastic optimization under the joint relaxation of these assumptions: objectives with $(L,s)$-Hölder continuous gradients, $s\in(0,1]$, and gradient noise satisfying only a bounded $\alpha$-th moment condition for $\alpha\in(1,2]$. We establish three convergence results. Firstly, that standard SGD converges at rate $O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$ whenever $\alpha\ge1+s$, extending the classical nonconvex SGD rate to heavy-tailed noise and Hölder smoothness simultaneously. Secondly, we analyze $\delta$-regularized gradient clipping ($\delta$-GClip), a provable trainer of wide and deep nets, and establish a stationarity rate of $O(T^{-2s(\alpha-1)/[(1+s)(2\alpha-1)]})$ under the same condition. Thirdly, we analyze standard gradient clipping (G-Clip) and show that it recovers the above rate for $\alpha\ge1+s$ while in the very heavy-tailed regime $\alpha<1+s$, it has a convergence rate $O(T^{-2s(\alpha-1)/[(\alpha-1)+s(2\alpha-1)]})$ --- the first convergence guarantee in this regime for any stochastic gradient based method.

</details>


### [258] [VertiFuseX: Generalizable Financial Forecasting via Multi-Stream Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12793)
*Aashish Bohra,Vivek Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VertiFuseX模型，通过在倒数第二层（penultimate layer）对LSTM、Bi-LSTM和St-LSTM三路时序特征进行垂直融合（vertical fusion），保留更丰富的中间层时序信息，同时结合并行的DNN流捕获非时序特征，实现跨市场、可泛化的股价预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习股价预测模型存在三大系统性缺陷：(1) 决策级融合（decision-level fusion）将时序表示压缩为标量预测后再集成，导致信息丢失和异构时序动态无法有效交互；(2) 深度集成模型依赖大量超参数调优，缺乏正则化，导致泛化崩溃；(3) 单尺度时序建模无法同时捕获短期波动和长期趋势。核心研究问题：如何设计混合LSTM架构保留多尺度时序特征、消除主观调优，并在不同市场制度下实现稳健泛化。

Method: 架构包含三层：(1) DATAETL层——从Yahoo Finance提取2010-2024年收盘价，严格按交易时间索引（去除非交易日），Min-Max归一化（仅在训练集拟合），采用20天滑动窗口和静态时序分割（训练集约3388天，测试集365天）。(2) FeatExt层——并行提取四路penultimate特征：LSTM分支（2层，128→64单元）捕获序列记忆；Bi-LSTM分支（2层双向，128→32/方向）捕获双向上下文；St-LSTM分支（3层，128→128→64）捕获层级趋势；DNN分支（MLP，128→64，ReLU+Dropout 0.3）捕获非时序抽象。各分支penultimate维度统一对齐为d_T=64。(3) VertiFuse层——两阶段垂直融合：Stage 1将三路时序特征沿特征轴拼接（192维），经可学习仿射变换W_fuse和ReLU映射回64维；Stage 2将时序融合特征与对齐后的DNN特征拼接（128维），再经W_comb仿射变换得64维联合表示，最终通过线性回归头输出。所有参数（含融合矩阵）联合端到端训练，使用Adam(lr=1e-4)、MSE损失、ℓ₂正则化(1e-4)、Dropout 0.3、Early Stopping(patience=10)。严格时序样本外测试，无前瞻偏差。

Result: 基线比较（S&P 500、DJIA、NYSE、NASDAQ）：相比LSTM/Bi-LSTM/St-LSTM/ARIMA，VertiFuseX在全部12个指标-数据集对中均最低，MAPE降低30-54%，MAE和RMSE降低超40%。S&P 500：MAE=30.22, RMSE=40.57, MAPE=0.59（vs St-LSTM降低35.0%/31.6%/47.3%）。SOTA比较（10个全球指数、33组指标对比）：在所有对比中均最优，HSI MAE降低34.63%，KOSPI MAE/RMSE/MAPE分别降低28.2%/29.36%/27.2%，DJIA（1991-2010长周期）RMSE降低36.4%。Diebold-Mariano检验在1%水平上显著。消融实验证实penultimate融合优于最终层融合和决策级集成（S&P 500上MAE进一步降低24.2%，NASDAQ降低21.1%）。梯度显著性分析显示模型一致关注滞后9-15天的中期依赖。经济验证：2024年样本外交易模拟中，S&P 500和DJIA接近买入持有收益但风险调整更优；COVID-19崩盘中最大回撤-26.45%（vs 买入持有-33.67%），2022熊市中最大回撤-17.67%（vs -25.38%）。计算效率：675k参数、2.6 MB内存、1.5 ms/样本推理延迟。

Conclusion: VertiFuseX通过在倒数第二层对异构时序表示进行垂直融合，有效保留中间层信息并实现互补专化，相比传统决策级融合在预测精度、跨市场泛化和计算效率上均取得显著提升。其轻量级设计和可解释性使其适合实际部署。局限性包括：仅使用单变量收盘价输入、采用固定超参数配置、缺乏完整的滚动前向验证和概率预测区间。未来工作将扩展至多变量输入、滚动验证、不确定性量化、注意力融合机制以及跨市场迁移学习。

Abstract: Stock price prediction remains challenging due to the non-stationary and noisy nature of financial time series. Existing deep learning models often rely on rigid decision-level fusion, ad hoc hyperparameter tuning, and compressed final-layer outputs, causing information loss, overfitting, and limited cross-market generalization. We propose VertiFuseX, a hybrid LSTM architecture using penultimate-layer vertical fusion of multi-scale temporal representations. VertiFuseX stacks and reweights penultimate features from LSTM, Bi-LSTM, and St-LSTM branches, integrates a parallel DNN stream, and jointly optimizes all components via backpropagation under a fixed hyperparameter configuration. This preserves richer intermediate temporal information across scales. Evaluated on 15 years (2010-2024) of closing prices from 10 global equity indices using strict chronological out-of-sample testing with the final 365 trading days held out, VertiFuseX achieves 30-54% MAPE reductions and over 40% improvements in MAE and RMSE versus LSTM-based baselines, and outperforms seven state-of-the-art models across 33 metric-dataset comparisons. Ablation studies confirm penultimate-layer fusion drives these gains over final-layer fusion and decision-level ensembling. Gradient-based saliency analysis shows consistent emphasis on mid-range dependencies at lags 9-15 days. Economic validation via algorithmic trading simulation under extreme market regimes shows reduced maximum drawdowns and superior risk-adjusted returns. With 675k parameters, a 2.6 MB memory footprint, and 1.5 ms/sample inference latency, VertiFuseX offers a lightweight, interpretable, deployment-ready framework for robust financial forecasting.

</details>


### [259] [GenOR-Twin: A Semantic Middleware for Integrating Operational Discourse with Mathematical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12863)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Lubin Cui,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GenOR-Twin框架，这是一个神经-符号（neuro-symbolic）系统，旨在将非结构化的运营日志转化为严格的数学优化问题。它利用大型语言模型（LLM）作为语义翻译器而非直接求解器，结合精确组合方法以保证可行性，并引入动态约束注入机制，使系统能够根据定性的运营扰动实时调整优化模型，适用于数字孪生场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实运营环境中存在大量非结构化日志和不确定扰动，传统的静态优化模型难以实时适应。现有方法在将自然语言/日志转化为可求解的数学优化问题时存在“翻译鸿沟”，且LLM直接求解缺乏可行性保证。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，构建一个兼具语义理解能力与数学严谨性的数字孪生决策系统。

Method: 1. **神经-符号架构**：将LLM定位为语义翻译器，负责将非结构化日志映射为形式化优化问题，而求解仍由精确组合优化方法完成，以保留可行性保证。2. **动态约束注入机制**：在运行时将定性的运营扰动事件（如设备故障、需求变更）转化为形式化的数学约束，实时修改优化问题的可行域。3. **双向耦合**：运营观测更新虚拟模型（数字孪生），优化决策反馈至知识图谱，满足数字孪生的同步需求。4. **自适应决策策略**：根据系统可用松弛（slack）自动选择低复杂度的调度修复或完全重优化。5. **跨域泛化**：在六个不同的优化领域验证框架的通用性。

Result: 该框架在六个不同的优化领域（如调度、路径、分配等）上验证了泛化能力，能够将静态优化模型转变为可适应真实环境不确定性的弹性系统。系统通过动态约束注入和自适应修复/重优化机制，在保持数学可行性的前提下，实现了对实时运营扰动的快速响应。

Conclusion: GenOR-Twin成功地将LLM的语义理解能力与精确优化的可行性保证相结合，通过神经-符号协同和动态约束注入机制，解决了非结构化日志到数学优化模型的实时翻译难题。该框架将静态模型转化为能够适应运营不确定性的弹性数字孪生系统，在多个优化领域展现出了良好的通用性。

Abstract: We introduce GenOR-Twin, a neuro-symbolic framework that bridges the translation gap between unstructured operational logs and rigorous mathematical optimization. Our architecture uniquely positions Large Language Models as semantic translators rather than direct solvers, ensuring that the system retains the feasibility guarantees of exact combinatorial methods. { \color{red}We design a dynamic constraint injection mechanism (the runtime translation of qualitative disruption events into formal mathematical constraints) that allows the system to structurally modify the optimization problem's feasibility region in real-time based on qualitative human inputs. The resulting bidirectional coupling---where operational observations update the virtual model state and optimized decisions are reflected back into the Knowledge Graph---satisfies the synchronization requirement of a proper Digital Twin. The framework features an adaptive decision policy} that automatically selects between low-complexity schedule repair and full re-optimization by analyzing the available system slack. Finally, we demonstrate the generalization of this approach across six distinct optimization domains, {\color{red}turning static models into resilient systems that adapt to the operational uncertainty and variability of real-world environments.}

</details>


### [260] [What an odour descriptor corpus can and cannot measure: valence, attenuation, and the ceiling of the public record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12875)
*Stylianos Kampakis,Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了四个公开气味描述符语料库（Dravnieks、Keller、Leffingwell、GoodScents），发现合并使用前必须进行系统偏差和异质性修正；分子结构与全部描述符标签仅能达到人类感知可靠性上限的约33%，而单个"愉悦度"评分即可达到54.6%，证实缺失的方差维度是效价（valence），且必须直接测量而非从描述符推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器嗅觉依赖合并的公共描述符语料库进行训练，但一个前提——即相同词汇在不同语料库中测量同一构念——从未被检验过；同时也从未界定这些语料库的测量上限。本文动机在于：质疑"共享词汇=可合并数据"这一未经检验的假设，并量化公共记录究竟能解释多少可靠的人类感知相似性。

Method: （1）对Pyrfume中四个语料库以PubChem CID为锚进行审计；（2）在分子层面条件化后，使用McNemar检验作为语料库效应的精确条件检验（用Lean 4对Mathlib机器验证了20条定理）；（3）用饱和双变量probit分离准则（threshold）与构念（tetrachoric相关系数），并以ETS delta量表报告微分项目功能（DIF）；（4）以Keller的55人小组剖面作为感知准则，将分子结构（Morgan指纹+RDKit描述符）和合并描述符的相关性与小组自身分半信力封顶值作比较；（5）将愉悦度、强度、熟悉度作为独立的非特征反应分离分析，并以Dravnieks 1985数据做外部复制；（6）预注册的IFRA-2019对Sharma-2021a配对做一次性确认。

Result: 语料库间存在高度异质（I²=80%）且非均匀的描述符级分歧（z=17.2），无单一偏移可修复合并；中位tetrachoric一致性0.795 vs Cohen κ 0.413，差异0.382表明分歧属"准则"而非"构念"；109个可估计描述符中36个在ETS量表上呈现大DIF；分子结构+全部描述符标签仅达感知相似性上限的33.9%，而单个愉悦度评分即达54.6%（在1985年Dravnieks独立仪器上复制为57.1%）；从描述符恢复效价的相关系数仅0.457，以此构建的效价坐标仅达16.6%；5名评分者即可超越结构+全部描述符；预注册的IFRA-Sharma配对证实异质性与衰减差距，但平均效应失败（log odds +1.426），说明合并必须同时修正系统偏移和描述符级分散。

Conclusion: 公共描述符记录未能承载气味感知的主导维度（效价），且无法通过推断获得——这是关于"应收集什么"而非"应如何建模"的结论；合并需要针对系统性偏移和异质描述符级成分分别修正，且非均匀性使两者都不能视为常数；交叉对照表（crosswalk）应作为有版本的对象发布，匹配类型需附证据；效价应在数据源头直接测量而非事后推导。实践建议：15–20名评分者即可接近饱和点，是唯一"投入有回报"的维度。

Abstract: Machine olfaction trains on pooled public descriptor corpora, but whether a shared descriptor word measures the same thing across corpora has not been tested, nor has the ceiling of what any of them can measure. We audit four corpora from Pyrfume. Conditioning on the molecule makes McNemar's test the exact conditional test of the corpus effect. Corpora disagree heterogeneously across descriptors ($I^2 = 80\%$) and non-uniformly with labelling breadth ($z = 17.2$), so no single offset repairs pooling. Median tetrachoric agreement is 0.795 against median $\kappa$ of 0.413: sources largely concur on which molecules deserve a word and differ on how readily they apply it. Of 109 descriptors with an estimable effect, 36 show large differential functioning on the ETS scale. Against a human panel's reliability, Morgan fingerprints with the full RDKit descriptor block reach 32.9\% of achievable; adding every label from two merged corpora reaches 33.9\%. The gap does not close with model capacity, encoding choice, more molecules, or more words. The missing variance is valence. One pleasantness rating per molecule reaches 54.6\% of achievable (57.1\% on an independent older instrument). Valence recovered from descriptors ($\rho = 0.457$) yields only 16.6\%, so it must be measured. Five raters exceed structure plus the full descriptor record; fifteen to twenty saturate. We release a descriptor crosswalk and twenty machine-checked theorems.

</details>


### [261] [Quantifying the Value of Privileged Information Using a PAC-Bayesian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12891)
*Vasily Bokov,Sebastian Schmitt,Vedran Dunjko,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PAC-Bayes框架的算法无关方法，通过比较带PI（特权信息）与不带PI时的最优风险上界证书之间的对数配分函数差（log-partition gap），来量化特权信息的潜在价值，无需测试时数据即可从训练阶段评估PI的真实效用增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUPI（Learning Using Privileged Information）理论保证均为特定算法和特定设置，缺乏一个通用的信息论框架来解释PI何时、如何传递有用知识。需要一个独立于算法的统一框架，在投入模型之前量化特权特征的潜在价值。

Method: 基于Catoni的PAC-Bayes界，定义两个理想化的端点Gibbs测度——always-PI（始终可用PI的oracle基准）和never-PI（永不使用PI的基线），通过二者对数配分函数之差ΔZ来量化PI的潜力；引入部署映射T处理推断时PI不可得的情况，并给出部署证书准则；进一步将该理论从标准Gibbs形式推广到指数族的固定特征子类。通过GMM贝叶斯估计和带特权噪声的高斯过程分类（GPC+）进行验证。

Result: 在两个实验设定下（无监督GMM的类别标签作为PI；监督GPC+中边界距离作为PI），对数配分函数差ΔZ与实际测试性能增益高度相关（GMM中Pearson r=0.911；GPC+中随PI噪声增大两者同步衰减至零），证明该训练时度量能可靠追踪真实测试时增益。

Conclusion: 该框架迈向了LUPI的信息论理解，使研究者在投入模型前即可从训练数据量化特权特征的潜在价值；未来工作需开发无界损失的扩展、可扩展的证据估计器以及能保留PI信息的部署映射学习原则。

Abstract: In practice, various learning scenarios provide access to auxiliary features exclusively during training. Incorporating such data to enhance model performance gave rise to a paradigm known as Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI). While this extra information is intended to improve the resulting model, establishing a generalized, cohesive understanding of how privileged information (PI) transfers useful knowledge remains a challenge. Vapnik's original theory and subsequent works offer performance guarantees in certain cases, but these results are inherently per-algorithm and rely on setting-specific proof approaches. Consequently, a more general framework explaining how and when PI transfers useful knowledge is still missing. To bridge this gap, we introduce an algorithm-agnostic, information-theoretic approach based on the PAC-Bayes framework. Rather than asking whether a particular algorithm exploits PI, we ask how much value it could offer: comparing the tightest achievable risk bound with and without PI yields its potential - an upper limit on the extractable gain. We introduce a metric that quantifies this potential directly from empirical training risk, bypassing the need for test-time data access, and validate our findings in both supervised and unsupervised settings. The results demonstrate a robust correspondence between our training-time metric and true test-time performance gains. Ultimately, this work takes a necessary step toward an information-theoretic understanding of LUPI, and quantifying the potential of privileged features before committing to a model.

</details>


### [262] [Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling for Full-Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12899)
*Chen Min,Haowen Jiang,Zheng Ma,Xiongbin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理状态引导扩散采样（PSG），一种将预训练扩散地质先验与全波形反演（FWI）耦合的新框架。通过高斯桥将持续更新的物理速度场与扩散先验连接，在分离波动方程伴随梯度与去噪器雅可比的同时，保留传统FWI的初始化与优化历史。在OpenFWI四个数据集及Marmousi、Overthrust、BP2004 Salt等分布外模型上均取得最优重建精度，并对噪声与缺道具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演（FWI）因高度非线性与严重不适定性，对初始化和先验信息依赖极强。经典的Tikhonov、TV等正则化方法难以表达复杂地质结构；现有的扩散后验采样（DPS）方法直接将去噪估计代入非线性波动方程求梯度，会产生不可靠的物理引导，且无法保留传统FWI的初始化与累积优化状态。亟需一种既能利用学习到的地质先验，又能稳定耦合物理一致性的反演框架。

Method: PSG通过引入高斯桥（Gaussian bridge）将"物理速度v"与"扩散先验场x₀"耦合：1）构建增强后验 π̃(x₀,v|y) ∝ p_data(x₀)·r(v|x₀)·p_obs(y|v)，其中r(v|x₀)=N(x₀,Σ)为桥接因子；2）通过Jensen不等式导出物理速度的目标函数Ψ_t(v)=ℓ_y(v)+(1/2)‖v−D_θ(x_t,t)‖²_{Σ⁻¹}，即波形拟合+去噪正则化；3）反向扩散每步执行：先用去噪器D_θ得到x̂₀，再对物理速度做Adam梯度下降，然后用更新后的v_t_{n-1}与x̂₀的残差作为扩散步骤的线性引导项；4）物理状态v在整个反向轨迹中持续存在，可从传统FWI初始化出发并继承优化器状态。扩散先验分支提供先验得分，物理分支提供波形一致性，两者通过高斯桥解耦却持续耦合。

Result: 在OpenFWI四个家族（FV-B、FF-B、CV-B、CF-B）的清洁数据上，PSG在所有指标上均最优：CF-B上MAE=112.0、RMSE=219.1、SSIM=0.860，远超TV-FWI（241.3/372.3/0.669）和RED-DiffEq（174.1/276.2/0.774）。在σ=0.1-0.5高斯噪声和10/70-60/70缺道条件下，PSG始终保持最高的SSIM，结构恢复能力最强。20次随机运行的集成显示主要地质结构稳定，方差集中在断层与界面附近，且与局部反演误差正相关（Spearman=0.7063）。冻结的CurveFault-B先验直接迁移到Marmousi、Overthrust和BP2004 Salt模型均成功恢复主体地质结构，Salt的RMSE=123.1 m/s、SSIM=0.956。单步耗时约0.143s（RTX 5090），总计算量约为标准FWI的5.5倍、RED-DiffEq的4倍。

Conclusion: PSG通过引入持续物理状态与高斯桥机制，成功将扩散先验与FWI的物理约束解耦又耦合，在保留传统FWI初始化灵活性的同时利用学习到的地质结构。实验证明该方法在分布内与分布外场景下均显著优于现有扩散基线与经典正则化方法，且集成方差可作为局部重建可靠性的有效指示。框架可推广至其他PDE约束反演问题，未来方向包括更真实的地质先验、采集设置以及更高效的少步采样方案。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) estimates subsurface velocity from seismic recordings, but its ill-posedness and nonlinearity make accurate reconstruction strongly dependent on initialization and prior information. Diffusion posterior sampling provides a learned geological prior, yet directly coupling its denoiser to the nonlinear wave solver can yield unreliable physical guidance. We propose Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling (PSG), which couples a persistent physical velocity to the diffusion prior through a Gaussian bridge. The physical state is refined by waveform fitting regularized by the denoised velocity, and in turn guides the reverse diffusion process. This formulation separates the wave-equation and denoiser gradients while preserving conventional FWI initialization and accumulated optimization history. On four OpenFWI families, PSG's terminal denoised estimates outperform classical and diffusion-based baselines under clean and missing-trace acquisitions and maintain strong structural recovery under measurement noise. Repeated stochastic runs preserve the dominant geological structures, with ensemble variability concentrated near geological interfaces and positively associated with local inversion error. A frozen OpenFWI-trained prior further supports inversion of the larger Marmousi, Overthrust, and BP2004 Salt models, recovering complex geological structures without retraining.

</details>


### [263] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Wind Farm Control: A Wind Tunnel Study under Dynamic Wind Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12905)
*Yuhan Su,Hongyang Dong,Simone Tamaro,Filippo Campagnolo,Carlo L. Bottasso,Xiaowei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MTD3-BC的离线强化学习算法，用于动态风向下的风电场偏航控制功率最大化，并在风洞实验中进行了首次实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有方法依赖精确尾流模型或大量仿真器交互，计算成本高；2. 在线RL存在"sim-to-real"差距；3. 已有RL方法多假设固定风向，难以应对动态风向变化；4. 离线RL虽有潜力但缺乏实验验证。

Method: 1. 提出MTD3-BC算法，基于TD3-BC扩展，加入动作一致性正则化项以抑制偏航抖动；2. 设计风向条件驱动的actor网络架构与状态归一化；3. 利用贪心策略和LUT方法的离线数据训练；4. 在Politecnico di Milano风洞的三台缩比G1风机上进行实验验证，进行了三轮部署-再训练迭代。

Result: 1. MTD3-BC相比贪心策略提升约10%功率；2. 性能与数据标定的LUT基准和在线PPO相当；3. 训练成本仅约为PPO的5%；4. 通过三轮部署-再训练工作流展示了实际可行性；5. 这是首个离线RL风电场控制策略的实验验证。

Conclusion: MTD3-BC作为一种完全数据驱动、无模型的方法，在动态风向条件下表现出与模型基准相当的性能，同时大幅降低训练成本。该工作为离线RL在实际风电场部署中提供了可行路径，未来计划在商业风电场进行进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the wind farm power maximization problem in the presence of wind direction changes. Specifically, a model-free Modified Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient with Behavior Cloning (MTD3-BC) algorithm is proposed to tackle this task through yaw control under varying wind direction conditions. MTD3-BC is an offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm that aims to infer good behavior from only a precollected offline dataset. Additionally, to ensure smooth and moderate yaw adjustments, a new action consistency term is introduced into the policy optimization objective. Unlike online RL methods, MTD3-BC does not require extensive interactions with a wind farm simulator during training, significantly reducing computational costs and training time. A wind tunnel experiment is conducted to validate the effectiveness of the algorithm under varying wind directions. The results demonstrate that MTD3-BC successfully mitigates wake effects, delivering farm-level power gains of approximately 10\% over the baseline greedy strategy and performance on par with a data-calibrated model-based wake-steering benchmark, while requiring no wake model and only a small fraction of the training cost of online RL. To our knowledge, this is the first time an offline RL wind farm control policy has been validated and demonstrated experimentally.

</details>


### [264] [A Large-Scale AIS Dataset from Finnish Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12938)
*Debayan Bhattacharya,Ikram Ul Haq,Carlos Pichardo Vicencio,Sebastien Lafond*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个来自芬兰水域（波罗的海及芬兰湖泊）的大型公开AIS数据集，包含2021年4月至2022年12月共20个月的约2.29亿个数据点，并综述了AIS数据在海事研究中的机器学习应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS数据最初用于防碰撞，但已发展为海事研究的多功能工具。现有的AIS数据集对波罗的海及芬兰湖泊这一具有独特地理特征（洲际货运、军事活动、冰冻水域、内陆湖泊）的区域覆盖不足，亟需一个大规模、区域性、可公开访问的数据集来推动海事研究创新与合作。

Method: 1. 通过Fintraffic的公共MQTT WebSocket API收集芬兰水域的AIS原始数据；2. 按天生成两个CSV文件（位置文件和元数据文件），包含MMSI、经纬度、SOG、COG、navStat、ROT、船舶类型等完整字段；3. 对文献进行系统综述（基于Web of Science，检索关键词"AIS"+"navigation"+"maritime"+"machine learning"，筛选出35篇论文）；4. 使用Folium地图库结合网格搜索聚合算法生成季节性热力图进行可视化分析。

Result: 1. 构建了一个包含2,293,129,345个数据点的大规模AIS数据集，覆盖20个月；2. 基于唯一MMSI统计，船舶类型以货船（51.53%）和油轮（22.29%）为主；3. 文献综述涵盖9个海事应用领域：避碰（11篇）、船舶轨迹预测（13篇）、船舶操纵行为模式（3篇）、搁浅风险评估（2篇）、船舶传感器（2篇）、港口类型预测（1篇）、风险分析（1篇）、异常检测（2篇）、事故分析（1篇）；4. 季节性热力图揭示了冬季湖泊冻结导致活动锐减、夏季湖泊小型船只活动激增的规律性模式；5. 芬兰湖泊区域共识别136艘船，以货船（51.96%）为主。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了芬兰水域（含波罗的海与内陆湖泊）AIS数据的空白，为海事研究人员提供了宝贵的区域性资源，有望推动船舶行为分析、交通模式研究、碰撞与搁浅风险评估等方面的创新，未来将促进更安全、高效的海事运营研究。

Abstract: This research paper contributes to the maritime research community by introducing a comprehensive AIS dataset from Finnish waters, specifically the Baltic Sea region. AIS data, initially designed for collision prevention, have evolved into a versatile tool with applications across diverse maritime domains. Our paper not only curates and categorises existing AIS datasets but also introduces a collected AIS dataset from the Baltic Sea area, renowned for its intercontinental cargo routes, military activities, and frozen water expanses. This dataset includes 229 millions data points and provides researchers with a resource for studying maritime activities and vessel behaviour in this dynamic region, notably distinguished by the inclusion of data from Finnish lakes. Our analysis includes a detailed listing of ship types and relevant features, empowering researchers to explore various maritime domains. To enhance comprehension and analysis, we provide visualisations of maritime traffic patterns. By publishing this AIS dataset, we aim to catalyse innovation and collaboration in maritime research, offering a gateway to deeper insights into maritime activities in the Baltic Sea and Finnish lakes.

</details>


### [265] [Information-Induced Training Geometry: Exact Reduction, Canonical Completion, and Structured Expressivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12991)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在仿射不变黎曼度量（AIRM）下系统地刻画了正定训练度量（cometric）被部分"可见"信息通道约束时的精确几何行为，给出了唯一正则补全的闭式公式、动通道下的秩层化配对度量，以及针对结构化优化器族的可行性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器（K-FAC、Adam、Shampoo 等）本质上选择一个正定度量，但其度量信息受当前数据所"看见"的余切子空间限制。需要严格回答：在给定一个可见正定目标和一个参考度量后，是否存在一个唯一最近的全空间正定补全，以及何种观测映射能实现"无损降维"。

Method: 三个层次的理论：(1) 度量空间层面的"度量淹没（metric submetry）"作为通用径向-可见降维的精确等价条件；(2) Hadamard 流形上"分裂-Hadamard 信息映射"给出相干全局面片、近端与梯度流提升；(3) 对 SPD 锥上满秩线性压缩 π_A(P)=AᵀPA，证明其为 split-Hadamard 淹没，推导闭式补全 Ψ_{P₀,A}(R)，并在通道移动时建立秩层化与闭式配对度量（含错配权 Ξ(R)=R+R⁻¹−2I），最后用凸锥相对内部刻画对角与块结构族的可表达性。

Result: 主要结论：(A) 闭式补全公式 Ψ_{P₀,A}(R) 是 AIRM 距离下的唯一最近补全，且全形变代价等于可见代价；(B) 移动通道下描述子空间形成 SPD 锥的规范不变的秩层化，配对度量 Gram 矩阵显式正半定，且精确揭示参考值模式的退化；(C) 对角/块结构族的可见目标可实现性等价于其凸锥像落在相对内部，并给出严格分离与界面证书；(D) 已知子空间与未知子空间下分别给出有限样本恢复界，将可见度量误差与子空间旋转误差分离。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个严格、可计算且与参考相关的几何框架：度量淹没给出通用无损降维，split-Hadamard 给出相干最短提升，SPD 闭式补全与动通道配对度量刻画了训练度量的精确运动；结构化证书与有限样本界进一步把理论落实到优化器实现与统计可恢复性。该框架对未来的度量演化与审计工作提供了基础，但其本身不规定演化规律，不识别 Fisher 与 Hessian，也不保证任意非线性观测映射可降维。

Abstract: Training data constrains optimizer geometry through the covectors visible to a declared information channel. We study how such partial information determines a full positive cometric relative to a reference and which degrees of freedom remain unidentified. Our central result resolves full-column-rank positive-definite compression under affine-invariant Riemannian geometry. The compression map is a split-Hadamard metric submetry and admits an explicit unique completion that is the affine-invariant nearest full geometry realizing a visible target and yields exact full-to-visible variational reduction. When the channel moves, the completions form a gauge-invariant rank stratification of the positive-definite cone. Its closed-form pullback pair metric separates visible-metric motion from subspace rotation through a reference-mismatch weight, yields an explicit positive-semidefinite multi-direction Gram matrix, and exposes the precise singularity of reference-valued modes. The mechanism is explained by a metric theorem equating ball submetry, attained fiber distance, and lossless reduction of every monotone radial visible decision problem. A smooth split-Hadamard theorem supplies coherent information sheets, proximal commutation, and solution-wise gradient-flow lifting. The positive-definite realization also gives closed-form prior-data shrinkage. Diagonal and block optimizer families reduce to relative-interior conic image tests with valid facial certificates, while deterministic and finite-sample bounds quantify recovery of the visible geometry and its subspace. Together these results characterize exact reduction, reference-dependent completion, and structured expressivity for the stated finite-dimensional affine-invariant model.

</details>


### [266] [Dimension-Corrected Hitting Times for Heavy-Tailed Spectral Emergence in Neural Optimizer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12994)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经网络权重矩阵谱重尾的出现建模为右删失首达时间问题，发现在全批量教师-学生动力学中，重尾出现时间遵循一个维度修正的谱隙定律 τ_HT ≈ C·Δ₁⁻ᵞ·dᵠ，而非仅由第一步spike-bulk间隔决定。理论证明Adam递归本身不能保证谱再分配，但在投影奇异基扩散条件下可推出该首达时间定律，并辅以实证支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾经验谱密度（ESD）被广泛用作隐式自正则化的诊断，但重尾出现的步骤复杂度（从spike到heavy tail的转变需要多少步）仍未被理解。现有工作主要关注最终时间的尾指数或基于随机梯度噪声的机制，缺乏对首达时间的定量刻画。本文将"重尾何时出现"视为一个右删失生存分析问题，而非简单的最终态描述。

Method: 1. 问题形式化：将重尾出现定义为满足共识诊断（Hill指数、KS得分、顶部尾质量、有效秩）的首达时间 τ_HT，未达到者视为右删失观测。2. 实证方法：255+75=330次全批量Adam教师-学生实验（d∈{200,500,1000}），有限起始回归拟合 τ_HT ≈ C·Δ₁⁻ᵞ·dᵠ；右删失lognormal加速失效时间（AFT）模型比较维度修正模型与仅gap模型（AIC）。3. 理论方法：(a) Proposition 1证明线性网络精确两步损失动力学不识别因子谱尾；(b) Proposition 2证明Adam递归本身不蕴含谱再分配（秩一锁定反例）；(c) 假设投影谱扩散条件后，Theorem 1通过KL势收缩推导出维度修正首达时间定律。4. 辅助实证：投影核机制检验、Adam/AdamW/GD/signGD优化器对比、Qwen2.5-0.5B和Pythia-70M预训练权重静态扫描、sklearn digits的MLP/ConvNet健全性检查。

Result: 1. 维度修正定律：τ_HT ≈ C·Δ₁⁻ᵞ·dᵠ，R²=0.683，γ=0.626，ρ=0.772（330次完成运行）。2. 删失分析：维度修正删失AFT模型AIC从706.62降至628.70（vs仅gap模型）。3. 健全性：排除η=1.0后R²=0.728；排除η≥0.8后R²=0.702。4. 理论边界：证明Adam递归本身不能保证谱再分配，必须有可测量的投影扩散条件。5. 优化器：Adam与弱衰减AdamW一致，GD和signGD在测试网格下未达到共识起始。6. 泛化警告：岭读出测试MSE在Hill-α区间非单调。7. 外部检验：Qwen2.5-0.5B的168个内部矩阵中94.05%显示比匹配高斯零假设更低的Hill α。

Conclusion: 重尾谱出现应被视为删失首达时间现象。在全批量Adam教师-学生动力学中，起始时间由维度修正谱隙定律控制，而非仅由第一步spike-bulk间隔。有限起始回归、删失生存建模、eta桥稳健性、投影核机制检验均支持该定律。本文明确不主张：(1) 从Adam协方差出发的完整首原理谱扩散定理；(2) 优化器普遍性（GD/signGD不触发）；(3) 尾越重泛化越好的单调结论。核心贡献是一个可复现的首达时间定律加上严格的条件理论框架。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed empirical spectral densities of neural-network weight matrices are widely used as diagnostics of implicit self-regularization, but the step complexity of heavy-tail emergence remains poorly understood. We formulate spectral heavy-tail formation as a right-censored hitting-time problem: a run that does not reach a heavy-tail diagnostic within the observation horizon is treated as censored rather than discarded. In controlled full-batch teacher--student dynamics, we find that the first-step spike--bulk gap alone does not explain onset time. Instead, finite-onset regression supports a dimension-corrected spectral-gap law, (\tau_{\mathrm{HT}}\approx C\Delta_1^{-\gamma}d^\rho), with (R^2=0.683), (\gamma=0.626), and (\rho=0.772) across 330 completed runs. Right-censored lognormal accelerated-failure-time models further favor the dimension-corrected model over a gap-only model, improving AIC from 706.62 to 628.70. Theoretically, we prove that exact early loss dynamics in linear networks do not determine factor spectral tails, that Adam recurrences alone do not imply spectral redistribution, and that projected singular-basis spreading implies contraction of a spectral-tail potential and hence a dimension-corrected hitting-time bound. Empirically, projected-kernel profiles support the sufficient spreading mechanism, Adam and AdamW agree under tested grids, GD and signGD do not reach onset in the same regimes, and real pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B and Pythia-70M transformer weights show non-Gaussian spectral-tail structure relative to matched Gaussian nulls. The result is a reproducible spectral hitting-time law with rigorous conditional theory, not a claim that Adam necessarily generates heavy tails from first principles.

</details>


### [267] [A Full Adam Theorem for Spectral Heavy-Tail Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12996)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在高斯Stein-Hermite师生状态演化模型中,严格证明了全批量Adam优化器从谱首尖峰-体相间隙出现到谱重尾形成的两侧命中时刻律 τ_ε=Θ(Δ₁⁻ᵞd^ρ log(Ψ₀/ε))。证明链条包含七个新闭合环节:精确Stein-Hermite梯度、有限宽度协方差集中、非中心高斯符号核、对角预条件器齐次化、Hermite边传递正则变化、Gram混合与近似KL收缩。同时证明了任意梯度下更强Adam定理的不可能性,以及精确两步线性网络损失动力学无法识别因子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中Adam优化器训练产生的权重矩阵常呈现谱重尾现象,但缺乏从Adam更新规则到谱重尾命中时刻的完整理论链条。现有工作或假设漂移项,或仅证明条件性命中,缺乏"完备"的Adam定理。本文旨在闭合同状态演化模型内从Adam动量、分母到谱命中律的每一步推导。

Method: 方法包含七步严格证明链:(1)用多元Stein恒等式推导精确的Stein-Hermite总体梯度,矩阵形式为G(W)=(1/√(hd))D_a{A(W)W+c(W)β⊤};(2)对有限宽度H=W的协方差作矩阵Bernstein集中,得到‖d⁻¹HH⊤-Σ_H‖_op ≤ C‖Σ_H‖_op(√((h+log(1/δ))/d)+(h+log(1/δ))/d);(3)用双变量高斯CDF Φ₂推导非中心Adam动量符号核K_t^sign(a,b)=1-2Φ(τ_a)-2Φ(τ_b)+4Φ₂(τ_a,τ_b;R_ab),并给出相关依赖的一阶Taylor展开;(4)证明对角预条件器在((k,χ_t))-平衡奇异基下保持投影指数,只需χ_t=o(1);(5)Hermite边传递定理:边廓x_i=Δ₁^{a₀}d^{-ρ₀}L(i)i^{-s}经标量传递函数T(x)=b_q x^q+O(x^{q+1})作用后,导出投影响应的正则变化指数γ=qa₀,ρ=qρ₀,α*=qs;(6)Gram精确更新分解为径向标量部分(在归一化廓形中抵消)与迹零小扰动,得到p_i(t+1)=(1-κ_t)p_i(t)+κ_t b_i(t)+ε_{i,t},κ_t≍Δ₁^γ d^{-ρ};(7)近似KL收缩引理给出E[Ψ_{t+1}|F_t]≤(1-c₁Δ₁^γ d^{-ρ})Ψ_t,迭代后用尾概率求和得上界,无瞬移下界给出下界。附录K用G_t=0反例排除任意梯度定理;附录I用规范对称性证明两步线性损失无法识别因子谱。

Result: 最终定理8.1给出命中时刻的双侧律τ_ε=Θ(Δ₁⁻ᵞd^ρ log(₀/ε)),其中(γ,ρ,α*)=(qa₀,qρ₀,qs)由边廓指数与首个非零Hermite传递阶q共同确定,非拟合。指数(γ,ρ)的数值恢复为(0.7199,0.8301),R²=0.999998;有限宽度协方差的Frobenius斜率-0.496、算子斜率-0.507(理论-0.5);符号核扫掠最大RMSE 0.00456、最小相关0.99265;正则变化指数中位误差8.06×10⁻;Gram混合扰动斜率1.031。外部分析显示Qwen2.5-0.5B与Pythia-70M的静态谱在入口洗牌零假设下保留上尾几何(仅作合理性检查,不构成动态定理证据)。

Conclusion: 论文贡献包括:(a)在封闭Stein-Hermite状态演化内完成从Adam动量、分母到谱命中律的完整推导链;(b)证明Adam动量符号核为非中心高斯核,中心化反正弦仅为推论;(c)在基平衡条件下证明Adam分母保持主投影谱指数;(d)从边廓与非零Hermite传递阶推出正则变化投影响应;(e)证明精确两步线性损失公式不能识别因子谱;(f)证明任意梯度Adam定理不成立。论文明确声明不主张任意自适应Adam梯度必产生重尾,亦不主张静态LLM谱可估计γ或ρ。数值审核验证了定理各环节的有限尺度行为与指数恢复精度。

Abstract: We prove a full Adam theorem for spectral heavy-tail onset in a closed Gaussian Stein-Hermite teacher-student state-evolution model. The theorem begins with the actual full-batch Adam recurrences, derives the population gradient by Stein-Hermite calculus, proves finite-width covariance concentration, converts multi-step Adam momentum into an exact non-centered Gaussian sign kernel, controls the diagonal Adam denominator by a basis-homogenization theorem, derives a regularly varying projected update response from a Hermite edge-transfer theorem, pushes the response through the exact Gram update, and proves approximate-target KL contraction with matching upper and lower hitting bounds. The final law is (\tau_\varepsilon=\Theta(\Delta_1^{-\gamma}d^\rho\log(\Psi_0/\varepsilon))), where (\Delta_1) is the first spike-bulk spectral gap. The result is full in the following precise sense: every step from Adam's momentum and denominator to the spectral hitting law is formalized inside the closed state-evolution model. We also prove that a stronger arbitrary-gradient Adam theorem is impossible, and that exact two-step linear-network loss dynamics do not identify factor spectra or heavy-tail hitting times.

</details>


### [268] [Dual-guided Hierarchical Edge Localization for Large-scale Optimal Transport Across Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13010)
*Wenzhou Xia,Qiaoqiao Ding,Jingwei Liang,Xiaoqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HELLO，一种用于大规模离散最优传输（OT）的层次化求解器，通过对偶引导的边定位方法在百万点规模上实现了跨维度的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无正则化的离散最优传输需要求解一个具有二次数量级传输变量的线性规划，在百万样本规模下直接求解在内存和计算上均不可行。现有方法（熵正则化、低秩OT、稀疏主动支撑方法）各有局限：熵正则化引入偏差且耦合密集；低秩方法受限于秩约束；现有稀疏方法在高维下初始化或精化效率低下。

Method: HELLO 将大规模离散 OT 转化为边定位问题，采用对偶势作为共同信号引导三个模块：(1) BuildHier 通过递归子采样构建共享一边的层次结构；(2) Init 通过 c-变换继承粗层对偶势并进行节点级 top-κ 双重分配以构建初始主动支撑；(3) 双引导精化交替进行受限 LP 求解（SolveLP）和主动支撑更新（UpdateSupp），后者通过双重违例检测（行/列 top-γ）插入边、预算剪枝（O(m+n) 大小）来维护支撑。精确算术下，证明使用符号字典序边际时有限迭代内终止于全局最优。

Result: 在百万点规模上，HELLO 在单维到数千维的特征维度上均比强基线（Sinkhorn、IPOT、MDOT-TNT、HiRef、HALO）实现数量级的加速且获得更低的传输目标。在单卡 H100 上扩展至每边际 128 万样本、8192 维，峰值 GPU 内存 41.6 GiB，完全相对 KKT 残差低于 10⁻⁶。框架还扩展至半离散 OT（提出经验对偶势平均方法，理论证明误差阶为 (n-1)/(mR)）、Gromov-Wasserstein、非平衡 OT（通过平移不变完全修正 Frank-Wolfe）以及基于 OT 的流匹配，在 MNIST-Fashion-MNIST 等基准上显著优于 Sinkhorn-GW 和 LOT-GW。

Conclusion: HELLO 通过统一的双重引导信号同时驱动跨层初始化和层内精化，结合 O(m+n) 内存预算和 GPU 并行流式评估，首次实现了百万点级跨维度的精确离散 OT 求解，并为多种 OT 变体提供了可扩展的精确平衡 OT 预言机。未来工作包括加速双重违例检测（无需穷举成对评估）以及将该框架直接扩展到多边际和非平衡 OT。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) compares distributions and aligns datasets in machine learning, yet unregularized discrete OT requires a linear program with quadratically many transport variables. We propose HELLO, a hierarchical solver that casts large-scale discrete OT as edge localization and uses dual potentials to guide both coarse-to-fine initialization and within-level refinement. Initialization propagates coarse dual potentials across a recursive subsampling hierarchy to assign candidate edges. Refinement then iteratively inserts the largest dual violators in each row and column until the relative KKT residual meets a prescribed tolerance, while budgeted pruning ensures linear memory complexity. For exact-arithmetic refinement, we prove finite termination at a global optimum under a symbolic lexicographic rule. At the million-point scale, HELLO attains lower transport objectives with order-of-magnitude runtime improvements over strong baselines across feature dimensions from single digits to thousands. It further scales to 1.28 million samples per marginal in 8192 dimensions on a single H100, using 41.6 GiB peak GPU memory while satisfying a full relative KKT residual below $10^{-6}$. Beyond standard discrete OT, the framework supports general pairwise costs and serves as a scalable balanced-OT oracle for semi-discrete OT, Gromov--Wasserstein, unbalanced OT, and OT-based Flow Matching.

</details>


### [269] [Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13039)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Pedro Ribeiro,Pedro Saleiro,Pedro Bizarro,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"演化域迁移学习"（TrED）这一新问题，将传统迁移学习的多个子领域（领域泛化、无监督域适应、有监督域适应、多域学习）统一为一个动态框架，其中目标域的数据和标签随时间逐步积累，并提出沿整个轨迹评估学习者的累积风险准则。论文形式化定义了数据可用性过程、学习协议和评估标准，指出经典设置只是TrED轨迹上的不同"阶段"，并讨论了阶段切换基线、TL文献中的构建模块（数据变换、模型适配、学习目标）以及基础模型作为潜在解决方案的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实部署场景中（如服务扩展到新市场、监管变化、新疾病爆发），新目标域的数据并非一次性可用，而是随时间逐步积累的，标签存在延迟。传统迁移学习将问题分割为基于固定数据可用性快照的孤立子问题，无法描述部署系统必须经历完整演化路径的现实需求。

Method: 1. 形式化定义TrED：三要素——由环境决定的"数据可用性过程"（含标签延迟）、学习者可自由选择的"学习协议"（更新时间和损失函数）、以及评估整个模型轨迹的"累积目标风险"准则R(P)。2. 将经典TL设置（DG、UDA、SDA、MDL）恢复为TrED轨迹中的不同阶段/区间。3. 分析现有解决方案：（a）阶段切换基线（按数据状态切换不同方法）；（b）按技术族分类的TL构建模块——数据变换（实例加权、特征映射、伪标签、特征扩展）、模型适配（模块化深度学习、集成）、学习目标（分布对齐、元学习、自监督）；（c）基础模型在每个阶段的适用能力映射及其四个未解决的差距。

Result: 1. TrED被证明是一个定义明确的问题：经典TL设置是TrED在不同时间点的快照/阶段，而非独立问题的拼接。2. 阶段切换基线存在两个根本局限：转换时机难以确定（标签延迟使目标风险不可观测），且分段常数策略的解空间可能不包含最优轨迹。3. 现有TL方法大多针对单一阶段设计；少数跨越相邻阶段（如UDA到SDA）的方法也仅处理单一边界。4. 基础模型在四个阶段各有适配机制（零样本、伪标签蒸馏、少样本ICL、检索增强），但仍存在四个差距：内部转换时机未自动化、未挖掘预训练模型未包含的无标签信号、逐阶段贪心优化不等价于累积优化、以及目标域可能泄露到预训练语料中的评估问题。

Conclusion: TrED是一个良构且未解决的问题。将迁移学习重新定义为演化过程而非孤立快照的集合，为更自适应和有韧性的模型铺平了道路，能够随时间持续改进，适合数据动态变化的真实部署场景。该问题的解决需要超越阶段切换和单一TL技术族的统一方法，是一个重要的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Transfer learning explores how to leverage knowledge from various tasks or domains (sources) to enhance predictive performance in related tasks or domains (targets). Typically, transfer learning research is segmented into several isolated sub-areas (such as domain generalisation, domain adaptation, or multi-domain learning), each making distinct assumptions about target data availability, namely how much data and how many labels are available at training time. However, in many real-world applications, data availability is not fixed but evolves over time, as instances and labels are progressively collected from a new domain. Each of the classical settings then describes only a snapshot of a trajectory that a deployed system must traverse in full. We formalise this trajectory as a transfer learning problem in its own right, Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains (TrED), specified by a data availability process fixed by the environment, a learning protocol that the method is free to choose, and an evaluation criterion that scores the whole trajectory of models rather than a single one. Within this formalism, the classical settings are recovered as regimes that a learner may pass through, rather than as separate problems that TrED concatenates. We then examine the transfer learning literature to identify mechanisms that are promising building blocks for a solution, and find that most methods are tailored to a single regime and that even the strongest existing candidates do not yet optimise the whole trajectory. We argue that TrED is a well-posed and unsolved problem, and an important direction for future research.

</details>


### [270] [Quantile-based Loss Filtering for Outlier-Robust Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13040)
*Jamie Haddock,Anna Ma,Elizaveta Rebrova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Quantile-k-Loss SGD (QkL-SGD)框架，通过在每次迭代中从k个采样损失的经验下分位数中均匀选择更新索引，实现对有限和优化中损坏组件函数的鲁棒随机梯度下降。该方法将min-k-loss SGD和标准SGD统一为一个两参数族，证明了大样本下的线性收敛性以及小样本下的概率收敛性，并在多项式回归、逻辑回归和合页损失实验中验证了中间分位数优于标准SGD和min-k-loss SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SGD在存在损坏数据（如错误标注、对抗扰动）时容易因单个损坏梯度而严重偏离优化轨迹。现有鲁棒方法各有局限：min-k-loss SGD过于保守容易停滞；传统鲁棒方法多针对特定问题（如线性系统）设计。论文旨在开发一种通用的、基于当前损失分位数过滤的采样规则，统一并改进现有方法。

Method: 方法核心是QkL-SGD算法：每次迭代从m个组件中均匀采样k个，计算它们的损失，然后从经验下q分位数（即损失最低的qk个）中均匀选择一个作为更新索引。理论分析分为两部分：(1)大样本确定性分析（假设k与损坏数s和子集强凸性阈值r_sc成正比），证明线性收敛到未损坏目标的最优解；(2)小样本概率分析，使用软分位数分离和良好步长曲率假设，得到依赖异常值选择概率的收敛率。特殊化为硬分离假设时，利用Hoeffding不等式给出异常选择概率的显式上界。

Result: 在多项式回归（无噪声和有噪声）、正则化逻辑回归、正则化合页损失四个实验场景中，与标准SGD和min-k-loss SGD比较。结果表明：(1)当选择q≈1-β（β为损坏比例）时，QkL-SGD在相对损失和参数误差上均优于SGD和min-k-loss SGD；(2)min-k-loss SGD因反复选择接近解的组件而早期停滞；(3)即使在理论假设不成立的情况下（如噪声、非光滑损失），中间分位数仍保持鲁棒性；(4)样本量k越大QkL-SGD方差越小，MkL-SGD则相反。

Conclusion: QkL-SGD为有限和优化的损坏数据场景提供了一个统一且有效的框架，通过分位数参数q显式控制鲁棒性与优化进度的权衡。理论和实验均表明中间分位数优于最小损失选择。未来方向包括：利用已计算的qk个损失信息进行批量更新或梯度平均、结合动量和方差缩减技术开发自适应变体。

Abstract: We study loss-based filtering for finite-sum optimization with a subset of corrupted component functions whose gradients may be highly unreliable. Motivated by minimum-loss-based SGD (min-$k$-loss) and quantile-based methods for corrupted linear systems, we propose and analyze a general loss-filtering framework -- Quantile-\(k\)-Loss SGD (Q\(k\)L-SGD) -- that samples \(k\) component losses at each iteration and updates using an index chosen uniformly from the lower empirical \(q\)-quantile. We prove linear convergence of this family of methods under standard convexity assumptions, requiring the sample size to scale with the number of corruptions and a subset strong-convexity threshold.
